

=== FILE: src/gatekeeper/laws.ts ===

/**
 * UBE GATEKEEPER — GLOBAL LAW ENGINE
 * Version: 3.0.0 — CIVILIZATION INFRASTRUCTURE LEVEL
 *
 * 197+ countries as machine-readable, enforceable legal rules.
 * GDPR, CCPA, LGPD, PDPA, PIPL, DPDP, POPIA, and 190+ more.
 * Sector-specific laws: HIPAA, FERPA, GLBA, SOX, PCI-DSS,
 * ITAR, EAR, Basel III, MiFID II, eIDAS, NIS2, DORA.
 * Cross-border transfer rules, Data residency enforcement,
 * Retention schedules, Breach notification timelines,
 * Automated legal conflict resolution, Treaty recognition.
 */

import { createLogger } from '../core/logger';

const logger = createLogger('GatekeeperLaws');

// ─── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type DataCategory =
  | 'PUBLIC' | 'INTERNAL' | 'CONFIDENTIAL' | 'REGULATED'
  | 'HEALTH' | 'FINANCIAL' | 'BIOMETRIC' | 'CHILDREN'
  | 'GOVERNMENT' | 'MILITARY' | 'CLASSIFIED' | 'LEGAL'
  | 'CRIMINAL' | 'POLITICAL' | 'RELIGIOUS' | 'GENETIC'
  | 'COMMUNICATIONS' | 'LOCATION' | 'BEHAVIORAL' | 'UNKNOWN'
  | 'RACIAL' | 'SEXUAL_ORIENTATION' | 'TRADE_UNION'
  | 'PHILOSOPHICAL' | 'IMMIGRATION' | 'SOCIAL_SECURITY'
  | 'CREDIT' | 'INSURANCE' | 'EMPLOYMENT' | 'EDUCATION'
  | 'CONSUMER' | 'VEHICLE' | 'VOTER' | 'TAX';

export type TransferRule =
  | 'ALLOW'
  | 'BLOCK'
  | 'REQUIRE_CONSENT'
  | 'REQUIRE_ENCRYPTION'
  | 'REQUIRE_AUDIT'
  | 'REQUIRE_DPA'              // Data Processing Agreement
  | 'REQUIRE_SCC'              // Standard Contractual Clauses
  | 'REQUIRE_BCR'              // Binding Corporate Rules
  | 'REQUIRE_ADEQUACY'         // Adequacy decision required
  | 'REQUIRE_SAFEGUARDS'       // Appropriate safeguards
  | 'GOVERNMENT_APPROVAL'      // Requires government approval
  | 'PROHIBITED_EXCEPT_TREATY'; // Only allowed under bilateral treaty

export type RetentionRule =
  | 'NO_STORAGE'
  | 'SESSION_ONLY'
  | 'MAX_24H'
  | 'MAX_7D'
  | 'MAX_30D'
  | 'MAX_90D'
  | 'MAX_180D'
  | 'MAX_1Y'
  | 'MAX_3Y'
  | 'MAX_5Y'
  | 'MAX_7Y'
  | 'MAX_10Y'
  | 'UNLIMITED'
  | 'UNTIL_PURPOSE_FULFILLED'
  | 'UNTIL_WITHDRAWAL';

export type BreachNotificationTimeline =
  | 'IMMEDIATE'           // Real-time
  | '1H'                  // Within 1 hour
  | '4H'                  // Within 4 hours
  | '12H'                 // Within 12 hours
  | '24H'                 // Within 24 hours
  | '48H'                 // Within 48 hours
  | '72H'                 // Within 72 hours (GDPR default)
  | '7D'                  // Within 7 days
  | '30D'                 // Within 30 days
  | '60D'                 // Within 60 days
  | 'WITHOUT_UNDUE_DELAY' // GDPR standard
  | 'EXPEDIENT';          // As soon as reasonably possible

export type SectorLaw =
  | 'HIPAA'        // US Health
  | 'FERPA'        // US Education
  | 'GLBA'         // US Financial
  | 'SOX'          // US Corporate
  | 'PCI_DSS'      // Payment Cards
  | 'COPPA'        // US Children
  | 'CCPA'         // California
  | 'ITAR'         // US Arms Export
  | 'EAR'          // US Export Admin
  | 'BASEL_III'    // Banking
  | 'MIFID_II'     // EU Financial Markets
  | 'EIDAS'        // EU Digital Identity
  | 'NIS2'         // EU Cybersecurity
  | 'DORA'         // EU Digital Operational Resilience
  | 'AI_ACT'       // EU AI Act
  | 'HL7_FHIR'     // Health Data Interoperability
  | 'SWIFT_CSP'    // Banking SWIFT
  | 'ISO27001'     // Information Security
  | 'SOC2';        // Service Organization Controls

export interface CountryLaw {
  countryCode: string;
  countryName: string;
  region: string;
  framework: string[];
  sectorLaws: SectorLaw[];
  blockedCategories: DataCategory[];
  restrictedCategories: DataCategory[];
  transferRules: Partial<Record<DataCategory, TransferRule>>;
  retentionRules: Partial<Record<DataCategory, RetentionRule>>;
  requiresExplicitConsent: DataCategory[];
  requiresEncryption: DataCategory[];
  requiresAuditLog: DataCategory[];
  requiresLocalStorage: DataCategory[];    // Must be stored in-country
  requiresDPO: boolean;                     // Data Protection Officer required
  requiresDPIA: boolean;                    // Data Protection Impact Assessment
  crossBorderAllowed: boolean;
  adequacyDecisions: string[];             // Countries with adequacy
  breachNotification: BreachNotificationTimeline;
  breachNotifyAuthority: string;
  breachNotifySubject: boolean;
  rightToErasure: boolean;
  rightToPortability: boolean;
  rightToExplanation: boolean;             // Automated decision explanation
  minConsentAge: number;
  dataLocalization: boolean;               // Data must stay in country
  sovereigntyClause: boolean;              // Government can demand data access
  extraterritorialScope: boolean;          // Law applies outside borders
  penaltyMaxEur?: number;                  // Max fine in EUR equivalent
  penaltyPercentRevenue?: number;          // % of global revenue
  notes: string;
  lastUpdated: string;
}

export interface SectorLawRule {
  law: SectorLaw;
  name: string;
  jurisdiction: string;
  applicableCategories: DataCategory[];
  requirements: string[];
  penalties: string;
  enforcer: string;
}

export interface TransferMechanism {
  from: string;
  to: string;
  mechanism: TransferRule;
  conditions: string[];
  validUntil?: string;
  treaty?: string;
}

export interface LegalConflict {
  originCountry: string;
  destCountry: string;
  dataCategory: DataCategory;
  conflictType: 'DIRECT_CONFLICT' | 'STRICTER_DEST' | 'STRICTER_ORIGIN' | 'INCOMPATIBLE';
  resolution: TransferRule;
  reasoning: string;
  requiresHumanReview: boolean;
}

export interface DataResidencyRequirement {
  countryCode: string;
  categories: DataCategory[];
  mustRemainIn: string[];
  exceptions: string[];
  legalBasis: string;
}

  framework: ['GDPR-equivalent (conservative default)'],

  },

  requiresLocalStorage: [],
  requiresDPO: false, requiresDPIA: false,
  crossBorderAllowed: false, adequacyDecisions: [],
  breachNotification: '72H', breachNotifyAuthority: 'Unknown',

// ─── Law Engine Functions ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function getLaw(countryCode: string): CountryLaw {
  const law = LAWS[countryCode.toUpperCase()];
  if (!law) {
    logger.warn(`[Laws] Unknown jurisdiction: ${countryCode} — applying conservative defaults`);
    return { ...DEFAULT_LAW, countryCode, countryName: `Unknown (${countryCode})` };
  }
  return law;
}

export function isDataAllowed(countryCode: string, category: DataCategory): TransferRule {
  const law = getLaw(countryCode);
  if (law.blockedCategories.includes(category)) return 'BLOCK';
  return law.transferRules[category] ?? (law.restrictedCategories.includes(category) ? 'REQUIRE_CONSENT' : 'ALLOW');
}

export function requiresConsent(countryCode: string, category: DataCategory): boolean {
  const law = getLaw(countryCode);
  return law.requiresExplicitConsent.includes(category);
}

export function requiresEncryption(countryCode: string, category: DataCategory): boolean {
  const law = getLaw(countryCode);
  return law.requiresEncryption.includes(category);
}

export function requiresAudit(countryCode: string, category: DataCategory): boolean {
  const law = getLaw(countryCode);
  return law.requiresAuditLog.includes(category);
}

export function requiresLocalStorage(countryCode: string, category: DataCategory): boolean {
  const law = getLaw(countryCode);
  return law.requiresLocalStorage.includes(category);
}

export function getRetentionRule(countryCode: string, category: DataCategory): RetentionRule {
  const law = getLaw(countryCode);
  return law.retentionRules[category] ?? 'UNLIMITED';
}

export function getBreachTimeline(countryCode: string): BreachNotificationTimeline {
  return getLaw(countryCode).breachNotification;
}

export function canTransferCrossBorder(
  originCode: string,
  destCode: string,
  category: DataCategory,
): { allowed: boolean; mechanism: TransferRule; reasoning: string } {
  const origin = getLaw(originCode);
  const dest = getLaw(destCode);

  // Check blocked categories
  if (origin.blockedCategories.includes(category)) {
    return { allowed: false, mechanism: 'BLOCK', reasoning: `${category} is blocked in origin country ${originCode}` };
  }
  if (dest.blockedCategories.includes(category)) {
    return { allowed: false, mechanism: 'BLOCK', reasoning: `${category} is blocked in destination country ${destCode}` };
  }

  // Check adequacy decisions
  if (origin.adequacyDecisions.includes(destCode)) {
    return { allowed: true, mechanism: 'ALLOW', reasoning: `${destCode} has adequacy decision from ${originCode}` };
  }

  // Check cross-border allowed
  if (!origin.crossBorderAllowed) {
    const originRule = origin.transferRules[category] ?? 'REQUIRE_CONSENT';
    return {
      allowed: originRule !== 'BLOCK',
      mechanism: originRule,
      reasoning: `${originCode} requires: ${originRule} for ${category} cross-border transfers`,
    };
  }

  return { allowed: true, mechanism: 'ALLOW', reasoning: 'Cross-border transfer allowed' };
}

export function detectConflict(
  originCode: string,
  destCode: string,
  category: DataCategory,
): LegalConflict | null {
  const origin = getLaw(originCode);
  const dest = getLaw(destCode);
  const originRule = origin.transferRules[category] ?? 'ALLOW';
  const destRule = dest.transferRules[category] ?? 'ALLOW';

  const ruleStrictness: Record<TransferRule, number> = {
    BLOCK: 10, PROHIBITED_EXCEPT_TREATY: 9, GOVERNMENT_APPROVAL: 8,
    REQUIRE_ADEQUACY: 7, REQUIRE_BCR: 6, REQUIRE_SCC: 5,
    REQUIRE_DPA: 4, REQUIRE_SAFEGUARDS: 4,
    REQUIRE_CONSENT: 3, REQUIRE_ENCRYPTION: 2, REQUIRE_AUDIT: 1, ALLOW: 0,
  };

  const originStrictness = ruleStrictness[originRule] ?? 0;
  const destStrictness = ruleStrictness[destRule] ?? 0;

  if (originRule === destRule) return null; // No conflict

  if ((originRule === 'BLOCK' && destRule !== 'BLOCK') ||
      (destRule === 'BLOCK' && originRule !== 'BLOCK')) {
    return {
      originCountry: originCode, destCountry: destCode,
      dataCategory: category,
      conflictType: 'DIRECT_CONFLICT',
      resolution: 'BLOCK',
      reasoning: `Direct conflict: one country blocks ${category} while other allows`,
      requiresHumanReview: false,
    };
  }

  if (originStrictness !== destStrictness) {
    const stricter = originStrictness > destStrictness ? originCode : destCode;
    return {
      originCountry: originCode, destCountry: destCode,
      dataCategory: category,
      conflictType: originStrictness > destStrictness ? 'STRICTER_ORIGIN' : 'STRICTER_DEST',
      resolution: originStrictness > destStrictness ? originRule : destRule,
      reasoning: `${stricter} has stricter requirements — applying most restrictive rule`,
      requiresHumanReview: Math.abs(originStrictness - destStrictness) >= 5,
    };
  }

  return null;
}

export function getSectorRequirements(
  law: SectorLaw,
  category: DataCategory,
): string[] {
  const rule = SECTOR_LAWS[law];
  if (!rule.applicableCategories.includes(category)) return [];
  return rule.requirements;
}

export function getAllLaws(): CountryLaw[] {
  return Object.values(LAWS);
}

export function getLawsByRegion(region: string): CountryLaw[] {
  return getAllLaws().filter(l => l.region === region);
}

export function getStrictestLaw(categories: DataCategory[]): CountryLaw {
  return getAllLaws().reduce((strictest, current) => {
    const currentBlocked = categories.filter(c => current.blockedCategories.includes(c)).length;
    const strictestBlocked = categories.filter(c => strictest.blockedCategories.includes(c)).length;
    return currentBlocked >= strictestBlocked ? current : strictest;
  });
}

export function getRetentionDays(rule: RetentionRule): number | null {
  const map: Record<RetentionRule, number | null> = {
    NO_STORAGE: 0, SESSION_ONLY: 0, MAX_24H: 1, MAX_7D: 7, MAX_30D: 30,
    MAX_90D: 90, MAX_180D: 180, MAX_1Y: 365, MAX_3Y: 1095, MAX_5Y: 1825,
    MAX_7Y: 2555, MAX_10Y: 3650, UNLIMITED: null,
    UNTIL_PURPOSE_FULFILLED: null, UNTIL_WITHDRAWAL: null,
  };
  return map[rule];
}

logger.info(`[Laws] Global Law Engine loaded — ${Object.keys(LAWS).length} jurisdictions — ${Object.keys(SECTOR_LAWS).length} sector laws`);

/**
 * UBE GATEKEEPER — FORBIDDEN REGISTRY
 * Version: 3.0.0 — CIVILIZATION INFRASTRUCTURE LEVEL
 *
 * Cryptographic fingerprint database for ALL restricted content.
 * Hash-based, pattern-based, binary signature, entropy analysis,
 * ML anomaly scoring, YARA-style rules, threat intelligence feeds,
 * Sanctions screening, Jurisdiction-aware blocking,
 * Real-time threat feed updates, Zero-knowledge content verification.
 * Engine NEVER reads content — only compares mathematical signatures.
 */

import crypto from 'crypto';
import { getStore } from '../core/database';
import { createLogger } from '../core/logger';

const logger = createLogger('GatekeeperRegistry');

// ─── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type ForbiddenCategory =
  | 'CSAM'                    // Child sexual abuse material — absolute block
  | 'CLASSIFIED_GOV'          // Government classified documents
  | 'COURT_SEALED'            // Court-sealed files
  | 'MILITARY_SECRET'         // Military/defense secrets
  | 'STOLEN_DATA'             // Known stolen datasets
  | 'MALWARE'                 // Malicious code/executables
  | 'RANSOMWARE'              // Ransomware payloads
  | 'SPYWARE'                 // Surveillance/spyware
  | 'EXPLOIT_KIT'             // Exploit frameworks
  | 'TERRORIST'               // Terrorism content
  | 'WEAPONS_CBRN'            // Chemical/biological/radiological/nuclear
  | 'WEAPONS_CONVENTIONAL'    // Conventional weapons manufacturing
  | 'DRUGS'                   // Drug synthesis data
  | 'HUMAN_TRAFFICKING'       // Human trafficking content
  | 'LEAKED_PII'              // Known leaked personal data dumps
  | 'RESTRICTED_LEGAL'        // Legally restricted by court order
  | 'SANCTIONS'               // Sanctioned entity data
  | 'FINANCIAL_FRAUD'         // Fraud schemes/scam content
  | 'DEEPFAKE'                // Non-consensual synthetic media
  | 'BIOWEAPON'               // Biological weapon synthesis
  | 'NUCLEAR'                 // Nuclear weapon data
  | 'INFRASTRUCTURE_ATTACK'   // Critical infrastructure attack plans
  | 'DISINFORMATION'          // State-sponsored disinformation
  | 'DARKWEB'                 // Dark web marketplace content
  | 'CUSTOM';                 // Custom blocklist entry

export type SeverityLevel = 'EXTINCTION' | 'CRITICAL' | 'HIGH' | 'MEDIUM' | 'LOW';
export type MatchType = 'HASH' | 'FUZZY_HASH' | 'PATTERN' | 'BINARY_HEADER' | 'ENTROPY' | 'YARA' | 'ML_SCORE' | 'SANCTIONS' | 'NONE';
export type ThreatIntelSource = 'INTERNAL' | 'INTERPOL' | 'FBI' | 'EUROPOL' | 'NCMEC' | 'IWF' | 'UNODC' | 'OFAC' | 'UN_SANCTIONS' | 'EU_SANCTIONS' | 'CUSTOM';
export type ScanDepth = 'SURFACE' | 'STANDARD' | 'DEEP' | 'FORENSIC';

export interface ForbiddenEntry {
  id: string;
  hash: string;               // SHA-256 of content
  fuzzyHash?: string;         // SSDEEP fuzzy hash for similar content
  category: ForbiddenCategory;
  description: string;
  addedAt: string;
  addedBy: string;
  severity: SeverityLevel;
  sourceRef?: string;
  jurisdiction?: string[];    // Which countries this applies to
  autoAlert: boolean;
  autoFreeze: boolean;        // Freeze client immediately
  autoEscalate: boolean;
  threatIntelSource?: ThreatIntelSource;
  expiresAt?: string;         // Some blocks expire
  verified: boolean;          // Verified by human team
  hitCount: number;           // How many times matched
  lastMatchAt?: string;
}

export interface PatternEntry {
  id: string;
  pattern: string;            // String pattern or regex
  isRegex: boolean;
  caseSensitive: boolean;
  category: ForbiddenCategory;
  description: string;
  severity: SeverityLevel;
  addedAt: string;
  jurisdiction?: string[];
  minLength?: number;         // Minimum content length to apply
  requiresContext?: string[]; // Other patterns that must also match
  confidence: number;         // 0-1 base confidence
  hitCount: number;
}

export interface YaraRule {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  category: ForbiddenCategory;
  severity: SeverityLevel;
  strings: YaraString[];
  condition: string;          // 'all of them' | 'any of them' | '2 of them'
  addedAt: string;
  hitCount: number;
}

export interface YaraString {
  identifier: string;         // $identifier
  value: string;
  type: 'text' | 'hex' | 'regex';
  modifiers?: string[];       // nocase, wide, ascii, fullword
}

export interface BinarySignature {
  id: string;
  bytes: number[];
  offset: number;             // Byte offset to check
  mask?: number[];            // Bitmask for flexible matching
  description: string;
  category: ForbiddenCategory;
  severity: SeverityLevel;
  fileExtensions?: string[];
}

export interface EntropyRule {
  id: string;
  description: string;
  category: ForbiddenCategory;
  severity: SeverityLevel;
  minEntropy: number;         // Shannon entropy threshold (0-8)
  maxEntropy: number;
  minSizeBytes: number;
  context?: string;           // What type of content triggers this
}

export interface SanctionEntry {
  id: string;
  entityName: string;
  entityType: 'PERSON' | 'ORGANIZATION' | 'VESSEL' | 'AIRCRAFT' | 'CRYPTO_WALLET';
  aliases: string[];
  identifiers: Record<string, string>; // passport, tax_id, etc.
  cryptoAddresses?: string[];
  country: string;
  sanctionType: string;
  sanctionAuthority: ThreatIntelSource;
  addedAt: string;
  severity: SeverityLevel;
}

export interface RegistryMatch {
  matched: boolean;
  entry?: ForbiddenEntry | PatternEntry | YaraRule | BinarySignature | SanctionEntry;
  matchType: MatchType;
  confidence: number;         // 0-1
  severity?: SeverityLevel;
  category?: ForbiddenCategory;
  details?: string;
  requiresImmediate?: boolean; // Requires immediate action
  recommendedAction?: 'BLOCK' | 'ESCALATE' | 'ALERT' | 'FREEZE_CLIENT' | 'NOTIFY_AUTHORITIES';
}

export interface FullScanResult {
  matched: boolean;
  matches: RegistryMatch[];
  highestSeverity?: SeverityLevel;
  requiresImmediate: boolean;
  scanDepth: ScanDepth;
  scanMs: number;
  contentHash: string;
  entropyScore: number;
  mlAnomalyScore: number;
  recommendedAction?: RegistryMatch['recommendedAction'];
}

export interface RegistryStats {
  builtInPatterns: number;
  customPatterns: number;
  yaraRules: number;
  forbiddenHashes: number;
  binarySignatures: number;
  entropyRules: number;
  sanctionEntries: number;
  totalScans: number;
  totalMatches: number;
  matchRate: number;
  lastUpdated: string;
  threatFeedStatus: Record<ThreatIntelSource, { active: boolean; lastSync?: string; entryCount: number }>;
}

export interface ThreatFeedUpdate {
  source: ThreatIntelSource;
  updatedAt: string;
  addedHashes: number;
  removedHashes: number;
  addedPatterns: number;
  addedSanctions: number;
}

// ─── Shannon Entropy Calculator ───────────────────────────────────────────────

class EntropyCalculator {
  compute(buf: Buffer, sampleSize = 65536): number {
    const sample = buf.slice(0, Math.min(buf.length, sampleSize));
    const freq = new Array(256).fill(0);
    for (const byte of sample) freq[byte]++;
    let entropy = 0;
    const len = sample.length;
    for (const f of freq) {
      if (f === 0) continue;
      const p = f / len;
      entropy -= p * Math.log2(p);
    }
    return Math.round(entropy * 1000) / 1000;
  }

  isHighEntropy(buf: Buffer, threshold = 7.0): boolean {
    return this.compute(buf) >= threshold;
  }
}

// ─── Fuzzy Hash Engine ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class FuzzyHashEngine {
  compute(buf: Buffer): string {
    // Simplified SSDEEP-like rolling hash
    // Production: use native ssdeep binding
    const blockSize = Math.max(3, Math.floor(buf.length / 64));
    const parts: string[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i += blockSize) {
      const chunk = buf.slice(i, i + blockSize);
      const h = crypto.createHash('md5').update(chunk).digest('hex').slice(0, 6);
      parts.push(h);
    }
    return `${blockSize}:${parts.join('')}`;
  }

  similarity(hash1: string, hash2: string): number {
    if (hash1 === hash2) return 1.0;
    const p1 = hash1.split(':');
    const p2 = hash2.split(':');
    if (p1.length < 2 || p2.length < 2) return 0;
    const s1 = p1[1]; const s2 = p2[1];
    if (!s1 || !s2) return 0;
    let matches = 0;
    const shorter = s1.length < s2.length ? s1 : s2;
    for (let i = 0; i < shorter.length - 5; i++) {
      if (s2.includes(s1.slice(i, i + 6))) matches++;
    }
    return matches / (shorter.length / 6);
  }
}

// ─── ML Anomaly Scorer ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class MlAnomalyScorer {
  private baselineEntropy = 4.5;
  private baselineByteVariance = 0.5;

  score(buf: Buffer, entropy: number): number {
    let score = 0;

    // Entropy deviation from normal
    const entropyDev = Math.abs(entropy - this.baselineEntropy) / 4;
    score += Math.min(0.3, entropyDev * 0.3);

    // Byte frequency analysis
    const freq = new Array(256).fill(0);
    for (const b of buf.slice(0, 4096)) freq[b]++;
    const avgFreq = buf.length / 256;
    let variance = 0;
    for (const f of freq) variance += Math.pow(f - avgFreq, 2);
    variance /= 256;
    const normVariance = Math.min(1, variance / (avgFreq * avgFreq));
    score += normVariance * 0.2;

    // Null byte ratio (packed/encrypted content)
    const nullBytes = buf.slice(0, 4096).filter(b => b === 0).length;
    const nullRatio = nullBytes / Math.min(buf.length, 4096);
    if (nullRatio > 0.3) score += 0.2;

    // Printable ratio (text vs binary)
    const printable = buf.slice(0, 4096).filter(b => b >= 32 && b <= 126).length;
    const printableRatio = printable / Math.min(buf.length, 4096);
    if (printableRatio < 0.2) score += 0.15; // Very low printable = likely encrypted/packed
    if (printableRatio > 0.98) score += 0.05; // Almost all printable = possible exfil

    // String density (many long strings = possible data dump)
    const text = buf.slice(0, 4096).toString('ascii');
    const longStrings = (text.match(/[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}/g) ?? []).length;
    if (longStrings > 50) score += 0.1;

    return Math.min(1.0, Math.round(score * 100) / 100);
  }
}

// ─── YARA Engine ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class YaraEngine {
  private rules: YaraRule[] = [];

  loadRules(rules: YaraRule[]): void {
    this.rules = rules;
  }

  scan(content: string, buf: Buffer): RegistryMatch | null {
    for (const rule of this.rules) {
      const stringMatches = new Map<string, boolean>();

      for (const s of rule.strings) {
        let matched = false;
        switch (s.type) {
          case 'text': {
            const val = s.modifiers?.includes('nocase')
              ? s.value.toLowerCase()
              : s.value;
            const src = s.modifiers?.includes('nocase')
              ? content.toLowerCase()
              : content;
            matched = src.includes(val);
            break;
          }
          case 'hex': {
            const bytes = s.value.replace(/\s/g, '').match(/.{2}/g)?.map(h => parseInt(h, 16)) ?? [];
            matched = bytes.length > 0 && bytes.every((b, i) => buf[i] === b);
            break;
          }
          case 'regex': {
            try {
              const flags = s.modifiers?.includes('nocase') ? 'i' : '';
              matched = new RegExp(s.value, flags).test(content);
            } catch { matched = false; }
            break;
          }
        }
        stringMatches.set(s.identifier, matched);
      }

      if (this.evaluateCondition(rule.condition, stringMatches, rule.strings)) {
        return {
          matched: true,
          entry: rule,
          matchType: 'YARA',
          confidence: 0.95,
          severity: rule.severity,
          category: rule.category,
          details: `YARA rule: ${rule.name}`,
          requiresImmediate: rule.severity === 'EXTINCTION' || rule.severity === 'CRITICAL',
          recommendedAction: rule.severity === 'EXTINCTION' ? 'NOTIFY_AUTHORITIES' : 'BLOCK',
        };
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  private evaluateCondition(condition: string, matches: Map<string, boolean>, strings: YaraString[]): boolean {
    if (condition === 'all of them') return Array.from(matches.values()).every(v => v);
    if (condition === 'any of them') return Array.from(matches.values()).some(v => v);

    const countMatch = condition.match(/^(\d+) of them$/);
    if (countMatch) {
      const required = parseInt(countMatch[1]);
      return Array.from(matches.values()).filter(v => v).length >= required;
    }

    const anyOfMatch = condition.match(/any of \(([^)]+)\)/);
    if (anyOfMatch) {
      const ids = anyOfMatch[1].split(',').map(s => s.trim());
      return ids.some(id => matches.get(id));
    }

    const andMatch = condition.includes(' and ');
    if (andMatch) {
      const parts = condition.split(' and ');
      return parts.every(part => {
        const trimmed = part.trim();
        if (trimmed.startsWith('any of (')) {
          const inner = trimmed.match(/any of \(([^)]+)\)/);
          if (inner) return inner[1].split(',').map(s => s.trim()).some(id => matches.get(id));
        }
        return matches.get(trimmed) ?? false;
      });
    }

    return matches.get(condition.trim()) ?? false;
  }
}

// ─── Sanctions Engine ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class SanctionsEngine {
  private entries: SanctionEntry[] = [];

  load(entries: SanctionEntry[]): void {
    this.entries = entries;
  }

  screen(text: string): RegistryMatch | null {
    const upper = text.toUpperCase();
    for (const entry of this.entries) {
      const names = [entry.entityName, ...entry.aliases];
      for (const name of names) {
        if (upper.includes(name.toUpperCase())) {
          return {
            matched: true,
            entry,
            matchType: 'SANCTIONS',
            confidence: 0.90,
            severity: entry.severity,
            category: 'SANCTIONS',
            details: `Sanctioned entity: ${entry.entityName} (${entry.sanctionAuthority})`,
            requiresImmediate: true,
            recommendedAction: 'BLOCK',
          };
        }
      }

      // Crypto address screening
      if (entry.cryptoAddresses) {
        for (const addr of entry.cryptoAddresses) {
          if (upper.includes(addr.toUpperCase())) {
            return {
              matched: true,
              entry,
              matchType: 'SANCTIONS',
              confidence: 1.0,
              severity: 'CRITICAL',
              category: 'SANCTIONS',
              details: `Sanctioned crypto address: ${addr}`,
              requiresImmediate: true,
              recommendedAction: 'BLOCK',
            };
          }
        }
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  screenIdentifiers(identifiers: Record<string, string>): RegistryMatch | null {
    for (const entry of this.entries) {
      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(identifiers)) {
        if (entry.identifiers[key]?.toUpperCase() === value.toUpperCase()) {
          return {
            matched: true,
            entry,
            matchType: 'SANCTIONS',
            confidence: 0.99,
            severity: entry.severity,
            category: 'SANCTIONS',
            details: `Sanctioned entity identifier match: ${key}=${value}`,
            requiresImmediate: true,
            recommendedAction: 'BLOCK',
          };
        }
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
}

// ─── Registry Engine ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ForbiddenRegistry {
  private patterns: PatternEntry[] = [];
  private yaraRules: YaraRule[] = [];
  private binarySigs: BinarySignature[] = [];
  private entropyRules: EntropyRule[] = [];
  private initialized = false;
  private totalScans = 0;
  private totalMatches = 0;

  private entropyCalc = new EntropyCalculator();
  private fuzzyHash = new FuzzyHashEngine();
  private mlScorer = new MlAnomalyScorer();
  private yaraEngine = new YaraEngine();
  private sanctionsEngine = new SanctionsEngine();

  initialize(): void {
    if (this.initialized) return;

    this.patterns = BUILT_IN_PATTERNS.map((p, i) => ({
      ...p,
      id: `builtin_${i}_${p.category}`,
      addedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      hitCount: 0,
    }));

    this.yaraRules = BUILT_IN_YARA_RULES.map((r, i) => ({
      ...r,
      id: `yara_${i}_${r.name}`,
      addedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      hitCount: 0,
    }));

    this.binarySigs = BUILT_IN_BINARY_SIGS.map((s, i) => ({
      ...s,
      id: `binsig_${i}_${s.category}`,
    }));

    this.entropyRules = BUILT_IN_ENTROPY_RULES.map((r, i) => ({
      ...r,
      id: `entropy_${i}`,
    }));

    this.yaraEngine.loadRules(this.yaraRules);

    this.initialized = true;
    logger.info(`[REGISTRY] Initialized — ${this.patterns.length} patterns, ${this.yaraRules.length} YARA rules, ${this.binarySigs.length} binary sigs`);
  }

  // ── Individual Check Methods ───────────────────────────────────────────────

  checkHash(content: Buffer): RegistryMatch {
    const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(content).digest('hex');
    const store = getStore('gatekeeper_forbidden_hashes');
    const entry = store.get(hash) as ForbiddenEntry | undefined;
    if (entry) {
      entry.hitCount++;
      entry.lastMatchAt = new Date().toISOString();
      store.set(hash, entry);
      logger.warn(`[REGISTRY] HASH MATCH — ${entry.category}: ${entry.description}`);
      return {
        matched: true, entry, matchType: 'HASH', confidence: 1.0,
        severity: entry.severity, category: entry.category,
        requiresImmediate: entry.autoAlert,
        recommendedAction: entry.autoFreeze ? 'FREEZE_CLIENT' : 'BLOCK',
      };
    }
    return { matched: false, matchType: 'NONE', confidence: 0 };
  }

  checkFuzzyHash(content: Buffer): RegistryMatch {
    const hash = this.fuzzyHash.compute(content);
    const store = getStore('gatekeeper_forbidden_fuzzy_hashes');
    const all = store.filter(() => true) as Array<{ fuzzyHash: string; entry: ForbiddenEntry }>;
    for (const item of all) {
      const similarity = this.fuzzyHash.similarity(hash, item.fuzzyHash);
      if (similarity > 0.85) {
        logger.warn(`[REGISTRY] FUZZY HASH MATCH — similarity=${similarity} — ${item.entry.category}`);
        return {
          matched: true, entry: item.entry, matchType: 'FUZZY_HASH',
          confidence: similarity, severity: item.entry.severity,
          category: item.entry.category, details: `Fuzzy hash similarity: ${Math.round(similarity * 100)}%`,
          requiresImmediate: similarity > 0.95,
          recommendedAction: 'BLOCK',
        };
      }
    }
    return { matched: false, matchType: 'NONE', confidence: 0 };
  }

  checkPatterns(content: string): RegistryMatch {
    for (const pattern of this.patterns) {
      const src = pattern.caseSensitive ? content : content.toLowerCase();
      const pat = pattern.caseSensitive ? pattern.pattern : pattern.pattern.toLowerCase();

      let matched = false;
      if (pattern.isRegex) {
        try {
          matched = new RegExp(pat, pattern.caseSensitive ? '' : 'i').test(content);
        } catch { /* invalid regex */ }
      } else {
        matched = src.includes(pat);
      }

      if (matched) {
        // Check context requirements
        if (pattern.requiresContext?.length) {
          const allContext = pattern.requiresContext.every(ctx =>
            content.toLowerCase().includes(ctx.toLowerCase())
          );
          if (!allContext) continue;
        }

        pattern.hitCount++;
        logger.warn(`[REGISTRY] PATTERN MATCH — ${pattern.category}: ${pattern.description}`);
        return {
          matched: true, entry: pattern, matchType: 'PATTERN',
          confidence: pattern.confidence, severity: pattern.severity,
          category: pattern.category,
          requiresImmediate: pattern.severity === 'EXTINCTION' || pattern.severity === 'CRITICAL',
          recommendedAction: pattern.severity === 'EXTINCTION' ? 'NOTIFY_AUTHORITIES' : 'BLOCK',
        };
      }
    }
    return { matched: false, matchType: 'NONE', confidence: 0 };
  }

  checkBinaryHeaders(buf: Buffer): RegistryMatch {
    for (const sig of this.binarySigs) {
      const slice = buf.slice(sig.offset, sig.offset + sig.bytes.length);
      const matched = sig.mask
        ? sig.bytes.every((b, i) => (slice[i] & (sig.mask![i] ?? 0xFF)) === (b & (sig.mask![i] ?? 0xFF)))
        : sig.bytes.every((b, i) => slice[i] === b);

      if (matched) {
        logger.warn(`[REGISTRY] BINARY SIG MATCH — ${sig.category}: ${sig.description}`);
        return {
          matched: true, entry: sig, matchType: 'BINARY_HEADER',
          confidence: 0.9, severity: sig.severity, category: sig.category,
          requiresImmediate: sig.severity === 'CRITICAL',
          recommendedAction: 'BLOCK',
        };
      }
    }
    return { matched: false, matchType: 'NONE', confidence: 0 };
  }

  checkEntropy(buf: Buffer): RegistryMatch {
    const entropy = this.entropyCalc.compute(buf);
    for (const rule of this.entropyRules) {
      if (buf.length >= rule.minSizeBytes && entropy >= rule.minEntropy && entropy <= rule.maxEntropy) {
        logger.warn(`[REGISTRY] ENTROPY MATCH — ${rule.description} entropy=${entropy}`);
        return {
          matched: true, matchType: 'ENTROPY', confidence: 0.70,
          severity: rule.severity, category: rule.category,
          details: `Shannon entropy: ${entropy} (range: ${rule.minEntropy}-${rule.maxEntropy})`,
          requiresImmediate: false,
          recommendedAction: 'ESCALATE',
        };
      }
    }
    return { matched: false, matchType: 'NONE', confidence: 0 };
  }

  checkYara(buf: Buffer): RegistryMatch {
    const content = buf.toString('utf8', 0, Math.min(buf.length, 65536));
    const result = this.yaraEngine.scan(content, buf);
    if (result) {
      logger.warn(`[REGISTRY] YARA MATCH — ${result.category}: ${result.details}`);
    }
    return result ?? { matched: false, matchType: 'NONE', confidence: 0 };
  }

  checkSanctions(content: string): RegistryMatch {
    const result = this.sanctionsEngine.screen(content);
    if (result) {
      logger.warn(`[REGISTRY] SANCTIONS MATCH — ${result.details}`);
    }
    return result ?? { matched: false, matchType: 'NONE', confidence: 0 };
  }

  // ── Full Scan — All Checks in Priority Order ───────────────────────────────

  fullScan(buf: Buffer, depth: ScanDepth = 'STANDARD'): FullScanResult {
    this.totalScans++;
    const startMs = Date.now();
    const matches: RegistryMatch[] = [];
    const entropy = this.entropyCalc.compute(buf);
    const mlScore = this.mlScorer.score(buf, entropy);
    const contentHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(buf).digest('hex');

    // 1. Binary header check (fastest — O(1))
    const headerCheck = this.checkBinaryHeaders(buf);
    if (headerCheck.matched) matches.push(headerCheck);

    // 2. Hash check (O(1) with hash map)
    const hashCheck = this.checkHash(buf);
    if (hashCheck.matched) matches.push(hashCheck);

    // 3. YARA rules
    if (depth !== 'SURFACE') {
      const yaraCheck = this.checkYara(buf);
      if (yaraCheck.matched) matches.push(yaraCheck);
    }

    // 4. Pattern check (text content)
    const isText = buf.slice(0, 512).filter(b => b === 0).length < 10;
    if (isText) {
      const text = buf.toString('utf8', 0, Math.min(buf.length, depth === 'FORENSIC' ? buf.length : 65536));

      const patternCheck = this.checkPatterns(text);
      if (patternCheck.matched) matches.push(patternCheck);

      // 5. Sanctions screening (text only)
      if (depth === 'DEEP' || depth === 'FORENSIC') {
        const sanctionCheck = this.checkSanctions(text);
        if (sanctionCheck.matched) matches.push(sanctionCheck);
      }
    }

    // 6. Entropy analysis
    if (depth === 'DEEP' || depth === 'FORENSIC') {
      const entropyCheck = this.checkEntropy(buf);
      if (entropyCheck.matched) matches.push(entropyCheck);
    }

    // 7. Fuzzy hash (forensic only)
    if (depth === 'FORENSIC') {
      const fuzzyCheck = this.checkFuzzyHash(buf);
      if (fuzzyCheck.matched) matches.push(fuzzyCheck);
    }

    // 8. ML anomaly score — always add if high
    if (mlScore > 0.7) {
      matches.push({
        matched: true, matchType: 'ML_SCORE', confidence: mlScore,
        severity: mlScore > 0.9 ? 'HIGH' : 'MEDIUM',
        category: 'MALWARE',
        details: `ML anomaly score: ${mlScore}`,
        requiresImmediate: mlScore > 0.9,
        recommendedAction: mlScore > 0.85 ? 'ESCALATE' : 'ALERT',
      });
    }

    const matched = matches.length > 0;
    if (matched) this.totalMatches++;

    const severityOrder: SeverityLevel[] = ['EXTINCTION', 'CRITICAL', 'HIGH', 'MEDIUM', 'LOW'];
    const highestSeverity = matches.reduce<SeverityLevel | undefined>((best, m) => {
      if (!m.severity) return best;
      if (!best) return m.severity;
      return severityOrder.indexOf(m.severity) < severityOrder.indexOf(best) ? m.severity : best;
    }, undefined);

    const requiresImmediate = matches.some(m => m.requiresImmediate);

    const actionPriority: RegistryMatch['recommendedAction'][] = ['NOTIFY_AUTHORITIES', 'FREEZE_CLIENT', 'BLOCK', 'ESCALATE', 'ALERT'];
    const recommendedAction = matches.reduce<RegistryMatch['recommendedAction']>((best, m) => {
      if (!m.recommendedAction) return best;
      if (!best) return m.recommendedAction;
      return actionPriority.indexOf(m.recommendedAction) < actionPriority.indexOf(best) ? m.recommendedAction : best;
    }, undefined);

    return {
      matched, matches, highestSeverity, requiresImmediate,
      scanDepth: depth, scanMs: Date.now() - startMs,
      contentHash, entropyScore: entropy, mlAnomalyScore: mlScore,
      recommendedAction,
    };
  }

  // ── Management Methods ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  addForbiddenHash(
    content: Buffer,
    category: ForbiddenCategory,
    description: string,
    addedBy: string,
    options: Partial<ForbiddenEntry> = {},
  ): ForbiddenEntry {
    const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(content).digest('hex');
    const fuzzyHashValue = this.fuzzyHash.compute(content);
    const entry: ForbiddenEntry = {
      id: 'custom_' + crypto.randomUUID(),
      hash, fuzzyHash: fuzzyHashValue,
      category, description,
      addedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      addedBy, severity: options.severity ?? 'CRITICAL',
      sourceRef: options.sourceRef,
      jurisdiction: options.jurisdiction,
      autoAlert: options.autoAlert ?? true,
      autoFreeze: options.autoFreeze ?? false,
      autoEscalate: options.autoEscalate ?? true,
      threatIntelSource: options.threatIntelSource ?? 'INTERNAL',
      verified: options.verified ?? false,
      hitCount: 0,
    };
    getStore('gatekeeper_forbidden_hashes').set(hash, entry);
    getStore('gatekeeper_forbidden_fuzzy_hashes').set(entry.id, { fuzzyHash: fuzzyHashValue, entry });
    logger.warn(`[REGISTRY] Added forbidden hash — ${category}: ${description}`);
    return entry;
  }

  addForbiddenPattern(
    pattern: string,
    category: ForbiddenCategory,
    description: string,
    options: Partial<PatternEntry> = {},
  ): PatternEntry {
    const entry: PatternEntry = {
      id: 'custom_pattern_' + crypto.randomUUID(),
      pattern, isRegex: options.isRegex ?? false,
      caseSensitive: options.caseSensitive ?? false,
      category, description,
      severity: options.severity ?? 'HIGH',
      addedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      jurisdiction: options.jurisdiction,
      confidence: options.confidence ?? 0.90,
      hitCount: 0,
    };
    this.patterns.push(entry);
    getStore('gatekeeper_custom_patterns').set(entry.id, entry);
    logger.warn(`[REGISTRY] Added pattern — ${category}: ${description}`);
    return entry;
  }

  addYaraRule(rule: Omit<YaraRule, 'id' | 'addedAt' | 'hitCount'>): YaraRule {
    const full: YaraRule = { ...rule, id: 'yara_custom_' + crypto.randomUUID(), addedAt: new Date().toISOString(), hitCount: 0 };
    this.yaraRules.push(full);
    this.yaraEngine.loadRules(this.yaraRules);
    getStore('gatekeeper_yara_rules').set(full.id, full);
    logger.warn(`[REGISTRY] Added YARA rule — ${rule.name}`);
    return full;
  }

  addSanctionEntry(entry: Omit<SanctionEntry, 'id' | 'addedAt'>): SanctionEntry {
    const full: SanctionEntry = { ...entry, id: 'sanction_' + crypto.randomUUID(), addedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
    getStore('gatekeeper_sanctions').set(full.id, full);
    this.sanctionsEngine.load([...this.getSanctions(), full]);
    logger.warn(`[REGISTRY] Added sanction entry — ${entry.entityName}`);
    return full;
  }

  removeForbiddenHash(hash: string): boolean {
    const store = getStore('gatekeeper_forbidden_hashes');
    const exists = store.get(hash);
    if (!exists) return false;
    store.delete(hash);
    logger.warn(`[REGISTRY] Removed forbidden hash: ${hash}`);
    return true;
  }

  // Simulate threat feed update
  async syncThreatFeed(source: ThreatIntelSource): Promise<ThreatFeedUpdate> {
    logger.info(`[REGISTRY] Syncing threat feed: ${source}`);
    // In production: fetch from actual threat intel APIs
    // NCMEC PhotoDNA API, INTERPOL notices, OFAC SDN list, etc.
    const update: ThreatFeedUpdate = {
      source, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      addedHashes: 0, removedHashes: 0,
      addedPatterns: 0, addedSanctions: 0,
    };
    getStore('gatekeeper_threat_feed_status').set(source, { active: true, lastSync: new Date().toISOString(), entryCount: 0 });
    return update;
  }

  private getSanctions(): SanctionEntry[] {
    return getStore('gatekeeper_sanctions').filter(() => true) as SanctionEntry[];
  }

  getStats(): RegistryStats {
    const hashStore = getStore('gatekeeper_forbidden_hashes');
    const patternStore = getStore('gatekeeper_custom_patterns');
    const feedStore = getStore('gatekeeper_threat_feed_status');

    const sources: ThreatIntelSource[] = ['INTERNAL', 'INTERPOL', 'FBI', 'EUROPOL', 'NCMEC', 'IWF', 'UNODC', 'OFAC', 'UN_SANCTIONS', 'EU_SANCTIONS'];
    const threatFeedStatus = Object.fromEntries(
      sources.map(s => [s, (feedStore.get(s) as { active: boolean; lastSync?: string; entryCount: number }) ?? { active: false, entryCount: 0 }])
    ) as RegistryStats['threatFeedStatus'];

    return {
      builtInPatterns: BUILT_IN_PATTERNS.length,
      customPatterns: (patternStore.filter(() => true) as unknown[]).length,
      yaraRules: this.yaraRules.length,
      forbiddenHashes: (hashStore.filter(() => true) as unknown[]).length,
      binarySignatures: this.binarySigs.length,
      entropyRules: this.entropyRules.length,
      sanctionEntries: this.getSanctions().length,
      totalScans: this.totalScans,
      totalMatches: this.totalMatches,
      matchRate: this.totalScans > 0 ? Math.round((this.totalMatches / this.totalScans) * 10000) / 100 : 0,
      lastUpdated: new Date().toISOString(),
      threatFeedStatus,
    };
  }
}

export const forbiddenRegistry = new ForbiddenRegistry();
forbiddenRegistry.initialize();

/**
 * UBE GATEKEEPER — CONSENT & PERMISSION REGISTRY
 * Version: 3.0.0 — CIVILIZATION INFRASTRUCTURE LEVEL
 *
 * Full consent lifecycle management.
 * Granular permissions, purpose limitation, consent versioning,
 * Withdrawal tracking, Parental consent, Delegated authority,
 * Consent proof generation, Legal basis mapping,
 * GDPR/CCPA/LGPD/PDPA/DPDP compliance per jurisdiction,
 * Immutable consent audit chain, Consent expiry enforcement,
 * Multi-party consent, Machine-readable consent receipts.
 */

import crypto from 'crypto';
import { getStore } from '../core/database';
import { createLogger } from '../core/logger';

const logger = createLogger('GatekeeperConsent');

// ─── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type TrustTier =
  | 'SANDBOX'           // Testing only — no real data
  | 'PUBLIC'            // Basic verified user
  | 'VERIFIED_ORG'      // Verified organization
  | 'CRITICAL_INFRA'    // Critical infrastructure operator
  | 'GOVERNMENT'        // Government entity
  | 'INTERNAL';         // UBE internal system

export type ProcessingMode =
  | 'ZERO_KNOWLEDGE'      // Engine sees only signatures, never content
  | 'METADATA_ONLY'       // Engine sees file structure, not content
  | 'ENCRYPTED_RUNTIME'   // Content encrypted during processing
  | 'USER_VISIBLE_ONLY'   // Only data user explicitly marked visible
  | 'ANONYMIZED'          // PII stripped before processing
  | 'PSEUDONYMIZED'       // PII replaced with tokens
  | 'AGGREGATED_ONLY';    // Only aggregate stats, no individual records

export type ConsentStatus =
  | 'ACTIVE'              // Valid and in force
  | 'WITHDRAWN'           // Explicitly withdrawn
  | 'EXPIRED'             // Past expiry date
  | 'SUSPENDED'           // Temporarily suspended
  | 'PENDING_RENEWAL'     // Needs renewal
  | 'NEVER_GIVEN'         // No consent on record
  | 'NOT_REQUIRED';       // Consent not required for this operation

export type LegalBasis =
  | 'CONSENT'             // Art. 6(1)(a) GDPR — explicit consent
  | 'CONTRACT'            // Art. 6(1)(b) — necessary for contract
  | 'LEGAL_OBLIGATION'    // Art. 6(1)(c) — required by law
  | 'VITAL_INTERESTS'     // Art. 6(1)(d) — life/death
  | 'PUBLIC_TASK'         // Art. 6(1)(e) — public interest
  | 'LEGITIMATE_INTEREST' // Art. 6(1)(f) — legitimate interests
  | 'SPECIAL_CATEGORY'    // Art. 9 — biometric/health/etc
  | 'NONE';               // No legal basis established

export type ConsentScope =
  | 'DATA_COLLECTION'     // Collecting the data
  | 'DATA_PROCESSING'     // Processing the data
  | 'DATA_STORAGE'        // Storing the data
  | 'DATA_TRANSFER'       // Transferring cross-border
  | 'DATA_SHARING'        // Sharing with third parties
  | 'DATA_ANALYSIS'       // Analyzing the data
  | 'DATA_PROFILING'      // Building profiles
  | 'DATA_MARKETING'      // Marketing use
  | 'DATA_TRAINING'       // ML training use
  | 'DATA_RETENTION';     // Retention period

export type ConsentMethod =
  | 'EXPLICIT_OPT_IN'     // Active checkbox/signature
  | 'WRITTEN_SIGNATURE'   // Physical or digital signature
  | 'ELECTRONIC_SIGNATURE'// Qualified electronic signature
  | 'API_TOKEN'           // Programmatic consent via API
  | 'CONTRACT_CLAUSE'     // Embedded in signed contract
  | 'LEGITIMATE_INTEREST' // LIA completed, no consent needed
  | 'LEGAL_REQUIREMENT'   // Mandated by law
  | 'PARENTAL_CONSENT';   // Parent/guardian consent for minor

export type WithdrawalReason =
  | 'USER_REQUEST'
  | 'EXPIRY'
  | 'VIOLATION_DETECTED'
  | 'COURT_ORDER'
  | 'REGULATORY_ACTION'
  | 'DATA_BREACH'
  | 'SYSTEM_OVERRIDE'
  | 'DEATH_OF_DATA_SUBJECT';

export interface ConsentRecord {
  id: string;
  version: number;                    // Increments on every change
  clientId: string;
  tenantId: string;
  dataSubjectId: string;              // The person whose data this is
  dataSubjectType: 'INDIVIDUAL' | 'ORGANIZATION' | 'MINOR' | 'DECEASED';
  trustTier: TrustTier;
  processingMode: ProcessingMode;
  legalBasis: LegalBasis;
  consentMethod: ConsentMethod;
  scopes: ConsentScopeEntry[];
  purposes: ConsentPurpose[];
  dataCategories: string[];
  jurisdiction: string;               // Primary jurisdiction
  additionalJurisdictions: string[];
  givenAt: string;
  expiresAt?: string;
  lastRenewedAt?: string;
  withdrawnAt?: string;
  withdrawalReason?: WithdrawalReason;
  withdrawalNote?: string;
  status: ConsentStatus;
  parentalConsentId?: string;         // If minor — parent consent ID
  delegatedBy?: string;               // If consent given by representative
  consentText: string;                // Exact text subject agreed to
  consentTextHash: string;            // Tamper detection
  consentTextVersion: string;         // Which version of T&C
  ipAddress?: string;                 // Where consent was given
  userAgent?: string;
  geoLocation?: string;
  witnessId?: string;                 // For high-trust operations
  notificationSent: boolean;          // Subject notified
  receiptId: string;                  // Machine-readable receipt
  evidenceHash: string;               // Cryptographic proof of consent
  auditTrail: ConsentAuditEntry[];
  metadata: Record<string, unknown>;
}

export interface ConsentScopeEntry {

}

}

}

}

  defaultConsentExpiryDays: number;
  requireExplicitConsentForCategories: string[];
  allowLegitimateInterestFor: string[];

}

// ─── Consent Proof Generator ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ConsentProofGenerator {
  generateEvidenceHash(record: Omit<ConsentRecord, 'evidenceHash' | 'receiptId' | 'auditTrail'>): string {
    const payload = JSON.stringify({
      id: record.id,
      clientId: record.clientId,
      tenantId: record.tenantId,
      dataSubjectId: record.dataSubjectId,
      legalBasis: record.legalBasis,
      consentMethod: record.consentMethod,
      scopes: record.scopes,
      purposes: record.purposes,
      givenAt: record.givenAt,
      consentTextHash: record.consentTextHash,
      jurisdiction: record.jurisdiction,
    });
    return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(payload).digest('hex');
  }

  generateReceipt(record: ConsentRecord, controllerName: string): ConsentReceipt {
    const receipt: Omit<ConsentReceipt, 'receiptHash' | 'signature'> = {
      receiptId: record.receiptId,
      version: '1.0',
      jurisdiction: record.jurisdiction,
      consentTimestamp: record.givenAt,
      dataControllerId: record.tenantId,
      dataControllerName: controllerName,
      dataSubjectId: record.dataSubjectId,
      purposes: record.purposes.map(p => ({
        purposeId: p.id,
        purposeName: p.name,
        legalBasis: p.legalBasis,
        dataCategories: p.dataCategories,
        retentionDays: p.retentionDays,
      })),
      consentScopes: record.scopes.filter(s => s.granted).map(s => s.scope),
      withdrawalMethod: 'API or written request to data controller',
      policyUrl: `https://ube.ai/privacy/${record.tenantId}`,
    };
    const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(receipt)).digest('hex');
    const sig = crypto.createHmac('sha256', 'ube-consent-signing-key').update(hash).digest('hex');
    return { ...receipt, receiptHash: hash, signature: sig };
  }

  verifyReceipt(receipt: ConsentReceipt): boolean {
    const { receiptHash, signature, ...rest } = receipt;
    const expectedHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(rest)).digest('hex');
    if (expectedHash !== receiptHash) return false;
    const expectedSig = crypto.createHmac('sha256', 'ube-consent-signing-key').update(receiptHash).digest('hex');
    return expectedSig === signature;
  }
}

// ─── Jurisdiction Consent Rules ───────────────────────────────────────────────

class JurisdictionConsentRules {
  private rules: Record<string, {
    minConsentAge: number;
    requiresParentalBelow: number;
    withdrawalRightDays: number;
    maxRetentionDays: number;
    requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: boolean;
    allowsLegitimateInterest: boolean;
    requiresDPIA: boolean;
    consentMustBeFreely: boolean;
    rightToErasure: boolean;
    portabilityRight: boolean;
    profitingFromDataAllowed: boolean;
  }> = {
    EU:  { minConsentAge: 16, requiresParentalBelow: 16, withdrawalRightDays: 0, maxRetentionDays: 365, requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: true, allowsLegitimateInterest: true, requiresDPIA: true, consentMustBeFreely: true, rightToErasure: true, portabilityRight: true, profitingFromDataAllowed: false },
    DE:  { minConsentAge: 16, requiresParentalBelow: 16, withdrawalRightDays: 0, maxRetentionDays: 365, requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: true, allowsLegitimateInterest: false, requiresDPIA: true, consentMustBeFreely: true, rightToErasure: true, portabilityRight: true, profitingFromDataAllowed: false },
    US:  { minConsentAge: 13, requiresParentalBelow: 13, withdrawalRightDays: 15, maxRetentionDays: 730, requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: true, allowsLegitimateInterest: true, requiresDPIA: false, consentMustBeFreely: false, rightToErasure: true, portabilityRight: false, profitingFromDataAllowed: true },
    GB:  { minConsentAge: 13, requiresParentalBelow: 13, withdrawalRightDays: 0, maxRetentionDays: 365, requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: true, allowsLegitimateInterest: true, requiresDPIA: true, consentMustBeFreely: true, rightToErasure: true, portabilityRight: true, profitingFromDataAllowed: false },
    CA:  { minConsentAge: 14, requiresParentalBelow: 14, withdrawalRightDays: 0, maxRetentionDays: 365, requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: true, allowsLegitimateInterest: false, requiresDPIA: false, consentMustBeFreely: true, rightToErasure: false, portabilityRight: false, profitingFromDataAllowed: false },
    AU:  { minConsentAge: 15, requiresParentalBelow: 15, withdrawalRightDays: 0, maxRetentionDays: 730, requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: true, allowsLegitimateInterest: true, requiresDPIA: false, consentMustBeFreely: true, rightToErasure: false, portabilityRight: false, profitingFromDataAllowed: false },
    IN:  { minConsentAge: 18, requiresParentalBelow: 18, withdrawalRightDays: 0, maxRetentionDays: 365, requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: true, allowsLegitimateInterest: false, requiresDPIA: false, consentMustBeFreely: true, rightToErasure: true, portabilityRight: false, profitingFromDataAllowed: false },
    BR:  { minConsentAge: 18, requiresParentalBelow: 18, withdrawalRightDays: 0, maxRetentionDays: 365, requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: true, allowsLegitimateInterest: true, requiresDPIA: true, consentMustBeFreely: true, rightToErasure: true, portabilityRight: true, profitingFromDataAllowed: false },
    JP:  { minConsentAge: 15, requiresParentalBelow: 15, withdrawalRightDays: 0, maxRetentionDays: 365, requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: true, allowsLegitimateInterest: false, requiresDPIA: false, consentMustBeFreely: true, rightToErasure: true, portabilityRight: false, profitingFromDataAllowed: false },
    KR:  { minConsentAge: 14, requiresParentalBelow: 14, withdrawalRightDays: 0, maxRetentionDays: 365, requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: true, allowsLegitimateInterest: false, requiresDPIA: true, consentMustBeFreely: true, rightToErasure: true, portabilityRight: true, profitingFromDataAllowed: false },
    CN:  { minConsentAge: 14, requiresParentalBelow: 14, withdrawalRightDays: 0, maxRetentionDays: 365, requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: true, allowsLegitimateInterest: false, requiresDPIA: true, consentMustBeFreely: false, rightToErasure: true, portabilityRight: false, profitingFromDataAllowed: false },
    SG:  { minConsentAge: 16, requiresParentalBelow: 16, withdrawalRightDays: 10, maxRetentionDays: 730, requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: true, allowsLegitimateInterest: true, requiresDPIA: false, consentMustBeFreely: true, rightToErasure: false, portabilityRight: false, profitingFromDataAllowed: false },
    ZA:  { minConsentAge: 18, requiresParentalBelow: 18, withdrawalRightDays: 0, maxRetentionDays: 365, requiresExplicitForSpecialCategory: true, allowsLegitimateInterest: true, requiresDPIA: true, consentMustBeFreely: true, rightToErasure: true, portabilityRight: false, profitingFromDataAllowed: false },
  };

  getRule(jurisdiction: string) {
    return this.rules[jurisdiction] ?? this.rules['EU']; // Default to EU (most restrictive)
  }

  getMinConsentAge(jurisdiction: string): number {
    return this.getRule(jurisdiction).minConsentAge;
  }

  requiresParentalConsent(jurisdiction: string, age: number): boolean {
    return age < this.getRule(jurisdiction).requiresParentalBelow;
  }

  allowsLegitimateInterest(jurisdiction: string): boolean {
    return this.getRule(jurisdiction).allowsLegitimateInterest;
  }

  getMaxRetentionDays(jurisdiction: string): number {
    return this.getRule(jurisdiction).maxRetentionDays;
  }

  hasRightToErasure(jurisdiction: string): boolean {
    return this.getRule(jurisdiction).rightToErasure;
  }
}

// ─── Consent Expiry Manager ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ConsentExpiryManager {
  private checkIntervalMs = 3600000; // Check every hour
  private timer?: NodeJS.Timeout;

  start(onExpiry: (record: ConsentRecord) => void): void {
    this.timer = setInterval(() => {
      this.checkExpiries(onExpiry);
    }, this.checkIntervalMs);
    logger.info('[ConsentExpiry] Expiry monitor started');
  }

  stop(): void {
    if (this.timer) clearInterval(this.timer);
  }

  private checkExpiries(onExpiry: (record: ConsentRecord) => void): void {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    // In production: query all active consents and check expiresAt
    // For now: log that check ran
    logger.info(`[ConsentExpiry] Checking consent expiries at ${now}`);
  }

  isExpired(record: ConsentRecord): boolean {
    if (!record.expiresAt) return false;
    return new Date() > new Date(record.expiresAt);
  }

  isNearExpiry(record: ConsentRecord, warningDays = 30): boolean {
    if (!record.expiresAt) return false;
    const warningMs = warningDays * 86400000;
    return new Date(record.expiresAt).getTime() - Date.now() < warningMs;
  }
}

// ─── Consent Registry ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ConsentRegistry {
  private proofGenerator = new ConsentProofGenerator();
  private jurisdictionRules = new JurisdictionConsentRules();
  private expiryManager = new ConsentExpiryManager();
  private policies = new Map<string, ConsentPolicy>();

  constructor() {
    this.expiryManager.start(record => {
      this.markExpired(record.id, record.tenantId);
    });
  }

  // ── Policy Management ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  setPolicy(policy: ConsentPolicy): void {
    this.policies.set(policy.tenantId, policy);
    logger.info(`[Consent] Policy set for tenant: ${policy.tenantId}`);
  }

  getPolicy(tenantId: string): ConsentPolicy {
    return this.policies.get(tenantId) ?? this.getDefaultPolicy(tenantId);
  }

  private getDefaultPolicy(tenantId: string): ConsentPolicy {
    return {
      tenantId,
      defaultLegalBasis: 'CONSENT',
      defaultProcessingMode: 'ZERO_KNOWLEDGE',
      defaultConsentExpiryDays: 365,
      requireExplicitConsentForCategories: ['HEALTH', 'BIOMETRIC', 'GENETIC', 'CHILDREN', 'CRIMINAL', 'POLITICAL', 'RELIGIOUS'],
      allowLegitimateInterestFor: ['INTERNAL', 'ANALYTICS'],
      requireParentalConsentUnderAge: 16,
      minConsentAge: 16,
      allowWithdrawalDelay: false,
      withdrawalDelayDays: 0,
      requireDPIA: true,
      requireDPO: false,
      consentTextVersioning: true,
      notifyOnWithdrawal: true,
      notifyOnExpiry: true,
      autoRenewEnabled: false,
      crossBorderPolicy: 'REQUIRE_CONSENT',
    };
  }

  // ── Consent Registration ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

  register(params: {
    clientId: string;
    tenantId: string;
    dataSubjectId: string;
    dataSubjectType: ConsentRecord['dataSubjectType'];
    trustTier: TrustTier;
    processingMode: ProcessingMode;
    legalBasis: LegalBasis;
    consentMethod: ConsentMethod;
    scopes: ConsentScopeEntry[];
    purposes: ConsentPurpose[];
    dataCategories: string[];
    jurisdiction: string;
    additionalJurisdictions?: string[];
    consentText: string;
    consentTextVersion: string;
    expiryDays?: number;
    parentalConsentId?: string;
    delegatedBy?: string;
    ipAddress?: string;
    userAgent?: string;
    geoLocation?: string;
    witnessId?: string;
    metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
  }): ConsentRecord {
    const policy = this.getPolicy(params.tenantId);
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const id = `consent_${params.tenantId}_${params.dataSubjectId}_${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
    const receiptId = `receipt_${crypto.randomUUID()}`;

    // Validate legal basis for jurisdiction
    const jsRule = this.jurisdictionRules.getRule(params.jurisdiction);
    if (!jsRule.allowsLegitimateInterest && params.legalBasis === 'LEGITIMATE_INTEREST') {
      logger.warn(`[Consent] Legitimate interest not allowed in ${params.jurisdiction} — upgrading to CONSENT`);
      params.legalBasis = 'CONSENT';
    }

    const expiryDays = params.expiryDays ?? policy.defaultConsentExpiryDays;
    const expiresAt = expiryDays > 0
      ? new Date(Date.now() + expiryDays * 86400000).toISOString()
      : undefined;

    const consentTextHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(params.consentText).digest('hex');

    const firstAuditEntry: ConsentAuditEntry = {
      timestamp: now,
      action: 'GIVEN',
      actorId: params.clientId,
      actorType: params.parentalConsentId ? 'GUARDIAN' : 'DATA_SUBJECT',
      details: `Consent given via ${params.consentMethod} for ${params.scopes.filter(s => s.granted).length} scopes`,
      hash: '',
      ipAddress: params.ipAddress,
    };
    firstAuditEntry.hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(firstAuditEntry)).digest('hex');

    const partial = {
      id, version: 1, receiptId,
      clientId: params.clientId,
      tenantId: params.tenantId,
      dataSubjectId: params.dataSubjectId,
      dataSubjectType: params.dataSubjectType,
      trustTier: params.trustTier,
      processingMode: params.processingMode,
      legalBasis: params.legalBasis,
      consentMethod: params.consentMethod,
      scopes: params.scopes,
      purposes: params.purposes,
      dataCategories: params.dataCategories,
      jurisdiction: params.jurisdiction,
      additionalJurisdictions: params.additionalJurisdictions ?? [],
      givenAt: now,
      expiresAt,
      status: 'ACTIVE' as ConsentStatus,
      parentalConsentId: params.parentalConsentId,
      delegatedBy: params.delegatedBy,
      consentText: params.consentText,
      consentTextHash,
      consentTextVersion: params.consentTextVersion,
      ipAddress: params.ipAddress,
      userAgent: params.userAgent,
      geoLocation: params.geoLocation,
      witnessId: params.witnessId,
      notificationSent: false,
      metadata: params.metadata ?? {},
      auditTrail: [firstAuditEntry],
    };

    const evidenceHash = this.proofGenerator.generateEvidenceHash(partial);
    const record: ConsentRecord = { ...partial, evidenceHash };

    const store = getStore(`gatekeeper_consent_${params.tenantId}`);
    store.set(id, record);

    // Index by dataSubjectId for fast lookup
    const subjectIndex = getStore(`gatekeeper_consent_subject_${params.tenantId}`);
    const existing = (subjectIndex.get(params.dataSubjectId) as string[] | undefined) ?? [];
    subjectIndex.set(params.dataSubjectId, [...existing, id]);

    logger.info(`[Consent] Registered: ${id} — ${params.legalBasis} — ${params.jurisdiction} — ${params.scopes.filter(s => s.granted).length} scopes`);
    return record;
  }

  // ── Consent Check ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  check(
    tenantId: string,
    dataSubjectId: string,
    scope: ConsentScope,
    dataCategory: string,
    jurisdiction: string,
  ): ConsentCheckResult {
    const record = this.getActive(tenantId, dataSubjectId);
    const policy = this.getPolicy(tenantId);
    const jsRule = this.jurisdictionRules.getRule(jurisdiction);

    // No record at all
    if (!record) {
      const requiresExplicit = policy.requireExplicitConsentForCategories.includes(dataCategory);
      if (requiresExplicit) {
        return {
          allowed: false,
          status: 'NEVER_GIVEN',
          processingMode: policy.defaultProcessingMode,
          conditions: [`Explicit consent required for ${dataCategory} in ${jurisdiction}`],
          requiresRenewal: false,
          requiresParentalConsent: false,
          blockReason: `No consent record found for ${dataCategory} — explicit consent required`,
          auditRequired: true,
        };
      }
      // Check if legitimate interest covers this
      if (jsRule.allowsLegitimateInterest && policy.allowLegitimateInterestFor.includes(scope)) {
        return {
          allowed: true,
          status: 'NOT_REQUIRED',
          legalBasis: 'LEGITIMATE_INTEREST',
          processingMode: policy.defaultProcessingMode,
          conditions: ['Legitimate interest applies — ensure LIA completed'],
          requiresRenewal: false,
          requiresParentalConsent: false,
          auditRequired: true,
        };
      }
      return {
        allowed: false,
        status: 'NEVER_GIVEN',
        processingMode: policy.defaultProcessingMode,
        conditions: [],
        requiresRenewal: false,
        requiresParentalConsent: false,
        blockReason: 'No consent record found',
        auditRequired: true,
      };
    }

    // Check expiry
    if (this.expiryManager.isExpired(record)) {
      this.markExpired(record.id, tenantId);
      return {
        allowed: false,
        status: 'EXPIRED',
        processingMode: record.processingMode,
        conditions: [`Consent expired at ${record.expiresAt}`],
        requiresRenewal: true,
        requiresParentalConsent: !!record.parentalConsentId,
        blockReason: `Consent expired at ${record.expiresAt}`,
        consentId: record.id,
        auditRequired: true,
      };
    }

    // Check if withdrawn
    if (record.status === 'WITHDRAWN') {
      return {
        allowed: false,
        status: 'WITHDRAWN',
        processingMode: record.processingMode,
        conditions: [],
        requiresRenewal: false,
        requiresParentalConsent: false,
        blockReason: `Consent withdrawn at ${record.withdrawnAt}: ${record.withdrawalReason}`,
        consentId: record.id,
        auditRequired: true,
      };
    }

    // Check if suspended
    if (record.status === 'SUSPENDED') {
      return {
        allowed: false,
        status: 'SUSPENDED',
        processingMode: record.processingMode,
        conditions: ['Consent suspended — contact data controller'],
        requiresRenewal: false,
        requiresParentalConsent: false,
        blockReason: 'Consent is currently suspended',
        consentId: record.id,
        auditRequired: true,
      };
    }

    // Check scope
    const scopeEntry = record.scopes.find(s => s.scope === scope);
    if (!scopeEntry?.granted) {
      return {
        allowed: false,
        status: 'ACTIVE',
        legalBasis: record.legalBasis,
        processingMode: record.processingMode,
        conditions: [`Scope ${scope} not granted`],
        requiresRenewal: false,
        requiresParentalConsent: false,
        blockReason: `Required scope "${scope}" not granted in consent record`,
        consentId: record.id,
        auditRequired: true,
      };
    }

    // Check scope expiry
    if (scopeEntry.expiresAt && new Date() > new Date(scopeEntry.expiresAt)) {
      return {
        allowed: false,
        status: 'EXPIRED',
        processingMode: record.processingMode,
        conditions: [`Scope ${scope} expired at ${scopeEntry.expiresAt}`],
        requiresRenewal: true,
        requiresParentalConsent: !!record.parentalConsentId,
        blockReason: `Scope "${scope}" consent expired`,
        consentId: record.id,
        auditRequired: true,
      };
    }

    // Check data category coverage
    if (!record.dataCategories.includes(dataCategory) && !record.dataCategories.includes('ALL')) {
      return {
        allowed: false,
        status: 'ACTIVE',
        legalBasis: record.legalBasis,
        processingMode: record.processingMode,
        conditions: [`Data category ${dataCategory} not covered`],
        requiresRenewal: false,
        requiresParentalConsent: false,
        blockReason: `Data category "${dataCategory}" not included in consent`,
        consentId: record.id,
        auditRequired: true,
      };
    }

    const conditions: string[] = [...(scopeEntry.conditions ?? [])];
    if (this.expiryManager.isNearExpiry(record)) {
      conditions.push(`Consent expires soon at ${record.expiresAt} — schedule renewal`);
    }

    return {
      allowed: true,
      status: 'ACTIVE',
      legalBasis: record.legalBasis,
      processingMode: record.processingMode,
      conditions,
      expiresAt: record.expiresAt,
      requiresRenewal: this.expiryManager.isNearExpiry(record),
      requiresParentalConsent: false,
      consentId: record.id,
      receiptId: record.receiptId,
      auditRequired: false,
    };
  }

  // ── Withdrawal ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  withdraw(
    tenantId: string,
    consentId: string,
    reason: WithdrawalReason,
    withdrawnBy: string,
    note?: string,
  ): ConsentRecord {
    const store = getStore(`gatekeeper_consent_${tenantId}`);
    const record = store.get(consentId) as ConsentRecord | undefined;
    if (!record) throw new Error(`CONSENT_NOT_FOUND: ${consentId}`);

    const auditEntry: ConsentAuditEntry = {
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      action: 'WITHDRAWN',
      actorId: withdrawnBy,
      actorType: 'DATA_SUBJECT',
      details: `Consent withdrawn: ${reason}${note ? ` — ${note}` : ''}`,
      hash: '',
    };
    auditEntry.hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(auditEntry)).digest('hex');

    const updated: ConsentRecord = {
      ...record,
      version: record.version + 1,
      status: 'WITHDRAWN',
      withdrawnAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      withdrawalReason: reason,
      withdrawalNote: note,
      auditTrail: [...record.auditTrail, auditEntry],
    };

    store.set(consentId, updated);
    logger.warn(`[Consent] Withdrawn: ${consentId} by ${withdrawnBy} — reason: ${reason}`);
    return updated;
  }

  // ── Renewal ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  renew(
    tenantId: string,
    consentId: string,
    renewedBy: string,
    newExpiryDays?: number,
    newConsentText?: string,
  ): ConsentRecord {
    const store = getStore(`gatekeeper_consent_${tenantId}`);
    const record = store.get(consentId) as ConsentRecord | undefined;
    if (!record) throw new Error(`CONSENT_NOT_FOUND: ${consentId}`);

    const policy = this.getPolicy(tenantId);
    const expiryDays = newExpiryDays ?? policy.defaultConsentExpiryDays;

    const auditEntry: ConsentAuditEntry = {
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      action: 'RENEWED',
      actorId: renewedBy,
      actorType: 'DATA_SUBJECT',
      details: `Consent renewed for ${expiryDays} days`,
      hash: '',
    };
    auditEntry.hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(auditEntry)).digest('hex');

    const updated: ConsentRecord = {
      ...record,
      version: record.version + 1,
      status: 'ACTIVE',
      lastRenewedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + expiryDays * 86400000).toISOString(),
      consentText: newConsentText ?? record.consentText,
      consentTextHash: newConsentText
        ? crypto.createHash('sha256').update(newConsentText).digest('hex')
        : record.consentTextHash,
      auditTrail: [...record.auditTrail, auditEntry],
    };

    store.set(consentId, updated);
    logger.info(`[Consent] Renewed: ${consentId} — expires ${updated.expiresAt}`);
    return updated;
  }

  // ── Suspension ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  suspend(tenantId: string, consentId: string, reason: string, suspendedBy: string): ConsentRecord {
    const store = getStore(`gatekeeper_consent_${tenantId}`);
    const record = store.get(consentId) as ConsentRecord | undefined;
    if (!record) throw new Error(`CONSENT_NOT_FOUND: ${consentId}`);

    const auditEntry: ConsentAuditEntry = {
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      action: 'SUSPENDED',
      actorId: suspendedBy,
      actorType: 'OPERATOR',
      details: `Suspended: ${reason}`,
      hash: '',
    };
    auditEntry.hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(auditEntry)).digest('hex');

    const updated: ConsentRecord = {
      ...record,
      version: record.version + 1,
      status: 'SUSPENDED',
      auditTrail: [...record.auditTrail, auditEntry],
    };

    store.set(consentId, updated);
    logger.warn(`[Consent] Suspended: ${consentId} by ${suspendedBy} — ${reason}`);
    return updated;
  }

  // ── Lookup Methods ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  getActive(tenantId: string, dataSubjectId: string): ConsentRecord | null {
    const subjectIndex = getStore(`gatekeeper_consent_subject_${tenantId}`);
    const ids = (subjectIndex.get(dataSubjectId) as string[] | undefined) ?? [];
    const store = getStore(`gatekeeper_consent_${tenantId}`);

    const records = ids
      .map(id => store.get(id) as ConsentRecord | undefined)
      .filter((r): r is ConsentRecord => !!r && r.status === 'ACTIVE');

    if (!records.length) return null;
    // Return most recent
    return records.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.givenAt).getTime() - new Date(a.givenAt).getTime())[0];
  }

  getById(tenantId: string, consentId: string): ConsentRecord | null {
    return (getStore(`gatekeeper_consent_${tenantId}`).get(consentId) as ConsentRecord) ?? null;
  }

  getAll(tenantId: string, dataSubjectId?: string): ConsentRecord[] {
    const store = getStore(`gatekeeper_consent_${tenantId}`);
    const all = store.filter(() => true) as ConsentRecord[];
    if (dataSubjectId) return all.filter(r => r.dataSubjectId === dataSubjectId);
    return all;
  }

  private markExpired(consentId: string, tenantId: string): void {
    const store = getStore(`gatekeeper_consent_${tenantId}`);
    const record = store.get(consentId) as ConsentRecord | undefined;
    if (!record || record.status !== 'ACTIVE') return;

    const auditEntry: ConsentAuditEntry = {
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      action: 'EXPIRED',
      actorId: 'system',
      actorType: 'SYSTEM',
      details: `Consent expired at ${record.expiresAt}`,
      hash: '',
    };
    auditEntry.hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(auditEntry)).digest('hex');

    store.set(consentId, {
      ...record,
      version: record.version + 1,
      status: 'EXPIRED',
      auditTrail: [...record.auditTrail, auditEntry],
    });

    logger.warn(`[Consent] Expired: ${consentId}`);
  }

  // ── Legitimate Interest Assessment ────────────────────────────────────────

  registerLia(lia: Omit<LegitimateInterestAssessment, 'id'>): LegitimateInterestAssessment {
    const full: LegitimateInterestAssessment = { ...lia, id: `lia_${crypto.randomUUID()}` };
    getStore(`gatekeeper_lia_${lia.tenantId}`).set(full.id, full);
    logger.info(`[Consent] LIA registered: ${full.id} — ${lia.purpose}`);
    return full;
  }

  getLia(tenantId: string, purpose: string): LegitimateInterestAssessment | null {
    const store = getStore(`gatekeeper_lia_${tenantId}`);
    const all = store.filter((l: LegitimateInterestAssessment) => l.purpose === purpose) as LegitimateInterestAssessment[];
    return all[0] ?? null;
  }

  // ── Receipt Generation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  generateReceipt(tenantId: string, consentId: string, controllerName: string): ConsentReceipt | null {
    const record = this.getById(tenantId, consentId);
    if (!record) return null;
    return this.proofGenerator.generateReceipt(record, controllerName);
  }

  verifyReceipt(receipt: ConsentReceipt): boolean {
    return this.proofGenerator.verifyReceipt(receipt);
  }

  // ── Erasure Request (GDPR Right to be Forgotten) ──────────────────────────

  processErasureRequest(
    tenantId: string,
    dataSubjectId: string,
    requestedBy: string,
    jurisdiction: string,
  ): { erased: number; retained: number; retentionReasons: string[] } {
    const jsRule = this.jurisdictionRules.getRule(jurisdiction);
    if (!jsRule.rightToErasure) {
      return { erased: 0, retained: 0, retentionReasons: [`Right to erasure not available in ${jurisdiction}`] };
    }

    const records = this.getAll(tenantId, dataSubjectId);
    let erased = 0;
    let retained = 0;
    const retentionReasons: string[] = [];

    for (const record of records) {
      // Can't erase if legal obligation requires retention
      if (record.legalBasis === 'LEGAL_OBLIGATION') {
        retained++;
        retentionReasons.push(`Record ${record.id} retained: legal obligation`);
        continue;
      }
      if (record.legalBasis === 'PUBLIC_TASK') {
        retained++;
        retentionReasons.push(`Record ${record.id} retained: public task`);
        continue;
      }
      this.withdraw(tenantId, record.id, 'USER_REQUEST', requestedBy, 'GDPR erasure request');
      erased++;
    }

    logger.warn(`[Consent] Erasure request: ${dataSubjectId} — erased=${erased} retained=${retained}`);
    return { erased, retained, retentionReasons };
  }

  // ── Stats ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  getStats(tenantId: string): ConsentStats {
    const all = this.getAll(tenantId);
    const byLegalBasis: Record<string, number> = {};
    const byJurisdiction: Record<string, number> = {};
    const byDataCategory: Record<string, number> = {};

    for (const r of all) {
      byLegalBasis[r.legalBasis] = (byLegalBasis[r.legalBasis] ?? 0) + 1;
      byJurisdiction[r.jurisdiction] = (byJurisdiction[r.jurisdiction] ?? 0) + 1;
      for (const cat of r.dataCategories) {
        byDataCategory[cat] = (byDataCategory[cat] ?? 0) + 1;
      }
    }

    const active = all.filter(r => r.status === 'ACTIVE').length;
    const withdrawn = all.filter(r => r.status === 'WITHDRAWN').length;
    const expired = all.filter(r => r.status === 'EXPIRED').length;
    const pendingRenewal = all.filter(r => r.status === 'ACTIVE' && this.expiryManager.isNearExpiry(r)).length;

    const avgAge = all.length > 0
      ? all.reduce((s, r) => s + (Date.now() - new Date(r.givenAt).getTime()), 0) / all.length / 86400000
      : 0;

    return {
      tenantId,
      totalConsents: all.length,
      activeConsents: active,
      withdrawnConsents: withdrawn,
      expiredConsents: expired,
      pendingRenewal,
      byLegalBasis: byLegalBasis as Record<LegalBasis, number>,
      byJurisdiction,
      byDataCategory,
      avgConsentAge: Math.round(avgAge),
      withdrawalRate: all.length > 0 ? Math.round((withdrawn / all.length) * 10000) / 100 : 0,
      renewalRate: all.length > 0 ? Math.round(((active - pendingRenewal) / all.length) * 10000) / 100 : 0,
      generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
  }

  // ── Jurisdiction Rules Access ──────────────────────────────────────────────

  getJurisdictionRule(jurisdiction: string) {
    return this.jurisdictionRules.getRule(jurisdiction);
  }

  requiresParentalConsent(jurisdiction: string, age: number): boolean {
    return this.jurisdictionRules.requiresParentalConsent(jurisdiction, age);
  }
}

export const consentRegistry = new ConsentRegistry();
