// ============================================================
// FIELD ARITHMETIC
// ============================================================

export class FieldArithmetic {
  readonly prime: bigint;
  readonly fieldSize: FieldSize;

  static readonly PRIMES: Record<FieldSize, bigint> = {
    [FieldSize.BN128]: 21888242871839275222246405745257275088548364400416034343698204186575808495617n,
    [FieldSize.BLS12_381]: 52435875175126190479447740508185965837690552500527637822603658699938581184513n,
    [FieldSize.SECP256K1]: 115792089237316195423570985008687907853269984665640564039457584007908834671663n,
    [FieldSize.ED25519]: 57896044618658097711785492504343953926634992332820282019728792003956564819949n,
  };

  constructor(fieldSize: FieldSize = FieldSize.BN128) {
    this.fieldSize = fieldSize;
    this.prime = FieldArithmetic.PRIMES[fieldSize];
  }

  add(a: bigint, b: bigint): bigint {
    return ((a + b) % this.prime + this.prime) % this.prime;
  }

  sub(a: bigint, b: bigint): bigint {
    return ((a - b) % this.prime + this.prime) % this.prime;
  }

  mul(a: bigint, b: bigint): bigint {
    return (a * b) % this.prime;
  }

  pow(base: bigint, exp: bigint): bigint {
    let result = 1n;
    base = base % this.prime;
    while (exp > 0n) {
      if (exp % 2n === 1n) result = (result * base) % this.prime;
      exp = exp / 2n;
      base = (base * base) % this.prime;
    }
    return result;
  }

  inv(a: bigint): bigint {
    return this.pow(a, this.prime - 2n);
  }

  div(a: bigint, b: bigint): bigint {
    return this.mul(a, this.inv(b));
  }

  neg(a: bigint): bigint {
    return (this.prime - (a % this.prime)) % this.prime;
  }

  isZero(a: bigint): boolean {
    return a % this.prime === 0n;
  }

  random(): bigint {
    const bytes = crypto.randomBytes(32);
    const num = BigInt('0x' + bytes.toString('hex'));
    return num % this.prime;
  }

  fromBytes(bytes: Buffer): bigint {
    return BigInt('0x' + bytes.toString('hex')) % this.prime;
  }

  toBytes(n: bigint): Buffer {
    const hex = n.toString(16).padStart(64, '0');
    return Buffer.from(hex, 'hex');
  }

  hashToField(data: Buffer | string): bigint {
    const buf = typeof data === 'string' ? Buffer.from(data, 'utf8') : data;
    const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(buf).digest('hex');
    return BigInt('0x' + hash) % this.prime;
  }

  multiexp(bases: bigint[], exponents: bigint[]): bigint {
    if (bases.length !== exponents.length) throw new Error('Length mismatch');
    return bases.reduce((acc, base, i) => this.mul(acc, this.pow(base, exponents[i]!)), 1n);
  }

  innerProduct(a: bigint[], b: bigint[]): bigint {
    if (a.length !== b.length) throw new Error('Length mismatch');
    return a.reduce((acc, ai, i) => this.add(acc, this.mul(ai, b[i]!)), 0n);
  }
}

// ============================================================
// PEDERSEN COMMITMENT SCHEME
// ============================================================

export class PedersenScheme {
  private readonly field: FieldArithmetic;
  private readonly G: bigint;
  private readonly H: bigint;

  constructor(field: FieldArithmetic) {
    this.field = field;
    this.G = field.hashToField(Buffer.from('UBE_PEDERSEN_GENERATOR_G'));
    this.H = field.hashToField(Buffer.from('UBE_PEDERSEN_GENERATOR_H'));
  }

  commit(value: bigint, blindingFactor?: bigint): PedersenCommitment {
    const r = blindingFactor ?? this.field.random();
    const gv = this.field.pow(this.G, value);
    const hr = this.field.pow(this.H, r);
    const commitment = this.field.mul(gv, hr);

    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      commitment,
      generator: { x: this.G, y: this.H, curve: 'BN128', isInfinity: false },
      blindingFactor: r,
      value,
      createdAt: new Date(),
    };
  }

  addCommitments(c1: PedersenCommitment, c2: PedersenCommitment): bigint {
    return this.field.mul(c1.commitment, c2.commitment);
  }

  verifyCommitment(commitment: bigint, value: bigint, blindingFactor: bigint): boolean {
    const gv = this.field.pow(this.G, value);
    const hr = this.field.pow(this.H, blindingFactor);
    const expected = this.field.mul(gv, hr);
    return commitment === expected;
  }

  batchCommit(values: bigint[]): { commitments: PedersenCommitment[]; sum: bigint } {
    const commitments = values.map(v => this.commit(v));
    const sum = commitments.reduce((acc, c) => this.field.mul(acc, c.commitment), 1n);
    return { commitments, sum };
  }
}

// ============================================================
// MERKLE TREE (ZK-FRIENDLY)
// ============================================================

export class ZKMerkleTree {
  private readonly field: FieldArithmetic;
  private tree: MerkleTree;
  private readonly depth: number;

  constructor(field: FieldArithmetic, depth: number = 20) {
    this.field = field;
    this.depth = depth;
    this.tree = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      root: 0n,
      depth,
      leaves: [],
      nodes: new Map(),
      nullifiers: new Set(),
    };
  }

  private poseidonHash(inputs: bigint[]): bigint {
    const combined = inputs.reduce((acc, v) => acc + v.toString(16), '');
    const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(combined).digest('hex');
    return BigInt('0x' + hash) % this.field.prime;
  }

  private hashPair(left: bigint, right: bigint): bigint {
    return this.poseidonHash([left, right]);
  }

  insert(leaf: bigint): number {
    const index = this.tree.leaves.length;
    this.tree.leaves.push(leaf);
    this.rebuild();
    return index;
  }

  insertBatch(leaves: bigint[]): number[] {
    const indices: number[] = [];
    for (const leaf of leaves) {
      indices.push(this.tree.leaves.length);
      this.tree.leaves.push(leaf);
    }
    this.rebuild();
    return indices;
  }

  private rebuild(): void {
    const size = Math.pow(2, this.depth);
    const paddedLeaves: bigint[] = [...this.tree.leaves];
    while (paddedLeaves.length < size) paddedLeaves.push(0n);

    let level = paddedLeaves.map((l, i) => {
      this.tree.nodes.set(`0-${i}`, l);
      return l;
    });

    for (let d = 1; d <= this.depth; d++) {
      const next: bigint[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < level.length; i += 2) {
        const parent = this.hashPair(level[i]!, level[i + 1] ?? 0n);
        this.tree.nodes.set(`${d}-${Math.floor(i / 2)}`, parent);
        next.push(parent);
      }
      level = next;
    }

    this.tree.root = level[0] ?? 0n;
  }

  generateProof(leafIndex: number): MerkleProof {
    const size = Math.pow(2, this.depth);
    const paddedLeaves: bigint[] = [...this.tree.leaves];
    while (paddedLeaves.length < size) paddedLeaves.push(0n);

    const siblings: bigint[] = [];
    const pathBits: boolean[] = [];
    let idx = leafIndex;

    for (let d = 0; d < this.depth; d++) {
      const siblingIdx = idx % 2 === 0 ? idx + 1 : idx - 1;
      siblings.push(this.tree.nodes.get(`${d}-${siblingIdx}`) ?? 0n);
      pathBits.push(idx % 2 === 1);
      idx = Math.floor(idx / 2);
    }

    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      leaf: this.tree.leaves[leafIndex] ?? 0n,
      leafIndex,
      siblings,
      pathBits,
      root: this.tree.root,
      valid: true,
    };
  }

  verifyProof(proof: MerkleProof): boolean {
    let current = proof.leaf;
    for (let i = 0; i < proof.siblings.length; i++) {
      const sibling = proof.siblings[i]!;
      const isRight = proof.pathBits[i];
      current = isRight
        ? this.hashPair(sibling, current)
        : this.hashPair(current, sibling);
    }
    return current === proof.root && proof.root === this.tree.root;
  }

  addNullifier(nullifier: bigint): boolean {
    if (this.tree.nullifiers.has(nullifier)) return false;
    this.tree.nullifiers.add(nullifier);
    return true;
  }

  isNullified(nullifier: bigint): boolean {
    return this.tree.nullifiers.has(nullifier);
  }

  getRoot(): bigint {
    return this.tree.root;
  }

  getTree(): MerkleTree {
    return { ...this.tree };
  }

  computeNullifier(leaf: bigint, secret: bigint): bigint {
    return this.poseidonHash([leaf, secret]);
  }
}

// ============================================================
// SCHNORR PROOF SYSTEM
// ============================================================

export class SchnorrProtocol {
  private readonly field: FieldArithmetic;

  constructor(field: FieldArithmetic) {
    this.field = field;
  }

  prove(secret: bigint, publicKey: bigint, message?: string): ZKProof {
    const r = this.field.random();
    const commitment = this.field.pow(this.getGenerator(), r);
    const challenge = this.computeChallenge(publicKey, commitment, message);
    const response = this.field.sub(r, this.field.mul(secret, challenge));

    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      type: ZKProofType.SCHNORR,
      circuitType: ZKCircuitType.SIGNATURE_VERIFY,
      statementId: crypto.randomUUID(),
      commitment: commitment.toString(16),
      challenge: challenge.toString(16),
      response: response.toString(16),
      createdAt: new Date(),
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 3600000),
      status: ZKStatus.PROVED,
      proofData: {
        pi_a: [commitment, r, 0n],
        pi_b: [[challenge, 0n], [response, 0n], [0n, 0n]],
        pi_c: [response, challenge, 0n],
        protocol: 'schnorr',
        curve: 'BN128',
      },
      publicSignals: [publicKey, commitment, challenge],
      metadata: { message },
    };
  }

  verify(proof: ZKProof, publicKey: bigint, message?: string): boolean {
    try {
      const commitment = BigInt('0x' + proof.commitment);
      const challenge = BigInt('0x' + proof.challenge);
      const response = BigInt('0x' + proof.response);
      const G = this.getGenerator();

      const expectedChallenge = this.computeChallenge(publicKey, commitment, message);
      if (challenge !== expectedChallenge) return false;

      const lhs = this.field.pow(G, response);
      const rhs_pk = this.field.pow(publicKey, challenge);
      const rhs = this.field.mul(commitment, this.field.inv(rhs_pk));

      return lhs === rhs;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  private computeChallenge(publicKey: bigint, commitment: bigint, message?: string): bigint {
    const data = `${publicKey.toString(16)}:${commitment.toString(16)}:${message || ''}`;
    return this.field.hashToField(Buffer.from(data));
  }

  private getGenerator(): bigint {
    return this.field.hashToField(Buffer.from('UBE_SCHNORR_GENERATOR'));
  }
}

// ============================================================
// RANGE PROOF SYSTEM (Bulletproof-style)
// ============================================================

export class RangeProofSystem {
  private readonly field: FieldArithmetic;
  private readonly pedersen: PedersenScheme;

  constructor(field: FieldArithmetic) {
    this.field = field;
    this.pedersen = new PedersenScheme(field);
  }

  prove(value: bigint, min: bigint, max: bigint): RangeProof {
    if (value < min || value > max) {
      throw new Error(`Value ${value} out of range [${min}, ${max}]`);
    }

    const bits = this.computeBits(max);
    const shifted = value - min;
    const commitment = this.pedersen.commit(value);

    const bitCommitments: bigint[] = [];
    let reconstructed = 0n;
    for (let i = 0n; i < BigInt(bits); i++) {
      const bit = (shifted >> i) & 1n;
      const bc = this.pedersen.commit(bit);
      bitCommitments.push(bc.commitment);
      reconstructed += bit * (2n ** i);
    }

    const proof: ZKProof = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      type: ZKProofType.RANGE_PROOF,
      circuitType: ZKCircuitType.RANGE_CHECK,
      statementId: crypto.randomUUID(),
      commitment: commitment.commitment.toString(16),
      challenge: this.field.random().toString(16),
      response: this.field.random().toString(16),
      createdAt: new Date(),
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 86400000),
      status: ZKStatus.PROVED,
      proofData: {
        pi_a: [commitment.commitment, min, max],
        pi_b: [[shifted, BigInt(bits)], bitCommitments.slice(0, 2) as [bigint, bigint], [0n, 0n]],
        pi_c: [reconstructed, value, commitment.blindingFactor],
        protocol: 'bulletproof',
        curve: 'BN128',
      },
      publicSignals: [commitment.commitment, min, max, BigInt(bits)],
    };

    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      commitment: commitment.commitment,
      proof,
      min,
      max,
      bits,
      verified: true,
    };
  }

  verify(rangeProof: RangeProof): boolean {
    try {
      const { pi_a, pi_b } = rangeProof.proof.proofData;
      const [commitment, min, max] = pi_a;
      const [[shifted, bits]] = pi_b;

      if (min !== rangeProof.min || max !== rangeProof.max) return false;
      if (commitment !== rangeProof.commitment) return false;

      const maxShifted = max - min;
      if (shifted < 0n || shifted > maxShifted) return false;

      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  private computeBits(max: bigint): number {
    let bits = 0;
    let n = max;
    while (n > 0n) { bits++; n >>= 1n; }
    return Math.max(bits, 1);
  }
}

// ZK PROOF CIRCUIT LIBRARY

export class ZKCircuitLibrary {
  private readonly circuits: Map<string, ZKCircuit> = new Map();
  private readonly field: FieldArithmetic;

  constructor(field: FieldArithmetic) {
    this.field = field;
    this.loadStandardCircuits();
  }

  private loadStandardCircuits(): void {
    const circuits: ZKCircuit[] = [
      {
        id: 'age-verify',
        type: ZKCircuitType.AGE_VERIFICATION,
        name: 'Age Verification',
        description: 'Proves age >= threshold without revealing exact age',
        constraints: [
          { id: 'c1', type: 'range', left: { type: 'variable', variable: 'age' }, right: { type: 'constant', constant: 18n } },
        ],
        publicInputCount: 1,
        privateInputCount: 2,
        gateCount: 256,
        depth: 10,
        version: '1.0.0',
      },
      {
        id: 'balance-check',
        type: ZKCircuitType.BALANCE_SUFFICIENCY,
        name: 'Balance Sufficiency',
        description: 'Proves balance >= required without revealing balance',
        constraints: [
          { id: 'c1', type: 'range', left: { type: 'variable', variable: 'balance' }, right: { type: 'variable', variable: 'required' } },
        ],
        publicInputCount: 1,
        privateInputCount: 3,
        gateCount: 512,
        depth: 15,
        version: '1.0.0',
      },
      {
        id: 'identity-bind',
        type: ZKCircuitType.IDENTITY_BINDING,
        name: 'Identity Binding',
        description: 'Proves ownership of identity credential without revealing it',
        constraints: [
          { id: 'c1', type: 'hash', left: { type: 'variable', variable: 'credential' } },
          { id: 'c2', type: 'equality', left: { type: 'variable', variable: 'commitment' }, right: { type: 'hash', hashAlgorithm: 'poseidon', operands: [] } },
        ],
        publicInputCount: 2,
        privateInputCount: 4,
        gateCount: 1024,
        depth: 20,
        version: '1.0.0',
      },
      {
        id: 'hash-preimage',
        type: ZKCircuitType.HASH_PREIMAGE,
        name: 'Hash Preimage',
        description: 'Proves knowledge of hash preimage without revealing it',
        constraints: [
          { id: 'c1', type: 'hash', left: { type: 'variable', variable: 'preimage' } },
        ],
        publicInputCount: 1,
        privateInputCount: 1,
        gateCount: 512,
        depth: 15,
        version: '1.0.0',
      },
      {
        id: 'merkle-inclusion',
        type: ZKCircuitType.MERKLE_INCLUSION,
        name: 'Merkle Inclusion',
        description: 'Proves membership in a Merkle tree without revealing the leaf',
        constraints: [
          { id: 'c1', type: 'membership', left: { type: 'variable', variable: 'leaf' } },
        ],
        publicInputCount: 2,
        privateInputCount: 20,
        gateCount: 2048,
        depth: 30,
        version: '1.0.0',
      },
      {
        id: 'compliance-check',
        type: ZKCircuitType.COMPLIANCE_CHECK,
        name: 'Regulatory Compliance',
        description: 'Proves compliance with regulatory requirements without revealing data',
        constraints: [
          { id: 'c1', type: 'range', left: { type: 'variable', variable: 'riskScore' }, right: { type: 'constant', constant: 100n } },
          { id: 'c2', type: 'membership', left: { type: 'variable', variable: 'jurisdiction' } },
        ],
        publicInputCount: 3,
        privateInputCount: 10,
        gateCount: 4096,
        depth: 40,
        version: '1.0.0',
      },
    ];

    for (const circuit of circuits) {
      this.circuits.set(circuit.id, circuit);
    }
  }

  get(id: string): ZKCircuit | undefined {
    return this.circuits.get(id);
  }

  getByType(type: ZKCircuitType): ZKCircuit[] {
    return Array.from(this.circuits.values()).filter(c => c.type === type);
  }

  register(circuit: ZKCircuit): void {
    this.circuits.set(circuit.id, circuit);
  }

  list(): ZKCircuit[] {
    return Array.from(this.circuits.values());
  }
}

// ============================================================
// SIGMA PROTOCOL (Generalized)
// ============================================================

export class SigmaProtocol {
  private readonly field: FieldArithmetic;

  constructor(field: FieldArithmetic) {
    this.field = field;
  }

  proveKnowledge(secret: bigint, publicValue: bigint): ZKProof {
    const r = this.field.random();
    const G = this.field.hashToField(Buffer.from('UBE_SIGMA_GENERATOR'));
    const commitment = this.field.pow(G, r);
    const challenge = this.field.hashToField(
      Buffer.from(`${publicValue.toString(16)}:${commitment.toString(16)}`)
    );
    const response = this.field.add(r, this.field.mul(secret, challenge));

    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      type: ZKProofType.SIGMA_PROTOCOL,
      circuitType: ZKCircuitType.COMPUTATION_INTEGRITY,
      statementId: crypto.randomUUID(),
      commitment: commitment.toString(16),
      challenge: challenge.toString(16),
      response: response.toString(16),
      createdAt: new Date(),
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 7200000),
      status: ZKStatus.PROVED,
      proofData: {
        pi_a: [commitment, publicValue, G],
        pi_b: [[challenge, 0n], [response, 0n], [0n, 0n]],
        pi_c: [response, challenge, G],
        protocol: 'sigma',
        curve: 'BN128',
      },
      publicSignals: [publicValue, commitment],
    };
  }

  verifyKnowledge(proof: ZKProof, publicValue: bigint): boolean {
    try {
      const G = this.field.hashToField(Buffer.from('UBE_SIGMA_GENERATOR'));
      const commitment = BigInt('0x' + proof.commitment);
      const challenge = BigInt('0x' + proof.challenge);
      const response = BigInt('0x' + proof.response);

      const expectedChallenge = this.field.hashToField(
        Buffer.from(`${publicValue.toString(16)}:${commitment.toString(16)}`)
      );
      if (challenge !== expectedChallenge) return false;

      const lhs = this.field.pow(G, response);
      const rhs = this.field.mul(commitment, this.field.pow(publicValue, challenge));
      return lhs === rhs;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  proveEquality(secret: bigint, pub1: bigint, pub2: bigint): ZKProof {
    const G1 = this.field.hashToField(Buffer.from('UBE_SIGMA_G1'));
    const G2 = this.field.hashToField(Buffer.from('UBE_SIGMA_G2'));
    const r = this.field.random();
    const t1 = this.field.pow(G1, r);
    const t2 = this.field.pow(G2, r);
    const challenge = this.field.hashToField(
      Buffer.from(`${pub1.toString(16)}:${pub2.toString(16)}:${t1.toString(16)}:${t2.toString(16)}`)
    );
    const response = this.field.add(r, this.field.mul(secret, challenge));

    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      type: ZKProofType.EQUALITY_PROOF,
      circuitType: ZKCircuitType.ARITHMETIC,
      statementId: crypto.randomUUID(),
      commitment: t1.toString(16),
      challenge: challenge.toString(16),
      response: response.toString(16),
      auxiliaryData: { t2: t2.toString(16) },
      createdAt: new Date(),
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 3600000),
      status: ZKStatus.PROVED,
      proofData: {
        pi_a: [t1, t2, 0n],
        pi_b: [[challenge, 0n], [response, 0n], [0n, 0n]],
        pi_c: [pub1, pub2, 0n],
        protocol: 'sigma_equality',
        curve: 'BN128',
      },
      publicSignals: [pub1, pub2],
    };
  }
}

// ============================================================
// ZK PROOF SESSION MANAGER
// ============================================================

export class ZKSessionManager {
  private readonly sessions: Map<string, ZKSession> = new Map();
  private readonly defaultExpiry: number;

  constructor(defaultExpiryMs: number = 3600000) {
    this.defaultExpiry = defaultExpiryMs;
  }

  create(participantId: string, circuitId: string): ZKSession {
    const session: ZKSession = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      participantId,
      circuitId,
      status: ZKStatus.PENDING,
      createdAt: new Date(),
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + this.defaultExpiry),
      attempts: 0,
      maxAttempts: 3,
      nonce: BigInt('0x' + crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex')),
    };
    this.sessions.set(session.id, session);
    return session;
  }

  get(id: string): ZKSession | undefined {
    const session = this.sessions.get(id);
    if (!session) return undefined;
    if (session.expiresAt < new Date()) {
      session.status = ZKStatus.EXPIRED;
    }
    return session;
  }

  update(id: string, updates: Partial<ZKSession>): boolean {
    const session = this.sessions.get(id);
    if (!session) return false;
    Object.assign(session, updates);
    return true;
  }

  attachProof(sessionId: string, proof: ZKProof): boolean {
    const session = this.sessions.get(sessionId);
    if (!session || session.status === ZKStatus.EXPIRED) return false;
    session.proof = proof;
    session.status = ZKStatus.PROVED;
    return true;
  }

  invalidate(sessionId: string): boolean {
    const session = this.sessions.get(sessionId);
    if (!session) return false;
    session.status = ZKStatus.REVOKED;
    return true;
  }

  cleanup(): number {
    const now = new Date();
    let cleared = 0;
    for (const [id, session] of this.sessions.entries()) {
      if (session.expiresAt < now || session.status === ZKStatus.REVOKED) {
        this.sessions.delete(id);
        cleared++;
      }
    }
    return cleared;
  }

  getActive(participantId: string): ZKSession[] {
    return Array.from(this.sessions.values()).filter(
      s => s.participantId === participantId && s.status !== ZKStatus.EXPIRED && s.status !== ZKStatus.REVOKED
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// MAIN ZERO KNOWLEDGE ENGINE
// ============================================================

export class ZeroKnowledgeEngine extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly field: FieldArithmetic;
  private readonly pedersen: PedersenScheme;
  private readonly schnorr: SchnorrProtocol;
  private readonly rangeProofs: RangeProofSystem;
  private readonly sigma: SigmaProtocol;
  private readonly merkle: ZKMerkleTree;
  private readonly circuits: ZKCircuitLibrary;
  private readonly sessions: ZKSessionManager;
  private readonly proofs: Map<string, ZKProof> = new Map();
  private readonly config: ZKEngineConfig;
  private readonly nullifiers: NullifierSet;

  constructor(config: ZKEngineConfig = {}) {
    super();
    this.config = {
      defaultFieldSize: FieldSize.BN128,
      defaultExpiry: 3600000,
      enableNullifiers: true,
      enableMerkle: true,
      enableBatching: false,
      cacheVerificationKeys: true,
      simulationMode: true,
      ...config,
    };
    this.field = new FieldArithmetic(this.config.defaultFieldSize);
    this.pedersen = new PedersenScheme(this.field);
    this.schnorr = new SchnorrProtocol(this.field);
    this.rangeProofs = new RangeProofSystem(this.field);
    this.sigma = new SigmaProtocol(this.field);
    this.merkle = new ZKMerkleTree(this.field, 20);
    this.circuits = new ZKCircuitLibrary(this.field);
    this.sessions = new ZKSessionManager(this.config.defaultExpiry);
    this.nullifiers = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      nullifiers: new Set(),
      accumulator: 0n,
      createdAt: new Date(),
      maxSize: 1000000,
    };
  }

  // === COMMITMENT ===

  commit(value: bigint, blindingFactor?: bigint): PedersenCommitment {
    const c = this.pedersen.commit(value, blindingFactor);
    this.emit('commitment:created', { id: c.id });
    return c;
  }

  addCommitments(c1: PedersenCommitment, c2: PedersenCommitment): bigint {
    return this.pedersen.addCommitments(c1, c2);
  }

  verifyCommitment(commitment: bigint, value: bigint, blindingFactor: bigint): boolean {
    return this.pedersen.verifyCommitment(commitment, value, blindingFactor);
  }

  // === SCHNORR ===

  proveKnowledge(secret: bigint, message?: string): ZKProof {
    const publicKey = this.field.pow(
      this.field.hashToField(Buffer.from('UBE_SCHNORR_GENERATOR')),
      secret
    );
    const proof = this.schnorr.prove(secret, publicKey, message);
    this.proofs.set(proof.id, proof);
    this.emit('proof:created', { id: proof.id, type: proof.type });
    return proof;
  }

  verifyKnowledge(proof: ZKProof, publicKey: bigint, message?: string): ZKVerificationResult {
    const start = Date.now();
    const valid = this.schnorr.verify(proof, publicKey, message);
    return {
      valid,
      proofId: proof.id,
      verifiedAt: new Date(),
      verificationMs: Date.now() - start,
      circuitType: proof.circuitType,
      publicSignals: proof.publicSignals,
      errorMessage: valid ? undefined : 'Schnorr verification failed',
    };
  }

  // === RANGE PROOFS ===

  proveRange(value: bigint, min: bigint, max: bigint): RangeProof {
    const rp = this.rangeProofs.prove(value, min, max);
    this.proofs.set(rp.proof.id, rp.proof);
    this.emit('range_proof:created', { id: rp.id, min, max });
    return rp;
  }

  verifyRange(rangeProof: RangeProof): ZKVerificationResult {
    const start = Date.now();
    const valid = this.rangeProofs.verify(rangeProof);
    return {
      valid,
      proofId: rangeProof.proof.id,
      verifiedAt: new Date(),
      verificationMs: Date.now() - start,
      circuitType: ZKCircuitType.RANGE_CHECK,
      publicSignals: [rangeProof.min, rangeProof.max],
      errorMessage: valid ? undefined : 'Range proof verification failed',
    };
  }

  // === AGE VERIFICATION (no birthdate revealed) ===

  proveAge(age: bigint, minAge: bigint): ZKProof {
    if (age < minAge) throw new Error('Age does not meet minimum requirement');
    const proof = this.rangeProofs.prove(age, minAge, 150n).proof;
    proof.circuitType = ZKCircuitType.AGE_VERIFICATION;
    this.proofs.set(proof.id, proof);
    this.emit('age_proof:created', { id: proof.id });
    return proof;
  }

  // === BALANCE PROOF ===

  proveBalanceSufficiency(balance: bigint, required: bigint): ZKProof {
    if (balance < required) throw new Error('Insufficient balance');
    const proof = this.rangeProofs.prove(balance, required, balance + 1n).proof;
    proof.circuitType = ZKCircuitType.BALANCE_SUFFICIENCY;
    this.proofs.set(proof.id, proof);
    this.emit('balance_proof:created', { id: proof.id });
    return proof;
  }

  // === MERKLE MEMBERSHIP ===

  insertLeaf(leaf: bigint): { index: number; root: bigint } {
    const index = this.merkle.insert(leaf);
    return { index, root: this.merkle.getRoot() };
  }

  proveMembership(leafIndex: number): MerkleProof {
    return this.merkle.generateProof(leafIndex);
  }

  verifyMembership(proof: MerkleProof): ZKVerificationResult {
    const start = Date.now();
    const valid = this.merkle.verifyProof(proof);
    return {
      valid,
      proofId: proof.id,
      verifiedAt: new Date(),
      verificationMs: Date.now() - start,
      circuitType: ZKCircuitType.MERKLE_INCLUSION,
      publicSignals: [proof.root, BigInt(proof.leafIndex)],
    };
  }

  // === HASH PREIMAGE ===

  proveHashPreimage(preimage: Buffer): ZKProof {
    const preimageField = this.field.fromBytes(crypto.createHash('sha256').update(preimage).digest());
    return this.sigma.proveKnowledge(preimageField, this.field.hashToField(preimage));
  }

  // === SIGMA EQUALITY ===

  proveEquality(secret: bigint, pub1: bigint, pub2: bigint): ZKProof {
    return this.sigma.proveEquality(secret, pub1, pub2);
  }

  // === NULLIFIERS ===

  createNullifier(leaf: bigint, secret: bigint): bigint {
    const nullifier = this.merkle.computeNullifier(leaf, secret);
    if (!this.merkle.addNullifier(nullifier)) {
      throw new Error('Nullifier already spent');
    }
    this.nullifiers.nullifiers.add(nullifier);
    return nullifier;
  }

  isNullified(nullifier: bigint): boolean {
    return this.nullifiers.nullifiers.has(nullifier);
  }

  // === SESSIONS ===

  createSession(participantId: string, circuitId: string): ZKSession {
    return this.sessions.create(participantId, circuitId);
  }

  getSession(id: string): ZKSession | undefined {
    return this.sessions.get(id);
  }

  // === BATCH VERIFICATION ===

  verifyBatch(proofs: ZKProof[], publicKeys: bigint[]): ZKVerificationResult[] {
    return proofs.map((proof, i) => {
      if (proof.type === ZKProofType.SCHNORR) {
        return this.verifyKnowledge(proof, publicKeys[i] ?? 0n);
      }
      return {
        valid: false,
        proofId: proof.id,
        verifiedAt: new Date(),
        verificationMs: 0,
        circuitType: proof.circuitType,
        publicSignals: proof.publicSignals,
        errorMessage: 'Unsupported proof type for batch verification',
      };
    });
  }

  // === FIELD UTILITIES ===

  randomField(): bigint { return this.field.random(); }
  hashToField(data: Buffer | string): bigint { return this.field.hashToField(data); }
  fieldAdd(a: bigint, b: bigint): bigint { return this.field.add(a, b); }
  fieldMul(a: bigint, b: bigint): bigint { return this.field.mul(a, b); }
  fieldInv(a: bigint): bigint { return this.field.inv(a); }
  fieldPow(base: bigint, exp: bigint): bigint { return this.field.pow(base, exp); }

  getProof(id: string): ZKProof | undefined { return this.proofs.get(id); }
  getCircuit(id: string): ZKCircuit | undefined { return this.circuits.get(id); }
  listCircuits(): ZKCircuit[] { return this.circuits.list(); }
  getMerkleRoot(): bigint { return this.merkle.getRoot(); }
  getField(): FieldArithmetic { return this.field; }
}

// ============================================================
// FACTORY
// ============================================================

export function createZKEngine(config?: ZKEngineConfig): ZeroKnowledgeEngine {
  return new ZeroKnowledgeEngine(config);
}

export const defaultZKEngine = createZKEngine();
export default ZeroKnowledgeEngine;

// ============================================================
// IDENTIFIER MANGLER
// ============================================================

export class IdentifierMangler {
  private counter: number = 0;
  private readonly map: Map<string, string> = new Map();
  private readonly reserved: Set<string>;
  private readonly chars = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ';
  private readonly startChars = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ_$';

  constructor(preservedNames: string[] = []) {
    this.reserved = new Set([
      'break', 'case', 'catch', 'class', 'const', 'continue', 'debugger',
      'default', 'delete', 'do', 'else', 'export', 'extends', 'false',
      'finally', 'for', 'function', 'if', 'import', 'in', 'instanceof',
      'let', 'new', 'null', 'return', 'static', 'super', 'switch', 'this',
      'throw', 'true', 'try', 'typeof', 'undefined', 'var', 'void', 'while',
      'with', 'yield', 'async', 'await', 'of', 'from', 'as', 'interface',
      'type', 'enum', 'implements', 'private', 'protected', 'public',
      'abstract', 'readonly', 'declare', 'namespace', 'module',
      ...preservedNames,
    ]);
  }

  mangle(name: string): string {
    if (this.reserved.has(name)) return name;
    if (this.map.has(name)) return this.map.get(name)!;
    const mangled = this.generateName();
    this.map.set(name, mangled);
    return mangled;
  }

  private generateName(): string {
    let n = this.counter++;
    let result = this.startChars[n % this.startChars.length]!;
    n = Math.floor(n / this.startChars.length);
    while (n > 0) {
      result += this.chars[(n - 1) % this.chars.length];
      n = Math.floor((n - 1) / this.chars.length);
    }
    if (this.reserved.has(result)) return this.generateName();
    return result;
  }

  getMap(): Map<string, string> {
    return new Map(this.map);
  }

  reset(): void {
    this.counter = 0;
    this.map.clear();
  }
}

// ============================================================
// JAVASCRIPT / TYPESCRIPT MINIFIER
// ============================================================

export class JSMinifier {
  private readonly mangler: IdentifierMangler;

  constructor(preservedNames: string[] = []) {
    this.mangler = new IdentifierMangler(preservedNames);
  }

  minify(code: string, options: MinificationOptions): string {
    let result = code;
    let pass = 0;
    const maxPasses = 3;

    while (pass < maxPasses) {
      const prev = result;
      if (options.dropConsole) result = this.dropConsole(result);
      if (options.dropDebugger) result = this.dropDebugger(result);
      if (options.removeComments !== false) result = this.removeComments(result);
      if (options.inlineConstants) result = this.inlineConstants(result);
      if (options.defineReplacements) result = this.applyDefines(result, options.defineReplacements);
      if (options.removeWhitespace !== false) result = this.collapseWhitespace(result);
      if (options.shortenIdentifiers && options.obfuscationLevel && options.obfuscationLevel >= ObfuscationLevel.LIGHT) {
        result = this.mangleIdentifiers(result, options.preservedNames || []);
      }
      if (result === prev) break;
      pass++;
    }

    if (options.bannerComment) {
      result = `/* ${options.bannerComment} */\n` + result;
    }

    return result;
  }

  private removeComments(code: string): string {
    let result = code;
    result = result.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, '');
    result = result.replace(/(?<!:)\/\/[^\n]*/g, '');
    return result;
  }

  private dropConsole(code: string): string {
    return code.replace(/console\s*\.\s*\w+\s*\([^)]*\)\s*;?/g, '');
  }

  private dropDebugger(code: string): string {
    return code.replace(/\bdebugger\s*;?/g, '');
  }

  private collapseWhitespace(code: string): string {
    let result = code;
    result = result.replace(/\s*\n\s*/g, '\n');
    result = result.replace(/[ \t]+/g, ' ');
    result = result.replace(/\s*([{};,=+\-*/<>!&|^~?:()])\s*/g, '$1');
    result = result.replace(/\n+/g, '\n');
    result = result.trim();
    return result;
  }

  private inlineConstants(code: string): string {
    const constPattern = /const\s+(\w+)\s*=\s*([^;{]+);/g;
    const constants = new Map<string, string>();
    let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
    while ((match = constPattern.exec(code)) !== null) {
      const name = match[1]!;
      const value = match[2]!.trim();
      if (/^(['"`].*['"`]|\d+|true|false|null|undefined)$/.test(value)) {
        constants.set(name, value);
      }
    }
    let result = code;
    for (const [name, value] of constants.entries()) {
      const usagePattern = new RegExp(`\\b${name}\\b`, 'g');
      let count = 0;
      result.replace(usagePattern, () => { count++; return ''; });
      if (count <= 3) {
        result = result.replace(usagePattern, value);
        result = result.replace(new RegExp(`const\\s+${name}\\s*=\\s*[^;]+;`, 'g'), '');
      }
    }
    return result;
  }

  private applyDefines(code: string, defines: Record<string, string>): string {
    let result = code;
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(defines)) {
      result = result.replace(new RegExp(`\\b${key}\\b`, 'g'), value);
    }
    return result;
  }

  private mangleIdentifiers(code: string, preserved: string[]): string {
    const mangler = new IdentifierMangler(preserved);
    return code.replace(/\b([a-zA-Z_$][a-zA-Z0-9_$]{4,})\b/g, (match) => {
      if (/^(function|const|let|var|class|import|export|return|if|else|for|while|switch|case|break|continue|new|this|super|typeof|instanceof|void|delete|throw|try|catch|finally|async|await|yield|of|from)$/.test(match)) {
        return match;
      }
      return mangler.mangle(match);
    });
  }
}

// ============================================================
// CSS MINIFIER
// ============================================================

export class CSSMinifier {
  minify(css: string): string {
    let result = css;
    result = result.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, '');
    result = result.replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
    result = result.replace(/\s*([{};:,>~+])\s*/g, '$1');
    result = result.replace(/;\}/g, '}');
    result = result.replace(/0+\.(\d+)/g, '.$1');
    result = result.replace(/(\d+)\.0+(%|em|rem|px|vh|vw)/g, '$1$2');
    result = result.replace(/#([0-9a-f])\1([0-9a-f])\2([0-9a-f])\3/gi, '#$1$2$3');
    result = result.replace(/\s*!important/g, '!important');
    result = result.replace(/\n/g, '');
    return result.trim();
  }

  extractCritical(css: string, usedSelectors: string[]): string {
    const rules = css.match(/[^{}]+\{[^{}]+\}/g) || [];
    return rules.filter(rule => {
      const selector = rule.match(/^([^{]+)/)?.[1]?.trim() || '';
      return usedSelectors.some(s => selector.includes(s));
    }).join('');
  }
}

// ============================================================
// HTML MINIFIER
// ============================================================

export class HTMLMinifier {
  minify(html: string, options: { removeComments?: boolean; collapseWhitespace?: boolean } = {}): string {
    let result = html;
    if (options.removeComments !== false) {
      result = result.replace(/<!--(?!\[if)[\s\S]*?-->/g, '');
    }
    if (options.collapseWhitespace !== false) {
      result = result.replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
      result = result.replace(/>\s+</g, '><');
      result = result.replace(/\s+(\/?>)/g, '$1');
      result = result.replace(/(<\w[^>]*)\s+>/g, '$1>');
    }
    result = result.replace(/(\s+)(\w+=)"/g, ' $2"');
    result = result.replace(/ type="text\/(css|javascript)"/g, '');
    return result.trim();
  }
}

// ============================================================
// JSON MINIFIER
// ============================================================

export class JSONMinifier {
  minify(json: string): string {
    try {
      return JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(json));
    } catch {
      return json.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/:\s+/g, ':').replace(/,\s+/g, ',').trim();
    }
  }

  sortKeys(json: string): string {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(json);
      return JSON.stringify(this.sortObject(parsed));
    } catch {
      return json;
    }
  }

  private sortObject(obj: unknown): unknown {
    if (Array.isArray(obj)) return obj.map(v => this.sortObject(v));
    if (obj !== null && typeof obj === 'object') {
      return Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(obj as Record<string, unknown>)
          .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
          .map(([k, v]) => [k, this.sortObject(v)])
      );
    }
    return obj;
  }
}

// ============================================================
// SQL MINIFIER
// ============================================================

export class SQLMinifier {
  minify(sql: string): string {
    let result = sql;
    result = result.replace(/--[^\n]*/g, '');
    result = result.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, '');
    result = result.replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
    result = result.replace(/\s*([,;()=<>!+\-*\/])\s*/g, '$1');
    result = result.replace(/\bSELECT\b/gi, 'SELECT');
    result = result.replace(/\bFROM\b/gi, ' FROM ');
    result = result.replace(/\bWHERE\b/gi, ' WHERE ');
    result = result.replace(/\bAND\b/gi, ' AND ');
    result = result.replace(/\bOR\b/gi, ' OR ');
    result = result.replace(/\bJOIN\b/gi, ' JOIN ');
    return result.trim();
  }
}

// ============================================================
// SECURITY SANITIZER
// ============================================================

export class SecuritySanitizer {
  private readonly secretPatterns: RegExp[] = [
    /api[_-]?key\s*[=:]\s*['"]?([a-zA-Z0-9_\-]{20,})['"]?/gi,
    /secret\s*[=:]\s*['"]?([a-zA-Z0-9_\-]{20,})['"]?/gi,
    /password\s*[=:]\s*['"]?([^\s'"]+)['"]?/gi,
    /token\s*[=:]\s*['"]?([a-zA-Z0-9_\-]{20,})['"]?/gi,
    /private[_-]?key\s*[=:]\s*['"]?([a-zA-Z0-9+/=]{40,})['"]?/gi,
    /-----BEGIN\s+(?:RSA\s+)?PRIVATE\s+KEY-----[\s\S]*?-----END\s+(?:RSA\s+)?PRIVATE\s+KEY-----/gi,
    /[A-Za-z0-9+/]{40,}={0,2}/g,
  ];

  private readonly xssPatterns: RegExp[] = [
    /<script\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi,
    /javascript\s*:/gi,
    /on\w+\s*=\s*(['"])[^'"]*\1/gi,
    /data\s*:\s*text\/html/gi,
    /<iframe\b[^>]*>/gi,
    /document\.cookie/gi,
    /window\.location/gi,
  ];

  private readonly injectionPatterns: RegExp[] = [
    /['";]\s*(?:DROP|DELETE|INSERT|UPDATE|CREATE|ALTER|EXEC)\b/gi,
    /UNION\s+(?:ALL\s+)?SELECT/gi,
    /\b(?:xp_cmdshell|sp_executesql)\b/gi,
    /\$\{[^}]*\}/g,
    /`[^`]*`/g,
  ];

  sanitize(code: string, target: MinificationTarget): SecuritySanitizationResult {
    let sanitized = code;
    const removedPatterns: string[] = [];
    let xssCleaned = 0;
    let secretsRedacted = 0;
    let injectionsPrevented = 0;
    const warnings: string[] = [];

    for (const pattern of this.secretPatterns) {
      const matches = sanitized.match(pattern);
      if (matches) {
        sanitized = sanitized.replace(pattern, (match) => {
          secretsRedacted++;
          return match.replace(/(['"])[^'"]{10,}(['"])/g, '"[REDACTED]"');
        });
        removedPatterns.push(`Secret pattern: ${pattern.source.substring(0, 30)}`);
      }
    }

    if ([MinificationTarget.HTML, MinificationTarget.JAVASCRIPT, MinificationTarget.TYPESCRIPT].includes(target)) {
      for (const pattern of this.xssPatterns) {
        const matches = sanitized.match(pattern);
        if (matches) {
          xssCleaned += matches.length;
          warnings.push(`XSS pattern detected: ${pattern.source.substring(0, 30)}`);
        }
      }
    }

    if (target === MinificationTarget.SQL) {
      for (const pattern of this.injectionPatterns) {
        const matches = sanitized.match(pattern);
        if (matches) {
          injectionsPrevented += matches.length;
          warnings.push(`SQL injection pattern: ${pattern.source.substring(0, 30)}`);
        }
      }
    }

    return { sanitized, removedPatterns, injectionsPrevented, xssCleaned, secretsRedacted, warnings };
  }
}

// ============================================================
// BUNDLE ANALYZER
// ============================================================

export class BundleAnalyzer {
  analyze(code: string, target: MinificationTarget): BundleAnalysis {
    const lines = code.split('\n');
    const totalSize = Buffer.byteLength(code, 'utf8');
    const gzippedSize = Math.round(totalSize * 0.3);

    const modules: ModuleInfo[] = [];
    const importPattern = /import\s+.*?from\s+['"]([^'"]+)['"]/g;
    let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
    const importedModules = new Set<string>();

    while ((match = importPattern.exec(code)) !== null) {
      importedModules.add(match[1]!);
    }

    for (const mod of importedModules) {
      modules.push({
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        path: mod,
        size: 0,
        gzippedSize: 0,
        imports: [],
        exports: [],
        isUsed: true,
        isExternal: !mod.startsWith('.'),
      });
    }

    const duplicates = this.findDuplicates(lines);
    const unusedExports = this.findUnusedExports(code);

    return {
      totalSize,
      gzippedSize,
      chunks: [{
        id: 'main',
        name: 'main',
        size: totalSize,
        modules: Array.from(importedModules),
        isEntry: true,
        isAsync: false,
        hash: crypto.createHash('sha256').update(code).digest('hex').substring(0, 8),
      }],
      modules,
      duplicates,
      unusedExports,
      treeshakingCandidates: unusedExports,
    };
  }

  private findDuplicates(lines: string[]): DuplicateInfo[] {
    const counts = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const line of lines) {
      const trimmed = line.trim();
      if (trimmed.length > 20) {
        counts.set(trimmed, (counts.get(trimmed) || 0) + 1);
      }
    }
    const dupes: DuplicateInfo[] = [];
    for (const [content, count] of counts.entries()) {
      if (count > 1) {
        dupes.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), content, occurrences: count, totalBytes: content.length * count, locations: [] });
      }
    }
    return dupes.slice(0, 20);
  }

  private findUnusedExports(code: string): string[] {
    const exported: string[] = [];
    const exportPattern = /export\s+(?:const|let|var|function|class)\s+(\w+)/g;
    let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
    while ((match = exportPattern.exec(code)) !== null) {
      exported.push(match[1]!);
    }
    return exported.filter(name => {
      const usageCount = (code.match(new RegExp(`\\b${name}\\b`, 'g')) || []).length;
      return usageCount <= 1;
    });
  }
}

// ============================================================
// MAIN MINIFIER ENGINE
// ============================================================

export class MinifierEngine extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly jsMinifier: JSMinifier;
  private readonly cssMinifier: CSSMinifier;
  private readonly htmlMinifier: HTMLMinifier;
  private readonly jsonMinifier: JSONMinifier;
  private readonly sqlMinifier: SQLMinifier;
  private readonly sanitizer: SecuritySanitizer;
  private readonly analyzer: BundleAnalyzer;
  private readonly cache: Map<string, MinificationResult> = new Map();
  private readonly config: MinifierConfig;
  private totalProcessed: number = 0;
  private totalBytesSaved: number = 0;

  constructor(config: MinifierConfig = {}) {
    super();
    this.config = {
      enableCaching: true,
      cacheSize: 500,
      enableParallel: false,
      enableProfiling: false,
      securityMode: true,
      ...config,
    };
    const preserved = config.defaultOptions?.preservedNames || [];
    this.jsMinifier = new JSMinifier(preserved);
    this.cssMinifier = new CSSMinifier();
    this.htmlMinifier = new HTMLMinifier();
    this.jsonMinifier = new JSONMinifier();
    this.sqlMinifier = new SQLMinifier();
    this.sanitizer = new SecuritySanitizer();
    this.analyzer = new BundleAnalyzer();
  }

  minify(code: string, options: MinificationOptions): MinificationResult {
    const start = Date.now();
    const cacheKey = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(code + JSON.stringify(options)).digest('hex');

    if (this.config.enableCaching) {
      const cached = this.cache.get(cacheKey);
      if (cached) return cached;
    }

    let minified = code;
    const stats: MinificationStats = {
      commentsRemoved: 0,
      whitespaceRemoved: 0,
      identifiersShortened: 0,
      deadCodeRemoved: 0,
      constantsInlined: 0,
      duplicatesRemoved: 0,
      linesOriginal: code.split('\n').length,
      linesMinified: 0,
      processingMs: 0,
      passes: 1,
    };

    if (this.config.securityMode) {
      const sanResult = this.sanitizer.sanitize(code, options.target);
      minified = sanResult.sanitized;
      if (sanResult.warnings.length) this.emit('security:warning', sanResult);
    }

    switch (options.target) {
      case MinificationTarget.JAVASCRIPT:
      case MinificationTarget.TYPESCRIPT:
        minified = this.jsMinifier.minify(minified, options);
        break;
      case MinificationTarget.CSS:
        minified = this.cssMinifier.minify(minified);
        break;
      case MinificationTarget.HTML:
        minified = this.htmlMinifier.minify(minified, { removeComments: options.removeComments, collapseWhitespace: options.removeWhitespace });
        break;
      case MinificationTarget.JSON:
        minified = this.jsonMinifier.minify(minified);
        break;
      case MinificationTarget.SQL:
        minified = this.sqlMinifier.minify(minified);
        break;
      default:
        minified = this.genericMinify(minified);
    }

    const originalSize = Buffer.byteLength(code, 'utf8');
    const minifiedSize = Buffer.byteLength(minified, 'utf8');
    stats.linesMinified = minified.split('\n').length;
    stats.whitespaceRemoved = originalSize - minifiedSize;
    stats.processingMs = Date.now() - start;

    const result: MinificationResult = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      original: code,
      minified,
      originalSize,
      minifiedSize,
      compressionRatio: originalSize > 0 ? minifiedSize / originalSize : 1,
      savingsPercent: originalSize > 0 ? ((originalSize - minifiedSize) / originalSize) * 100 : 0,
      target: options.target,
      options,
      stats,
      processedAt: new Date(),
      checksum: crypto.createHash('sha256').update(minified).digest('hex'),
    };

    if (this.config.enableCaching) {
      if (this.cache.size >= (this.config.cacheSize || 500)) {
        const firstKey = this.cache.keys().next().value;
        if (firstKey) this.cache.delete(firstKey);
      }
      this.cache.set(cacheKey, result);
    }

    this.totalProcessed++;
    this.totalBytesSaved += originalSize - minifiedSize;
    this.emit('minified', { id: result.id, target: options.target, savings: result.savingsPercent.toFixed(1) + '%' });
    return result;
  }

  minifyBatch(items: Array<{ code: string; options: MinificationOptions }>): MinificationResult[] {
    return items.map(item => this.minify(item.code, item.options));
  }

  analyze(code: string, target: MinificationTarget): BundleAnalysis {
    return this.analyzer.analyze(code, target);
  }

  sanitize(code: string, target: MinificationTarget): SecuritySanitizationResult {
    return this.sanitizer.sanitize(code, target);
  }

  private genericMinify(code: string): string {
    return code
      .replace(/\s*\n\s*/g, '\n')
      .replace(/[ \t]+/g, ' ')
      .replace(/^\s+|\s+$/gm, '')
      .trim();
  }

  getStats() {
    return {
      totalProcessed: this.totalProcessed,
      totalBytesSaved: this.totalBytesSaved,
      cacheSize: this.cache.size,
      averageSavings: this.totalProcessed > 0 ? this.totalBytesSaved / this.totalProcessed : 0,
    };
  }

  clearCache(): void {
    this.cache.clear();
    this.emit('cache:cleared');
  }
}

// ============================================================
// FACTORY
// ============================================================

export function createMinifier(config?: MinifierConfig): MinifierEngine {
  return new MinifierEngine(config);
}

export const defaultMinifier = createMinifier();
export default MinifierEngine;

// STATIC ANALYZER

export class StaticAnalyzer {
  private readonly dangerousJSPatterns: Array<{ pattern: RegExp; type: SandboxViolation; severity: ThreatLevel; desc: string }> = [
    { pattern: /require\s*\(\s*['"]child_process['"]\s*\)/g, type: SandboxViolation.PROCESS_SPAWN, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'child_process module import detected' },
    { pattern: /require\s*\(\s*['"]fs['"]\s*\)/g, type: SandboxViolation.FILE_SYSTEM_ESCAPE, severity: ThreatLevel.MEDIUM, desc: 'fs module import detected' },
    { pattern: /require\s*\(\s*['"]net['"]\s*\)/g, type: SandboxViolation.NETWORK_ACCESS, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'net module import detected' },
    { pattern: /process\.env/g, type: SandboxViolation.ENV_ACCESS, severity: ThreatLevel.MEDIUM, desc: 'Environment variable access detected' },
    { pattern: /exec\s*\(/g, type: SandboxViolation.PROCESS_SPAWN, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'exec() call detected' },
    { pattern: /eval\s*\(/g, type: SandboxViolation.INJECTION_ATTEMPT, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'eval() call detected — dynamic code execution risk' },
    { pattern: /Function\s*\(/g, type: SandboxViolation.INJECTION_ATTEMPT, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'Function() constructor detected' },
    { pattern: /\.\.__\//g, type: SandboxViolation.PATH_TRAVERSAL, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'Path traversal pattern detected' },
    { pattern: /while\s*\(\s*true\s*\)/g, type: SandboxViolation.INFINITE_LOOP, severity: ThreatLevel.MEDIUM, desc: 'while(true) loop detected — potential infinite loop' },
    { pattern: /setInterval\s*\([^,]+,\s*0\s*\)/g, type: SandboxViolation.INFINITE_LOOP, severity: ThreatLevel.MEDIUM, desc: 'setInterval with 0ms delay detected' },
    { pattern: /crypto\.subtle|WebCrypto|mine|hash.*nonce/gi, type: SandboxViolation.CRYPTO_MINING, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'Potential crypto mining pattern detected' },
    { pattern: /Buffer\.alloc\s*\(\s*\d{8,}/g, type: SandboxViolation.MEMORY_EXCEEDED, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'Large buffer allocation detected' },
    { pattern: /\bfork\s*\(\)/g, type: SandboxViolation.FORK_BOMB, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'Fork call detected — potential fork bomb' },
    { pattern: /global\s*\.\s*\w+\s*=/g, type: SandboxViolation.PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION, severity: ThreatLevel.MEDIUM, desc: 'Global object mutation detected' },
    { pattern: /__proto__\s*=/g, type: SandboxViolation.PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'Prototype pollution attempt detected' },
    { pattern: /constructor\s*\[\s*['"]prototype['"]\s*\]/g, type: SandboxViolation.PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'Prototype access via constructor detected' },
    { pattern: /require\s*\(\s*['"]cluster['"]\s*\)/g, type: SandboxViolation.FORK_BOMB, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'cluster module import — fork risk' },
    { pattern: /XMLHttpRequest|fetch\s*\(/g, type: SandboxViolation.NETWORK_ACCESS, severity: ThreatLevel.MEDIUM, desc: 'Network request detected' },
    { pattern: /\bsocket\b.*\bconnect\b/gi, type: SandboxViolation.NETWORK_ACCESS, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'Socket connection attempt detected' },
  ];

  private readonly dangerousPythonPatterns: Array<{ pattern: RegExp; type: SandboxViolation; severity: ThreatLevel; desc: string }> = [
    { pattern: /import\s+os/g, type: SandboxViolation.SYSCALL_BLOCKED, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'os module import' },
    { pattern: /import\s+subprocess/g, type: SandboxViolation.PROCESS_SPAWN, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'subprocess module import' },
    { pattern: /import\s+socket/g, type: SandboxViolation.NETWORK_ACCESS, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'socket module import' },
    { pattern: /__import__\s*\(/g, type: SandboxViolation.INJECTION_ATTEMPT, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: '__import__ dynamic import detected' },
    { pattern: /exec\s*\(/g, type: SandboxViolation.INJECTION_ATTEMPT, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'exec() call detected' },
    { pattern: /eval\s*\(/g, type: SandboxViolation.INJECTION_ATTEMPT, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'eval() call detected' },
    { pattern: /open\s*\([^)]*['"]\s*[wa]/g, type: SandboxViolation.FILE_SYSTEM_ESCAPE, severity: ThreatLevel.MEDIUM, desc: 'File write operation detected' },
    { pattern: /\.\.\//g, type: SandboxViolation.PATH_TRAVERSAL, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'Path traversal detected' },
    { pattern: /ctypes|cffi/g, type: SandboxViolation.SYSCALL_BLOCKED, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'Native FFI library import detected' },
    { pattern: /while\s+True:/g, type: SandboxViolation.INFINITE_LOOP, severity: ThreatLevel.MEDIUM, desc: 'while True loop detected' },
    { pattern: /pickle\.loads/g, type: SandboxViolation.INJECTION_ATTEMPT, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'pickle.loads — arbitrary code execution risk' },
    { pattern: /marshal\.loads/g, type: SandboxViolation.INJECTION_ATTEMPT, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'marshal.loads — code injection risk' },
    { pattern: /import\s+signal/g, type: SandboxViolation.SYSCALL_BLOCKED, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'signal module import' },
  ];

  private readonly dangerousShellPatterns: Array<{ pattern: RegExp; type: SandboxViolation; severity: ThreatLevel; desc: string }> = [
    { pattern: /rm\s+-rf\s+\//g, type: SandboxViolation.FILE_SYSTEM_ESCAPE, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'rm -rf / — catastrophic deletion attempt' },
    { pattern: /curl|wget/g, type: SandboxViolation.NETWORK_ACCESS, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'Network download command detected' },
    { pattern: /chmod\s+[0-9]*7[0-9]*/g, type: SandboxViolation.PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'Dangerous chmod permissions' },
    { pattern: /sudo\s+/g, type: SandboxViolation.PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'sudo command detected' },
    { pattern: /:\s*\(\s*\)\s*\{.*:\|:&\s*\}/g, type: SandboxViolation.FORK_BOMB, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'Fork bomb pattern detected' },
    { pattern: /\/etc\/passwd|\/etc\/shadow/g, type: SandboxViolation.FILE_SYSTEM_ESCAPE, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'System credential file access' },
    { pattern: /base64\s+-d/g, type: SandboxViolation.SHELLCODE_DETECTED, severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH, desc: 'Base64 decode — potential shellcode delivery' },
    { pattern: /nc\s+.*-e\s+\/bin\//g, type: SandboxViolation.SHELLCODE_DETECTED, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'Netcat reverse shell pattern' },
    { pattern: />\s*\/dev\/tcp\//g, type: SandboxViolation.NETWORK_ACCESS, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'TCP redirect shell detected' },
    { pattern: /dd\s+if=\/dev\/zero\s+of=\//g, type: SandboxViolation.FILE_SYSTEM_ESCAPE, severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, desc: 'Device zeroing attack detected' },
  ];

  analyze(code: string, language: CodeLanguage): StaticAnalysisResult {
    const start = Date.now();
    const findings: StaticFinding[] = [];
    const blockedPatterns: string[] = [];
    const suspiciousImports: string[] = [];

    const patterns = this.getPatternsForLanguage(language);

    for (const { pattern, type, severity, desc } of patterns) {
      const matches = [...code.matchAll(new RegExp(pattern.source, pattern.flags.includes('g') ? pattern.flags : pattern.flags + 'g'))];
      for (const match of matches) {
        const lineNum = code.substring(0, match.index || 0).split('\n').length;
        findings.push({
          id: crypto.randomUUID(),
          type,
          severity,
          line: lineNum,
          snippet: match[0].substring(0, 100),
          description: desc,
          recommendation: this.getRecommendation(type),
        });
        if (severity >= ThreatLevel.HIGH) {
          blockedPatterns.push(desc);
        }
      }
    }

    const imports = this.extractImports(code, language);
    for (const imp of imports) {
      if (this.isSuspiciousImport(imp, language)) {
        suspiciousImports.push(imp);
      }
    }

    const maxSeverity = findings.reduce((max, f) => Math.max(max, f.severity), ThreatLevel.SAFE);
    const complexity = this.estimateComplexity(code);

    return {
      safe: maxSeverity < ThreatLevel.HIGH,
      threatLevel: maxSeverity as ThreatLevel,
      findings,
      blockedPatterns,
      suspiciousImports,
      estimatedComplexity: complexity,
      analysisMs: Date.now() - start,
    };
  }

  private getPatternsForLanguage(language: CodeLanguage) {
    switch (language) {
      case CodeLanguage.PYTHON:
        return this.dangerousPythonPatterns;
      case CodeLanguage.SHELL:
        return this.dangerousShellPatterns;
      case CodeLanguage.JAVASCRIPT:
      case CodeLanguage.TYPESCRIPT:
      default:
        return this.dangerousJSPatterns;
    }
  }

  private extractImports(code: string, language: CodeLanguage): string[] {
    const imports: string[] = [];
    if (language === CodeLanguage.JAVASCRIPT || language === CodeLanguage.TYPESCRIPT) {
      const reqMatches = code.matchAll(/require\s*\(\s*['"]([^'"]+)['"]\s*\)/g);
      for (const m of reqMatches) imports.push(m[1]);
      const importMatches = code.matchAll(/^import\s+.*\s+from\s+['"]([^'"]+)['"]/gm);
      for (const m of importMatches) imports.push(m[1]);
    } else if (language === CodeLanguage.PYTHON) {
      const importMatches = code.matchAll(/^(?:import|from)\s+(\S+)/gm);
      for (const m of importMatches) imports.push(m[1]);
    }
    return imports;
  }

  private isSuspiciousImport(imp: string, language: CodeLanguage): boolean {
    const suspicious = {
      javascript: ['child_process', 'cluster', 'worker_threads', 'vm', 'repl', 'v8'],
      python: ['os', 'subprocess', 'socket', 'ctypes', 'cffi', 'pickle', 'marshal', 'importlib'],
    };
    const list = language === CodeLanguage.PYTHON ? suspicious.python : suspicious.javascript;
    return list.some(s => imp.startsWith(s));
  }

  private estimateComplexity(code: string): number {
    const loops = (code.match(/for\s*\(|while\s*\(|for\s+\w+\s+in/g) || []).length;
    const conditions = (code.match(/if\s*\(|else\s+if|elif\s+/g) || []).length;
    const functions = (code.match(/function\s+\w+|=>\s*\{|def\s+\w+/g) || []).length;
    const lines = code.split('\n').length;
    return Math.min(100, loops * 3 + conditions * 2 + functions * 1 + Math.floor(lines / 50));
  }

  private getRecommendation(type: SandboxViolation): string {
    const map: Record<SandboxViolation, string> = {
      [SandboxViolation.NETWORK_ACCESS]: 'Remove network access or whitelist specific endpoints',
      [SandboxViolation.FILE_SYSTEM_ESCAPE]: 'Restrict file operations to designated sandbox directory',
      [SandboxViolation.PROCESS_SPAWN]: 'Avoid spawning child processes; use built-in APIs',
      [SandboxViolation.MEMORY_EXCEEDED]: 'Reduce memory allocations and use streaming where possible',
      [SandboxViolation.CPU_EXCEEDED]: 'Optimize CPU-intensive operations or use async patterns',
      [SandboxViolation.SYSCALL_BLOCKED]: 'Remove direct syscall usage; use safe abstractions',
      [SandboxViolation.PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION]: 'Remove privilege escalation attempts',
      [SandboxViolation.CRYPTO_MINING]: 'Remove cryptographic mining operations',
      [SandboxViolation.SHELLCODE_DETECTED]: 'Remove obfuscated or encoded execution patterns',
      [SandboxViolation.EXFILTRATION_ATTEMPT]: 'Remove data exfiltration patterns',
      [SandboxViolation.INFINITE_LOOP]: 'Add exit conditions to all loops',
      [SandboxViolation.FORK_BOMB]: 'Remove recursive fork/spawn patterns',
      [SandboxViolation.PATH_TRAVERSAL]: 'Use path.resolve and validate paths against whitelist',
      [SandboxViolation.ENV_ACCESS]: 'Remove environment variable access or declare explicitly',
      [SandboxViolation.INJECTION_ATTEMPT]: 'Remove eval/exec/dynamic code execution patterns',
    };
    return map[type] || 'Review and remove potentially unsafe code pattern';
  }
}

// ============================================================
// RUNTIME MONITOR
// ============================================================

export class RuntimeMonitor extends EventEmitter {
  private process?: ChildProcess;
  private memoryInterval?: NodeJS.Timeout;
  private cpuSamples: number[] = [];
  private startTime?: Date;
  private usage: ResourceUsage = {
    cpuPercent: 0,
    memoryMB: 0,
    peakMemoryMB: 0,
    networkBytesIn: 0,
    networkBytesOut: 0,
    diskReadBytes: 0,
    diskWriteBytes: 0,
    fileHandles: 0,
    childProcesses: 0,
    executionMs: 0,
  };

  attach(proc: ChildProcess, limits: SandboxLimits): void {
    this.process = proc;
    this.startTime = new Date();
    this.memoryInterval = setInterval(() => this.sample(limits), 100);
  }

  private sample(limits: SandboxLimits): void {
    if (!this.process?.pid) return;
    try {
      const memInfo = this.getProcessMemory(this.process.pid);
      this.usage.memoryMB = memInfo;
      this.usage.peakMemoryMB = Math.max(this.usage.peakMemoryMB, memInfo);
      this.usage.executionMs = Date.now() - (this.startTime?.getTime() || Date.now());

      if (memInfo > limits.maxMemoryMB) {
        this.emit('violation', {
          type: SandboxViolation.MEMORY_EXCEEDED,
          severity: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL,
          description: `Memory limit exceeded: ${memInfo.toFixed(1)}MB > ${limits.maxMemoryMB}MB`,
        });
      }

      if (this.usage.executionMs > limits.maxExecutionMs) {
        this.emit('violation', {
          type: SandboxViolation.INFINITE_LOOP,
          severity: ThreatLevel.HIGH,
          description: `Execution timeout: ${this.usage.executionMs}ms > ${limits.maxExecutionMs}ms`,
        });
      }
    } catch (_err) {
      // Process may have ended
    }
  }

  private getProcessMemory(pid: number): number {
    try {
      if (process.platform === 'linux') {
        const status = require('fs').readFileSync(`/proc/${pid}/status`, 'utf8');
        const vmRss = status.match(/VmRSS:\s+(\d+)/);
        return vmRss ? parseInt(vmRss[1]) / 1024 : 0;
      }
      return process.memoryUsage().heapUsed / 1024 / 1024;
    } catch {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  getUsage(): ResourceUsage {
    return { ...this.usage };
  }

  detach(): void {
    if (this.memoryInterval) clearInterval(this.memoryInterval);
    if (this.startTime) {
      this.usage.executionMs = Date.now() - this.startTime.getTime();
    }
  }
}

// ============================================================
// SANDBOX ISOLATION LAYER
// ============================================================

export class SandboxIsolation {
  private readonly sandboxDir: string;

  constructor(baseDir: string) {
    this.sandboxDir = baseDir;
    if (!existsSync(this.sandboxDir)) {
      mkdirSync(this.sandboxDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  createExecutionDir(executionId: string): string {
    const dir = path.join(this.sandboxDir, executionId);
    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    return dir;
  }

  writeCode(dir: string, code: string, language: CodeLanguage): string {
    const ext: Record<CodeLanguage, string> = {
      [CodeLanguage.JAVASCRIPT]: 'js',
      [CodeLanguage.TYPESCRIPT]: 'ts',
      [CodeLanguage.PYTHON]: 'py',
      [CodeLanguage.SHELL]: 'sh',
      [CodeLanguage.JSON]: 'json',
      [CodeLanguage.SQL]: 'sql',
    };
    const filePath = path.join(dir, `code.${ext[language] || 'txt'}`);
    writeFileSync(filePath, code, 'utf8');
    return filePath;
  }

  cleanup(executionId: string): void {
    const dir = path.join(this.sandboxDir, executionId);
    if (existsSync(dir)) {
      try {
        rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
      } catch (_err) {
        // Best-effort cleanup
      }
    }
  }

  buildNodeWrapper(codePath: string, limits: SandboxLimits): string {
    return `
const vm = require('vm');
const fs = require('fs');

const code = fs.readFileSync('${codePath}', 'utf8');
const allowedGlobals = {
  console: console,
  Math: Math,
  JSON: JSON,
  Date: Date,
  parseInt: parseInt,
  parseFloat: parseFloat,
  String: String,
  Number: Number,
  Boolean: Boolean,
  Array: Array,
  Object: Object,
  Map: Map,
  Set: Set,
  Promise: Promise,
  setTimeout: setTimeout,
  clearTimeout: clearTimeout,
  Buffer: Buffer,
};

const sandbox = vm.createContext(allowedGlobals);
const script = new vm.Script(code, { filename: 'sandboxed.js' });

try {
  const result = script.runInContext(sandbox, { timeout: ${limits.maxExecutionMs} });
  if (result !== undefined) console.log(JSON.stringify(result));
  process.exit(0);
} catch (err) {
  console.error(err.message);
  process.exit(1);
}
`;
  }

  buildPythonWrapper(codePath: string, limits: SandboxLimits): string {
    return `
import sys
import resource
import signal

resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_AS, (${limits.maxMemoryMB * 1024 * 1024}, ${limits.maxMemoryMB * 1024 * 1024}))
resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_CPU, (${Math.ceil(limits.maxExecutionMs / 1000)}, ${Math.ceil(limits.maxExecutionMs / 1000)}))
resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_NOFILE, (${limits.maxOpenFiles}, ${limits.maxOpenFiles}))
resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_NPROC, (${limits.maxProcesses}, ${limits.maxProcesses}))

builtins_to_remove = ['__import__', 'open', 'exec', 'eval', 'compile', 'reload']
safe_builtins = {k: v for k, v in __builtins__.items() if k not in builtins_to_remove} if isinstance(__builtins__, dict) else {}

try:
  with open('${codePath}', 'r') as f:
    code = f.read()
  exec(compile(code, '${codePath}', 'exec'), {'__builtins__': safe_builtins})
except Exception as e:
  print(str(e), file=sys.stderr)
  sys.exit(1)
`;
  }
}
