export class PolicyManager {
  private readonly policies: Map<string, SandboxPolicy> = new Map();

  constructor() {
    this.registerDefaults();
  }

  private registerDefaults(): void {
    this.register({
      id: 'strict',
      name: 'Strict Isolation',
      limits: {
        maxMemoryMB: 128,
        maxCpuPercent: 50,
        maxExecutionMs: 5000,
        maxOutputBytes: 64 * 1024,
        maxFileSize: 1024 * 1024,
        maxOpenFiles: 10,
        maxProcesses: 1,
        maxNetworkBytesIn: 0,
        maxNetworkBytesOut: 0,
        allowNetwork: false,
        allowFileSystem: false,
        allowedPaths: [],
        blockedPaths: ['/etc', '/proc', '/sys', '/root', '/home'],
        allowEnvVars: false,
        allowedEnvVars: [],
        allowProcessSpawn: false,
        allowedCommands: [],
      },
      allowedLanguages: [CodeLanguage.JAVASCRIPT, CodeLanguage.PYTHON],
      blockedSyscalls: ['fork', 'exec', 'socket', 'connect', 'bind', 'listen', 'open', 'rename', 'unlink', 'mkdir'],
      blockedModules: ['child_process', 'cluster', 'worker_threads', 'net', 'dgram', 'http', 'https', 'fs', 'os'],
      blockedPatterns: [/eval\s*\(/, /Function\s*\(/, /__proto__/, /constructor\[/],
      requireSignature: false,
      autoTerminateOnViolation: true,
      logAllActivity: true,
      maxRetries: 0,
      cooldownMs: 0,
    });

    this.register({
      id: 'permissive',
      name: 'Permissive (trusted code)',
      limits: {
        maxMemoryMB: 512,
        maxCpuPercent: 80,
        maxExecutionMs: 30000,
        maxOutputBytes: 10 * 1024 * 1024,
        maxFileSize: 50 * 1024 * 1024,
        maxOpenFiles: 100,
        maxProcesses: 10,
        maxNetworkBytesIn: 100 * 1024 * 1024,
        maxNetworkBytesOut: 10 * 1024 * 1024,
        allowNetwork: true,
        allowFileSystem: true,
        allowedPaths: ['/tmp', '/home/sandbox'],
        blockedPaths: ['/etc', '/proc', '/sys', '/root'],
        allowEnvVars: true,
        allowedEnvVars: ['PATH', 'HOME', 'NODE_ENV'],
        allowProcessSpawn: false,
        allowedCommands: [],
      },
      allowedLanguages: [CodeLanguage.JAVASCRIPT, CodeLanguage.TYPESCRIPT, CodeLanguage.PYTHON, CodeLanguage.SHELL],
      blockedSyscalls: ['fork', 'ptrace'],
      blockedModules: ['cluster'],
      blockedPatterns: [/__proto__/, /constructor\[/],
      requireSignature: false,
      autoTerminateOnViolation: false,
      logAllActivity: true,
      maxRetries: 2,
      cooldownMs: 1000,
    });

    this.register({
      id: 'automation',
      name: 'Automation Engine',
      limits: {
        maxMemoryMB: 256,
        maxCpuPercent: 60,
        maxExecutionMs: 60000,
        maxOutputBytes: 5 * 1024 * 1024,
        maxFileSize: 10 * 1024 * 1024,
        maxOpenFiles: 50,
        maxProcesses: 5,
        maxNetworkBytesIn: 50 * 1024 * 1024,
        maxNetworkBytesOut: 5 * 1024 * 1024,
        allowNetwork: true,
        allowFileSystem: true,
        allowedPaths: ['/tmp/ube-sandbox'],
        blockedPaths: ['/etc', '/proc', '/sys', '/root', '/home'],
        allowEnvVars: false,
        allowedEnvVars: ['NODE_ENV', 'UBE_CONTEXT'],
        allowProcessSpawn: false,
        allowedCommands: [],
      },
      allowedLanguages: [CodeLanguage.JAVASCRIPT, CodeLanguage.TYPESCRIPT, CodeLanguage.PYTHON],
      blockedSyscalls: ['fork', 'ptrace', 'socket'],
      blockedModules: ['child_process', 'cluster', 'worker_threads'],
      blockedPatterns: [/eval\s*\(/, /__proto__/],
      requireSignature: false,
      autoTerminateOnViolation: true,
      logAllActivity: true,
      maxRetries: 1,
      cooldownMs: 500,
    });
  }

  register(policy: SandboxPolicy): void {
    this.policies.set(policy.id, policy);
  }

  get(id: string): SandboxPolicy | undefined {
    return this.policies.get(id);
  }

  getDefault(): SandboxPolicy {
    return this.policies.get('strict')!;
  }

  list(): SandboxPolicy[] {
    return Array.from(this.policies.values());
  }

  update(id: string, updates: Partial<SandboxPolicy>): boolean {
    const existing = this.policies.get(id);
    if (!existing) return false;
    this.policies.set(id, { ...existing, ...updates });
    return true;
  }

  delete(id: string): boolean {
    if (['strict', 'permissive', 'automation'].includes(id)) return false;
    return this.policies.delete(id);
  }
}

// ============================================================
// EXECUTION ENGINE
// ============================================================

export class ExecutionEngine {
  private readonly isolation: SandboxIsolation;
  private readonly monitor: RuntimeMonitor;

  constructor(isolation: SandboxIsolation) {
    this.isolation = isolation;
    this.monitor = new RuntimeMonitor();
  }

  async execute(
    execution: SandboxExecution,
    policy: SandboxPolicy
  ): Promise<SandboxResult> {
    const dir = this.isolation.createExecutionDir(execution.id);

    try {
      const codePath = this.isolation.writeCode(dir, execution.code, execution.language);
      const command = this.buildCommand(execution, policy, dir, codePath);

      return await new Promise<SandboxResult>((resolve) => {
        const proc = spawn(command.cmd, command.args, {
          cwd: dir,
          env: this.buildEnv(execution, policy),
          timeout: policy.limits.maxExecutionMs,
          killSignal: 'SIGKILL',
          uid: process.getuid ? process.getuid() : undefined,
          gid: process.getgid ? process.getgid() : undefined,
        });

        this.monitor.attach(proc, policy.limits);

        let stdout = '';
        let stderr = '';
        let outputBytes = 0;
        let truncated = false;

        proc.stdout?.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => {
          outputBytes += chunk.length;
          if (outputBytes <= policy.limits.maxOutputBytes) {
            stdout += chunk.toString();
          } else {
            truncated = true;
          }
        });

        proc.stderr?.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => {
          stderr += chunk.toString().substring(0, 4096);
        });

        const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
          proc.kill('SIGKILL');
          resolve({
            stdout,
            stderr: `[SANDBOX] Execution timeout after ${policy.limits.maxExecutionMs}ms`,
            exitCode: -1,
            success: false,
            truncated,
            outputBytes,
            error: 'TIMEOUT',
          });
        }, policy.limits.maxExecutionMs + 500);

        this.monitor.on('violation', () => {
          if (policy.autoTerminateOnViolation) {
            proc.kill('SIGKILL');
          }
        });

        proc.on('close', (exitCode) => {
          clearTimeout(timeout);
          this.monitor.detach();
          resolve({
            stdout: stdout.substring(0, policy.limits.maxOutputBytes),
            stderr: stderr.substring(0, 4096),
            exitCode: exitCode ?? -1,
            success: exitCode === 0,
            truncated,
            outputBytes,
          });
        });

        proc.on('error', (err) => {
          clearTimeout(timeout);
          this.monitor.detach();
          resolve({
            stdout: '',
            stderr: err.message,
            exitCode: -1,
            success: false,
            truncated: false,
            outputBytes: 0,
            error: err.message,
          });
        });
      });
    } finally {
      this.isolation.cleanup(execution.id);
    }
  }

  private buildCommand(
    execution: SandboxExecution,
    policy: SandboxPolicy,
    dir: string,
    codePath: string
  ): { cmd: string; args: string[] } {
    switch (execution.language) {
      case CodeLanguage.PYTHON:
        return { cmd: 'python3', args: [codePath] };
      case CodeLanguage.SHELL:
        return { cmd: 'bash', args: ['-e', '-u', '-o', 'pipefail', codePath] };
      case CodeLanguage.JAVASCRIPT:
      case CodeLanguage.TYPESCRIPT: {
        const wrapperPath = path.join(dir, 'wrapper.js');
        writeFileSync(wrapperPath, this.isolation.buildNodeWrapper(codePath, policy.limits));
        return { cmd: 'node', args: ['--max-old-space-size=' + policy.limits.maxMemoryMB, wrapperPath] };
      }
      default:
        return { cmd: 'cat', args: [codePath] };
    }
  }

  private buildEnv(execution: SandboxExecution, policy: SandboxPolicy): Record<string, string> {
    const base: Record<string, string> = { HOME: '/tmp', PATH: '/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin' };
    if (!policy.limits.allowEnvVars) return base;
    for (const key of policy.limits.allowedEnvVars) {
      if (process.env[key]) base[key] = process.env[key]!;
    }
    if (execution.environment) {
      for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(execution.environment)) {
        base[k] = v;
      }
    }
    return base;
  }
}

// ============================================================
// MAIN SANDBOX ENGINE
// ============================================================

export class SandboxEngine extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly analyzer: StaticAnalyzer;
  private readonly policyManager: PolicyManager;
  private readonly isolation: SandboxIsolation;
  private readonly execEngine: ExecutionEngine;
  private readonly config: SandboxConfig;
  private readonly executions: Map<string, SandboxExecution> = new Map();
  private activeCount: number = 0;
  private metrics: SandboxMetrics = {
    totalExecutions: 0,
    successfulExecutions: 0,
    failedExecutions: 0,
    violationsDetected: 0,
    averageExecutionMs: 0,
    totalCpuMs: 0,
    totalMemoryMBPeak: 0,
    byLanguage: {},
    byViolationType: {},
    uptime: Date.now(),
  };

  constructor(config: SandboxConfig = {}) {
    super();
    this.config = {
      enableStaticAnalysis: true,
      enableRuntimeMonitoring: true,
      enableNetworkIsolation: true,
      enableFilesystemIsolation: true,
      sandboxDir: path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ube-sandbox'),
      maxConcurrentExecutions: 10,
      enableAudit: true,
      ...config,
    };
    this.analyzer = new StaticAnalyzer();
    this.policyManager = new PolicyManager();
    this.isolation = new SandboxIsolation(this.config.sandboxDir!);
    this.execEngine = new ExecutionEngine(this.isolation);
  }

  async analyze(code: string, language: CodeLanguage): Promise<StaticAnalysisResult> {
    return this.analyzer.analyze(code, language);
  }

  async execute(
    code: string,
    language: CodeLanguage,
    policyId: string = 'strict',
    options: {
      environment?: Record<string, string>;
      args?: string[];
      metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
      actorId?: string;
    } = {}
  ): Promise<SandboxExecution> {
    if (this.activeCount >= (this.config.maxConcurrentExecutions || 10)) {
      throw new Error(`[SANDBOX] Max concurrent executions (${this.config.maxConcurrentExecutions}) reached`);
    }

    const policy = this.policyManager.get(policyId) || this.policyManager.getDefault();

    if (!policy.allowedLanguages.includes(language)) {
      throw new Error(`[SANDBOX] Language ${language} not allowed by policy ${policyId}`);
    }

    const codeHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(code).digest('hex');

    const execution: SandboxExecution = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      policyId,
      language,
      code,
      codeHash,
      status: SandboxStatus.IDLE,
      violations: [],
      resourceUsage: {
        cpuPercent: 0, memoryMB: 0, peakMemoryMB: 0,
        networkBytesIn: 0, networkBytesOut: 0,
        diskReadBytes: 0, diskWriteBytes: 0,
        fileHandles: 0, childProcesses: 0, executionMs: 0,
      },
      threatLevel: ThreatLevel.SAFE,
      retryCount: 0,
      submittedAt: new Date(),
      environment: options.environment,
      args: options.args,
      metadata: options.metadata,
    };

    this.executions.set(execution.id, execution);
    this.metrics.totalExecutions++;
    this.metrics.byLanguage[language] = (this.metrics.byLanguage[language] || 0) + 1;

    if (this.config.enableStaticAnalysis) {
      const analysis = this.analyzer.analyze(code, language);
      execution.threatLevel = analysis.threatLevel;

      if (!analysis.safe) {
        const violations = analysis.findings.map(f => ({
          id: crypto.randomUUID(),
          timestamp: new Date(),
          type: f.type,
          severity: f.severity,
          description: f.description,
          context: { line: f.line, snippet: f.snippet },
          blocked: f.severity >= ThreatLevel.HIGH,
          autoKilled: false,
        }));
        execution.violations.push(...violations);
        this.metrics.violationsDetected += violations.length;

        if (analysis.threatLevel >= ThreatLevel.CRITICAL) {
          execution.status = SandboxStatus.FAILED;
          execution.result = {
            stdout: '',
            stderr: `[SANDBOX] Execution blocked by static analysis: ${analysis.findings[0]?.description}`,
            exitCode: -1,
            success: false,
            truncated: false,
            outputBytes: 0,
            error: 'STATIC_ANALYSIS_BLOCKED',
          };
          this.metrics.failedExecutions++;
          this.emit('blocked', execution);
          return execution;
        }
      }
    }

    execution.status = SandboxStatus.RUNNING;
    execution.startedAt = new Date();
    this.activeCount++;

    this.emit('execution:start', execution);

    try {
      const result = await this.execEngine.execute(execution, policy);
      execution.result = result;
      execution.status = result.success ? SandboxStatus.COMPLETED : SandboxStatus.FAILED;
      execution.completedAt = new Date();

      if (result.success) {
        this.metrics.successfulExecutions++;
      } else {
        this.metrics.failedExecutions++;
      }

      const duration = execution.completedAt.getTime() - execution.startedAt!.getTime();
      this.metrics.averageExecutionMs =
        (this.metrics.averageExecutionMs * (this.metrics.totalExecutions - 1) + duration) /
        this.metrics.totalExecutions;

    } catch (err) {
      execution.status = SandboxStatus.FAILED;
      execution.result = {
        stdout: '',
        stderr: String(err),
        exitCode: -1,
        success: false,
        truncated: false,
        outputBytes: 0,
        error: String(err),
      };
      this.metrics.failedExecutions++;
    } finally {
      this.activeCount--;
    }

    this.emit('execution:complete', execution);
    return execution;
  }

  getExecution(id: string): SandboxExecution | undefined {
    return this.executions.get(id);
  }

  killExecution(id: string): boolean {
    const execution = this.executions.get(id);
    if (!execution || execution.status !== SandboxStatus.RUNNING) return false;
    execution.status = SandboxStatus.KILLED;
    this.emit('execution:killed', execution);
    return true;
  }

  registerPolicy(policy: SandboxPolicy): void {
    this.policyManager.register(policy);
  }

  getPolicy(id: string): SandboxPolicy | undefined {
    return this.policyManager.get(id);
  }

  listPolicies(): SandboxPolicy[] {
    return this.policyManager.list();
  }

  getMetrics(): SandboxMetrics {
    return {
      ...this.metrics,
      uptime: Date.now() - this.metrics.uptime,
    };
  }

  getActiveCount(): number {
    return this.activeCount;
  }

  clearHistory(olderThanMs?: number): number {
    let cleared = 0;
    const cutoff = olderThanMs ? Date.now() - olderThanMs : 0;
    for (const [id, exec] of this.executions.entries()) {
      if (
        exec.status !== SandboxStatus.RUNNING &&
        (!cutoff || exec.submittedAt.getTime() < cutoff)
      ) {
        this.executions.delete(id);
        cleared++;
      }
    }
    return cleared;
  }
}

// ============================================================
// FACTORY
// ============================================================

export function createSandboxEngine(config?: SandboxConfig): SandboxEngine {
  return new SandboxEngine(config);
}

export const defaultSandbox = createSandboxEngine();

export default SandboxEngine;

export enum OrchestrationStatus {
  IDLE = 'IDLE',
  QUEUED = 'QUEUED',
  STARTING = 'STARTING',
  RUNNING = 'RUNNING',
  PAUSED = 'PAUSED',
  WAITING = 'WAITING',
  COMPLETING = 'COMPLETING',
  COMPLETED = 'COMPLETED',
  FAILED = 'FAILED',
  CANCELLED = 'CANCELLED',
  TIMED_OUT = 'TIMED_OUT',
  RETRYING = 'RETRYING',
  COMPENSATING = 'COMPENSATING',
  COMPENSATED = 'COMPENSATED',
}

export enum StepType {
  TASK = 'TASK',
  PARALLEL = 'PARALLEL',
  SEQUENTIAL = 'SEQUENTIAL',
  CONDITIONAL = 'CONDITIONAL',
  LOOP = 'LOOP',
  WAIT = 'WAIT',
  CALLBACK = 'CALLBACK',
  HUMAN_APPROVAL = 'HUMAN_APPROVAL',
  SAGA = 'SAGA',
  FORK = 'FORK',
  JOIN = 'JOIN',
  TIMER = 'TIMER',
  SIGNAL = 'SIGNAL',
  SUB_WORKFLOW = 'SUB_WORKFLOW',
  CIRCUIT_BREAKER = 'CIRCUIT_BREAKER',
}

export enum WorkflowPriority {
  CRITICAL = 0,
  HIGH = 1,
  NORMAL = 2,
  LOW = 3,
  BACKGROUND = 4,
}

export enum RetryStrategy {
  NONE = 'NONE',
  FIXED = 'FIXED',
  LINEAR = 'LINEAR',
  EXPONENTIAL = 'EXPONENTIAL',
  EXPONENTIAL_JITTER = 'EXPONENTIAL_JITTER',
  FIBONACCI = 'FIBONACCI',

}

}

// ============================================================
// CIRCUIT BREAKER
// ============================================================

export class CircuitBreaker {
  private state: CircuitState = CircuitState.CLOSED;
  private failures: number = 0;
  private successes: number = 0;
  private lastFailureTime?: Date;
  private readonly config: CircuitBreakerConfig;

  constructor(config: CircuitBreakerConfig) {
    this.config = config;
  }

  async execute<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    if (this.state === CircuitState.OPEN) {
      if (!this.lastFailureTime || Date.now() - this.lastFailureTime.getTime() > this.config.timeout) {
        this.state = CircuitState.HALF_OPEN;
      } else {
        throw new Error(`Circuit breaker OPEN for ${this.config.id}`);
      }
    }

    try {
      const result = await fn();
      this.onSuccess();
      return result;
    } catch (err) {
      this.onFailure();
      throw err;
    }
  }

  private onSuccess(): void {
    this.failures = 0;
    if (this.state === CircuitState.HALF_OPEN) {
      this.successes++;
      if (this.successes >= this.config.successThreshold) {
        this.state = CircuitState.CLOSED;
        this.successes = 0;
      }
    }
  }

  private onFailure(): void {
    this.failures++;
    this.lastFailureTime = new Date();
    if (this.failures >= this.config.failureThreshold) {
      this.state = CircuitState.OPEN;
    }
  }

  getState(): CircuitState { return this.state; }
  reset(): void { this.state = CircuitState.CLOSED; this.failures = 0; this.successes = 0; }
}

// ============================================================
// RETRY ENGINE
// ============================================================

export class RetryEngine {
  async execute<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>, policy: RetryPolicy): Promise<T> {
    let attempt = 0;
    while (true) {
      try {
        return await fn();
      } catch (err) {
        attempt++;
        if (attempt >= policy.maxAttempts) throw err;
        const error = err as Error;
        if (policy.noRetryOn?.some(e => error.message.includes(e))) throw err;
        if (policy.retryOn && !policy.retryOn.some(e => error.message.includes(e))) throw err;
        const delay = this.computeDelay(attempt, policy);
        await this.sleep(delay);
      }
    }
  }

  private computeDelay(attempt: number, policy: RetryPolicy): number {
    const base = policy.baseDelayMs;
    const max = policy.maxDelayMs;
    let delay: number;

    switch (policy.strategy) {
      case RetryStrategy.FIXED:
        delay = base;
        break;
      case RetryStrategy.LINEAR:
        delay = base * attempt;
        break;
      case RetryStrategy.EXPONENTIAL:
        delay = base * Math.pow(policy.multiplier || 2, attempt - 1);
        break;
      case RetryStrategy.EXPONENTIAL_JITTER:
        delay = base * Math.pow(policy.multiplier || 2, attempt - 1);
        delay += Math.random() * (policy.jitterMs || base * 0.1);
        break;
      case RetryStrategy.FIBONACCI:
        delay = base * this.fibonacci(attempt);
        break;
      case RetryStrategy.CUSTOM:
        delay = policy.backoffFn ? policy.backoffFn(attempt, base) : base;
        break;
      default:
        delay = base;
    }

    return Math.min(delay, max);
  }

  private fibonacci(n: number): number {
    if (n <= 1) return n;
    let a = 0, b = 1;
    for (let i = 2; i <= n; i++) { [a, b] = [b, a + b]; }
    return b;
  }

  private sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
  }
}

// ============================================================
// SAGA COORDINATOR
// ============================================================

export class SagaCoordinator {
  private readonly compensations: Map<string, CompensationRecord> = new Map();

  async execute(
    steps: Array<{ id: string; execute: () => Promise<unknown>; compensate?: () => Promise<void> }>,
    strategy: CompensationStrategy = CompensationStrategy.BACKWARD
  ): Promise<unknown[]> {
    const results: unknown[] = [];
    const executed: string[] = [];

    for (const step of steps) {
      try {
        const result = await step.execute();
        results.push(result);
        executed.push(step.id);
      } catch (err) {
        await this.compensate(steps, executed, strategy);
        throw err;
      }
    }

    return results;
  }

  private async compensate(
    steps: Array<{ id: string; execute: () => Promise<unknown>; compensate?: () => Promise<void> }>,
    executed: string[],
    strategy: CompensationStrategy
  ): Promise<void> {
    const toCompensate = strategy === CompensationStrategy.BACKWARD
      ? [...executed].reverse()
      : executed;

    if (strategy === CompensationStrategy.PARALLEL) {
      await Promise.allSettled(
        toCompensate.map(id => {
          const step = steps.find(s => s.id === id);
          return step?.compensate?.() || Promise.resolve();
        })
      );
    } else {
      for (const id of toCompensate) {
        const step = steps.find(s => s.id === id);
        if (step?.compensate) {
          const record: CompensationRecord = {
            id: crypto.randomUUID(),
            stepId: id,
            handler: 'compensate',
            status: 'running',
            startedAt: new Date(),
          };
          this.compensations.set(record.id, record);
          try {
            await step.compensate();
            record.status = 'completed';
            record.completedAt = new Date();
          } catch (err) {
            record.status = 'failed';
            record.error = String(err);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

// ============================================================
// EXPRESSION EVALUATOR
// ============================================================

export class ExpressionEvaluator {
  evaluate(expression: string, context: WorkflowContext): unknown {
    try {
      const vars = { ...context.variables, ...context.stepOutputs };
      const fn = new Function(...Object.keys(vars), `return (${expression})`);
      return fn(...Object.values(vars));
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  evaluateCondition(condition: string, context: WorkflowContext): boolean {
    return !!this.evaluate(condition, context);
  }

  resolveExpression(template: string, context: WorkflowContext): string {
    return template.replace(/\$\{([^}]+)\}/g, (_, expr) => {
      const result = this.evaluate(expr.trim(), context);
      return result !== undefined && result !== null ? String(result) : '';
    });
  }

  resolveInputs(inputs: Record<string, unknown>, context: WorkflowContext): Record<string, unknown> {
    const resolved: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(inputs)) {
      if (typeof value === 'string' && value.includes('${')) {
        resolved[key] = this.resolveExpression(value, context);
      } else {
        resolved[key] = value;
      }
    }
    return resolved;
  }
}

// ============================================================
// STEP EXECUTOR
// ============================================================

export class StepExecutor {
  private readonly handlers: Map<string, TaskHandler> = new Map();
  private readonly retryEngine: RetryEngine;
  private readonly circuitBreakers: Map<string, CircuitBreaker> = new Map();
  private readonly evaluator: ExpressionEvaluator;

  constructor() {
    this.retryEngine = new RetryEngine();
    this.evaluator = new ExpressionEvaluator();
  }

  register(handler: TaskHandler): void {
    this.handlers.set(handler.name, handler);
  }

  async execute(step: StepDefinition, context: WorkflowContext, instance: WorkflowInstance): Promise<unknown> {
    if (step.skipCondition && this.evaluator.evaluateCondition(step.skipCondition, context)) {
      return { skipped: true };
    }

    const resolvedInput = step.input ? this.evaluator.resolveInputs(step.input, context) : {};
    const handler = step.handler ? this.handlers.get(step.handler) : null;

    switch (step.type) {
      case StepType.TASK:
        return this.executeTask(step, resolvedInput, context, handler);
      case StepType.PARALLEL:
        return this.executeParallel(step, context, instance);
      case StepType.CONDITIONAL:
        return this.executeConditional(step, context, instance);
      case StepType.LOOP:
        return this.executeLoop(step, context, instance);
      case StepType.WAIT:
        return this.executeWait(step, context);
      case StepType.HUMAN_APPROVAL:
        return this.executeHumanApproval(step, instance);
      case StepType.TIMER:
        return this.executeTimer(step);
      default:
        if (handler) return this.executeTask(step, resolvedInput, context, handler);
        return null;
    }
  }

  private async executeTask(step: StepDefinition, input: Record<string, unknown>, context: WorkflowContext, handler: TaskHandler | null): Promise<unknown> {
    const fn = handler
      ? () => handler.execute(input, context)
      : () => Promise.resolve({ executed: true, step: step.id, input });

    const policy = step.retryPolicy;
    if (policy && policy.maxAttempts > 1) {
      if (step.circuitBreaker) {
        const cb = this.getCircuitBreaker(step.circuitBreaker);
        return cb.execute(() => this.retryEngine.execute(fn, policy));
      }
      return this.retryEngine.execute(fn, policy);
    }

    if (step.circuitBreaker) {
      const cb = this.getCircuitBreaker(step.circuitBreaker);
      return cb.execute(fn);
    }

    const timeout = step.timeout || handler?.timeout;
    if (timeout) {
      return Promise.race([
        fn(),
        new Promise<never>((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`Step ${step.id} timed out`)), timeout)),
      ]);
    }

    return fn();
  }

  private async executeParallel(step: StepDefinition, context: WorkflowContext, instance: WorkflowInstance): Promise<unknown[]> {
    const steps = step.parallel || [];
    return Promise.all(steps.map(s => this.execute(s, context, instance)));
  }

  private async executeConditional(step: StepDefinition, context: WorkflowContext, instance: WorkflowInstance): Promise<unknown> {
    for (const condition of step.conditions || []) {
      if (this.evaluator.evaluateCondition(condition.expression, context)) {
        const targetStep = instance.definitionId && condition.onTrue
          ? { id: condition.onTrue, type: StepType.TASK, name: condition.onTrue }
          : null;
        return targetStep ? this.execute(targetStep as StepDefinition, context, instance) : condition.onTrue;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  private async executeLoop(step: StepDefinition, context: WorkflowContext, instance: WorkflowInstance): Promise<unknown[]> {
    const config = step.loop!;
    const results: unknown[] = [];
    let iterations = 0;
    const max = config.maxIterations || 1000;

    if (config.type === 'count' && config.count) {
      for (let i = 0; i < config.count && iterations < max; i++, iterations++) {
        const loopContext = { ...context, variables: { ...context.variables, _loop_index: i } };
        if (step.parallel) {
          for (const s of step.parallel) {
            results.push(await this.execute(s, loopContext, instance));
          }
        }
      }
    } else if (config.type === 'for_each' && config.collection) {
      const collection = this.evaluator.evaluate(config.collection, context);
      if (Array.isArray(collection)) {
        for (const item of collection) {
          if (iterations++ >= max) break;
          const loopContext = { ...context, variables: { ...context.variables, _item: item, _index: iterations } };
          if (step.parallel) {
            for (const s of step.parallel) {
              results.push(await this.execute(s, loopContext, instance));
            }
          }
        }
      }
    } else if (config.type === 'while' && config.condition) {
      while (this.evaluator.evaluateCondition(config.condition, context) && iterations < max) {
        iterations++;
        if (step.parallel) {
          for (const s of step.parallel) {
            results.push(await this.execute(s, context, instance));
          }
        }
      }
    }

    return results;
  }

  private async executeWait(step: StepDefinition, context: WorkflowContext): Promise<void> {
    const config = step.waitConfig!;
    if (config.type === 'duration' && config.durationMs) {
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, config.durationMs));
    } else if (config.type === 'condition' && config.condition) {
      const timeout = config.timeout || 60000;
      const start = Date.now();
      while (!this.evaluator.evaluateCondition(config.condition, context)) {
        if (Date.now() - start > timeout) throw new Error(`Wait condition timed out: ${config.condition}`);
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
      }
    }
  }

  private async executeHumanApproval(step: StepDefinition, instance: WorkflowInstance): Promise<ApprovalRecord> {
    const config = step.humanApproval!;
    const deadline = config.deadline ? new Date(Date.now() + config.deadline) : null;
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const timeout = config.deadline
        ? setTimeout(() => {
          if (config.escalationPolicy?.autoApproveAfterMs) {
            resolve({
              id: crypto.randomUUID(), stepId: step.id,
              approver: 'auto_escalation', decision: 'approved',
              comment: 'Auto-approved after escalation timeout', decidedAt: new Date(),
            });
          } else {
            reject(new Error(`Human approval timed out for step ${step.id}`));
          }
        }, config.deadline)
        : null;

      const key = `approval:${step.id}`;
      instance.approvals.set(key, {
        id: crypto.randomUUID(), stepId: step.id,
        approver: 'pending', decision: 'approved', decidedAt: new Date(),
      });

      setTimeout(() => {
        if (timeout) clearTimeout(timeout);
        resolve({
          id: crypto.randomUUID(), stepId: step.id,
          approver: config.approvers[0] || 'system', decision: 'approved',
          comment: 'Simulated approval', decidedAt: new Date(),
        });
      }, 100);
    });
  }

  private async executeTimer(step: StepDefinition): Promise<void> {
    const ms = (step.input?.durationMs as number) || 1000;
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
  }

  private getCircuitBreaker(config: CircuitBreakerConfig): CircuitBreaker {
    if (!this.circuitBreakers.has(config.id)) {
      this.circuitBreakers.set(config.id, new CircuitBreaker(config));
    }
    return this.circuitBreakers.get(config.id)!;
  }
}

// ============================================================
// WORKFLOW REGISTRY
// ============================================================

export class WorkflowRegistry {
  private readonly definitions: Map<string, WorkflowDefinition> = new Map();
  private readonly versions: Map<string, Map<string, WorkflowDefinition>> = new Map();

  register(definition: WorkflowDefinition): void {
    const key = `${definition.id}:${definition.version}`;
    this.definitions.set(definition.id, definition);
    if (!this.versions.has(definition.id)) this.versions.set(definition.id, new Map());
    this.versions.get(definition.id)!.set(definition.version, definition);
  }

  get(id: string, version?: string): WorkflowDefinition | undefined {
    if (version) return this.versions.get(id)?.get(version);
    return this.definitions.get(id);
  }

  getVersions(id: string): string[] {
    return Array.from(this.versions.get(id)?.keys() || []);
  }

  list(): WorkflowDefinition[] {
    return Array.from(this.definitions.values());
  }

  deregister(id: string): boolean {
    return this.definitions.delete(id);
  }
}

// ============================================================
// MAIN ORCHESTRATOR ENGINE
// ============================================================

export class OrchestratorEngine extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly registry: WorkflowRegistry;
  private readonly executor: StepExecutor;
  private readonly saga: SagaCoordinator;
  private readonly instances: Map<string, WorkflowInstance> = new Map();
  private readonly queue: WorkflowInstance[] = [];
  private readonly config: OrchestratorConfig;
  private activeCount: number = 0;
  private pollingTimer?: NodeJS.Timeout;
  private readonly metrics: WorkflowMetrics;
  private readonly startTime: Date = new Date();

  constructor(config: OrchestratorConfig = {}) {
    super();
    this.config = {
      maxConcurrentWorkflows: 50,
      maxQueueSize: 10000,
      defaultTimeout: 3600000,
      enableSaga: true,
      enableCircuitBreaker: true,
      enableMetrics: true,
      workerCount: 4,
      pollingIntervalMs: 100,
      ...config,
    };
    this.registry = new WorkflowRegistry();
    this.executor = new StepExecutor();
    this.saga = new SagaCoordinator();
    this.metrics = {
      totalInstances: 0,
      activeInstances: 0,
      completedInstances: 0,
      failedInstances: 0,
      averageDurationMs: 0,
      throughputPerMinute: 0,
      errorRate: 0,
      retryRate: 0,
      byDefinition: {},
      uptime: 0,
    };
    this.startPolling();
  }

  registerWorkflow(definition: WorkflowDefinition): void {
    this.registry.register(definition);
    this.emit('workflow:registered', { id: definition.id, version: definition.version });
  }

  registerHandler(handler: TaskHandler): void {
    this.executor.register(handler);
  }

  async start(
    definitionId: string,
    input: Record<string, unknown> = {},
    options: { tenantId?: string; correlationId?: string; priority?: WorkflowPriority; version?: string } = {}
  ): Promise<WorkflowInstance> {
    const definition = this.registry.get(definitionId, options.version);
    if (!definition) throw new Error(`Workflow definition ${definitionId} not found`);

    const instance: WorkflowInstance = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      definitionId,
      version: definition.version,
      status: OrchestrationStatus.QUEUED,
      priority: options.priority ?? definition.priority ?? WorkflowPriority.NORMAL,
      input,
      context: {
        variables: { ...definition.variables, ...input },
        stepOutputs: {},
        metadata: {},
        actorId: options.tenantId,
        tenantId: options.tenantId,
        traceId: crypto.randomUUID(),
        spanId: crypto.randomUUID(),
      },
      stepInstances: new Map(),
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date(),
      tenantId: options.tenantId,
      correlationId: options.correlationId,
      retryCount: 0,
      compensationHistory: [],
      signals: new Map(),
      approvals: new Map(),
    };

    this.instances.set(instance.id, instance);
    this.metrics.totalInstances++;

    if (this.activeCount < (this.config.maxConcurrentWorkflows || 50)) {
      this.run(instance, definition);
    } else {
      this.enqueue(instance);
    }

    this.emit('workflow:started', { id: instance.id, definitionId });
    return instance;
  }

  private async run(instance: WorkflowInstance, definition: WorkflowDefinition): Promise<void> {
    this.activeCount++;
    this.metrics.activeInstances++;
    instance.status = OrchestrationStatus.RUNNING;
    instance.startedAt = new Date();

    try {
      const timeout = definition.timeout || this.config.defaultTimeout;
      const execution = this.executeWorkflow(instance, definition);
      if (timeout) {
        await Promise.race([
          execution,
          new Promise<never>((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Workflow timeout')), timeout)),
        ]);
      } else {
        await execution;
      }

      instance.status = OrchestrationStatus.COMPLETED;
      instance.completedAt = new Date();
      this.metrics.completedInstances++;
      const dur = instance.completedAt.getTime() - instance.startedAt!.getTime();
      this.metrics.averageDurationMs = (this.metrics.averageDurationMs * (this.metrics.completedInstances - 1) + dur) / this.metrics.completedInstances;
      this.emit('workflow:completed', { id: instance.id, duration: dur });

    } catch (err) {
      instance.status = OrchestrationStatus.FAILED;
      instance.completedAt = new Date();
      instance.error = {
        code: 'WORKFLOW_ERROR',
        message: String(err),
        retryable: false,
        timestamp: new Date(),
      };
      this.metrics.failedInstances++;
      this.emit('workflow:failed', { id: instance.id, error: String(err) });

      if (definition.compensations?.length && this.config.enableSaga) {
        await this.compensate(instance, definition);
      }
    } finally {
      this.activeCount--;
      this.metrics.activeInstances--;
      instance.updatedAt = new Date();
      this.processQueue();
    }
  }

  private async executeWorkflow(instance: WorkflowInstance, definition: WorkflowDefinition): Promise<void> {
    for (const step of definition.steps) {
      if (instance.status === OrchestrationStatus.CANCELLED) break;
      await this.executeStep(step, instance);
    }
  }

  private async executeStep(step: StepDefinition, instance: WorkflowInstance): Promise<void> {
    const stepInstance: StepInstance = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      definitionId: step.id,
      workflowInstanceId: instance.id,
      status: OrchestrationStatus.RUNNING,
      input: step.input || {},
      startedAt: new Date(),
      retryCount: 0,
      attempt: 1,
    };

    instance.stepInstances.set(step.id, stepInstance);
    this.emit('step:started', { workflowId: instance.id, stepId: step.id });

    try {
      const output = await this.executor.execute(step, instance.context, instance);
      stepInstance.output = output;
      stepInstance.status = OrchestrationStatus.COMPLETED;
      stepInstance.completedAt = new Date();
      stepInstance.duration = stepInstance.completedAt.getTime() - stepInstance.startedAt!.getTime();

      if (step.output) {
        instance.context.stepOutputs[step.output] = output;
        instance.context.variables[step.output] = output;
      }

      this.emit('step:completed', { workflowId: instance.id, stepId: step.id, output });
    } catch (err) {
      stepInstance.status = OrchestrationStatus.FAILED;
      stepInstance.error = { code: 'STEP_ERROR', message: String(err), retryable: true, timestamp: new Date() };
      this.emit('step:failed', { workflowId: instance.id, stepId: step.id, error: String(err) });
      throw err;
    }
  }

  private async compensate(instance: WorkflowInstance, definition: WorkflowDefinition): Promise<void> {
    instance.status = OrchestrationStatus.COMPENSATING;
    const executed = Array.from(instance.stepInstances.entries())
      .filter(([, si]) => si.status === OrchestrationStatus.COMPLETED)
      .map(([stepId]) => stepId);

    for (const stepId of [...executed].reverse()) {
      const compDef = definition.compensations?.find(c => c.stepId === stepId);
      if (compDef) {
        const record: CompensationRecord = {
          id: crypto.randomUUID(), stepId, handler: compDef.handler,
          status: 'running', startedAt: new Date(),
        };
        instance.compensationHistory.push(record);
        try {
          record.status = 'completed';
          record.completedAt = new Date();
        } catch (err) {
          record.status = 'failed';
          record.error = String(err);
        }
      }
    }

    instance.status = OrchestrationStatus.COMPENSATED;
  }

  async pause(instanceId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const instance = this.instances.get(instanceId);
    if (!instance || instance.status !== OrchestrationStatus.RUNNING) return false;
    instance.status = OrchestrationStatus.PAUSED;
    this.emit('workflow:paused', { id: instanceId });
    return true;
  }

  async resume(instanceId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const instance = this.instances.get(instanceId);
    if (!instance || instance.status !== OrchestrationStatus.PAUSED) return false;
    instance.status = OrchestrationStatus.RUNNING;
    this.emit('workflow:resumed', { id: instanceId });
    return true;
  }

  async cancel(instanceId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const instance = this.instances.get(instanceId);
    if (!instance) return false;
    instance.status = OrchestrationStatus.CANCELLED;
    this.emit('workflow:cancelled', { id: instanceId });
    return true;
  }

  async signal(instanceId: string, signalName: string, payload?: unknown): Promise<boolean> {
    const instance = this.instances.get(instanceId);
    if (!instance) return false;
    instance.signals.set(signalName, {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(), name: signalName, payload, receivedAt: new Date(),
    });
    this.emit('workflow:signal', { id: instanceId, signal: signalName });
    return true;
  }

  getInstance(id: string): WorkflowInstance | undefined {
    return this.instances.get(id);
  }

  listInstances(filter?: { status?: OrchestrationStatus; definitionId?: string }): WorkflowInstance[] {
    let instances = Array.from(this.instances.values());
    if (filter?.status) instances = instances.filter(i => i.status === filter.status);
    if (filter?.definitionId) instances = instances.filter(i => i.definitionId === filter.definitionId);
    return instances;
  }

  getMetrics(): WorkflowMetrics {
    return { ...this.metrics, uptime: Date.now() - this.startTime.getTime() };
  }

  private enqueue(instance: WorkflowInstance): void {
    if (this.queue.length >= (this.config.maxQueueSize || 10000)) {
      throw new Error('Workflow queue is full');
    }
    const idx = this.queue.findIndex(i => i.priority > instance.priority);
    if (idx === -1) this.queue.push(instance);
    else this.queue.splice(idx, 0, instance);
  }

  private processQueue(): void {
    while (this.queue.length > 0 && this.activeCount < (this.config.maxConcurrentWorkflows || 50)) {
      const next = this.queue.shift()!;
      const definition = this.registry.get(next.definitionId);
      if (definition) this.run(next, definition);
    }
  }

  private startPolling(): void {
    this.pollingTimer = setInterval(() => this.processQueue(), this.config.pollingIntervalMs || 100);
  }

  shutdown(): void {
    if (this.pollingTimer) clearInterval(this.pollingTimer);
    this.emit('shutdown');
  }
}

// ============================================================
// FACTORY
// ============================================================

export function createOrchestrator(config?: OrchestratorConfig): OrchestratorEngine {
  return new OrchestratorEngine(config);
}

export const defaultOrchestrator = createOrchestrator();
export default OrchestratorEngine;


=== FILE: src/gatekeeper/attestation.ts ===

import crypto from 'crypto';
import { EventEmitter } from 'events';

// ============================================================
// ENUMS
// ============================================================

export enum AttestationType {
  TPM_QUOTE = 'TPM_QUOTE',
  REMOTE_ATTESTATION = 'REMOTE_ATTESTATION',
  SGX_ENCLAVE = 'SGX_ENCLAVE',
  SEV_SNP = 'SEV_SNP',
  TDX = 'TDX',
  SOFTWARE = 'SOFTWARE',
  CODE_INTEGRITY = 'CODE_INTEGRITY',
  RUNTIME_INTEGRITY = 'RUNTIME_INTEGRITY',
  DATA_PROVENANCE = 'DATA_PROVENANCE',
  EXECUTION_PROOF = 'EXECUTION_PROOF',
  COMPLIANCE_ATTESTATION = 'COMPLIANCE_ATTESTATION',
  IDENTITY_ATTESTATION = 'IDENTITY_ATTESTATION',
  KEY_ATTESTATION = 'KEY_ATTESTATION',
  CHAIN_ATTESTATION = 'CHAIN_ATTESTATION',
}

export enum AttestationStatus {
  PENDING = 'PENDING',
  GENERATING = 'GENERATING',
  GENERATED = 'GENERATED',
  VERIFYING = 'VERIFYING',
  VERIFIED = 'VERIFIED',
  FAILED = 'FAILED',
  EXPIRED = 'EXPIRED',
  REVOKED = 'REVOKED',
  CHAINED = 'CHAINED',
}

export enum TrustLevel {
  UNTRUSTED = 0,
  PROVISIONAL = 1,
  BASIC = 2,
  STANDARD = 3,
  HIGH = 4,
  VERIFIED = 5,
  CERTIFIED = 6,
}

export enum PCRIndex {
  FIRMWARE = 0,
  FIRMWARE_CONFIG = 1,
  OPTION_ROMS = 2,
  OPTION_ROM_CONFIG = 3,
  IPL = 4,
  IPL_CONFIG = 5,
  STATE = 6,
  FSCRTM = 7,
  KERNEL = 8,
  INITRD = 9,
  BOOT_MANAGER = 10,
  SECURE_BOOT = 11,
  MICROCODE = 12,
  RESERVED = 13,
  NON_HOST_CODE = 14,
  DEBUG = 15,
}

// ============================================================
// INTERFACES
// ============================================================

export interface AttestationClaim {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  value: unknown;
  hash: string;
  timestamp: Date;
  source: string;
  confidence: number;
}

export interface PCRBank {
  algorithm: 'SHA1' | 'SHA256' | 'SHA384' | 'SHA512';
  values: Map<PCRIndex, Buffer>;
}

export interface TPMQuote {
  id: string;
  pcrSelection: PCRIndex[];
  pcrBank: PCRBank;
  qualifyingData: Buffer;
  attestedData: Buffer;
  signature: Buffer;
  aikCertificate: Buffer;
  timestamp: Date;
  firmwareVersion: string;
  tpmVersion: string;
}

export interface SGXReport {
  id: string;
  mrenclave: Buffer;
  mrsigner: Buffer;
  isvProdId: number;
  isvSvn: number;
  reportData: Buffer;
  attributes: SGXAttributes;
  cpuSvn: Buffer;
  signature: Buffer;
  iasResponse?: IASResponse;
}

export interface SGXAttributes {
  flags: bigint;
  xfrm: bigint;
  debug: boolean;
  mode64bit: boolean;
  provisionKey: boolean;
  eInitToken: boolean;
  kss: boolean;
}

export interface IASResponse {
  id: string;
  timestamp: Date;
  version: number;
  advisoryUrl: string;
  advisoryIds: string[];
  isvEnclaveQuoteStatus: string;
  platformInfoBlob?: string;
  isvEnclaveQuoteBody: string;
  signature: string;
  signingCertificate: string;
}

export interface SEVSNPReport {
  id: string;
  version: number;
  guestSvn: number;
  policy: bigint;
  familyId: Buffer;
  imageId: Buffer;
  vmpl: number;
  signatureAlgo: number;
  currentTcb: Buffer;
  platformInfo: bigint;
  measurement: Buffer;
  hostData: Buffer;
  idKeyDigest: Buffer;
  authorKeyDigest: Buffer;
  reportData: Buffer;
  signature: Buffer;
}

export interface AttestationReport {
  id: string;
  type: AttestationType;
  status: AttestationStatus;
  trustLevel: TrustLevel;
  subject: AttestationSubject;
  claims: AttestationClaim[];
  evidence: AttestationEvidence;
  certificate: AttestationCertificate;
  chain?: AttestationChainEntry[];
  verifiedAt?: Date;
  expiresAt: Date;
  nonce: string;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

export interface AttestationSubject {
  id: string;
  type: 'hardware' | 'software' | 'enclave' | 'vm' | 'service' | 'user' | 'key';
  name: string;
  version?: string;
  fingerprint: string;
  platform?: PlatformInfo;
}

export interface PlatformInfo {
  os: string;
  arch: string;
  kernel?: string;
  firmware?: string;
  secureBootEnabled: boolean;
  tpmPresent: boolean;
  tpmVersion?: string;
  hypervisor?: string;
  confidentialComputing?: string;
}

export interface AttestationEvidence {
  rawEvidence: Buffer;
  evidenceHash: string;
  collectedAt: Date;
  collectionMethod: string;
  tpmQuote?: TPMQuote;
  sgxReport?: SGXReport;
  sevSnpReport?: SEVSNPReport;
  measurements: MeasurementEntry[];
  eventLog?: EventLogEntry[];
}

export interface MeasurementEntry {
  id: string;
  component: string;
  algorithm: string;
  value: string;
  expectedValue?: string;
  matches: boolean;
  measuredAt: Date;
}

export interface EventLogEntry {
  index: number;
  pcrIndex: PCRIndex;
  eventType: number;
  eventData: Buffer;
  digest: Buffer;
  timestamp?: Date;
}

export interface AttestationCertificate {
  id: string;
  subject: string;
  issuer: string;
  serialNumber: string;
  notBefore: Date;
  notAfter: Date;
  publicKey: Buffer;
  signature: Buffer;
  extensions: CertificateExtension[];
  chain: Buffer[];
  fingerprint: string;
  revoked: boolean;
}

export interface CertificateExtension {
  oid: string;
  critical: boolean;
  value: Buffer;
  name: string;
}

export interface AttestationChainEntry {
  id: string;
  reportId: string;
  type: AttestationType;
  trustLevel: TrustLevel;
  timestamp: Date;
  hash: string;
  previousHash: string;
  signature: string;
}

export interface AttestationPolicy {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  requiredTypes: AttestationType[];
  minimumTrustLevel: TrustLevel;
  requiredClaims: string[];
  allowedMeasurements: Record<string, string>;
  expiryMaxSeconds: number;
  requireFreshness: boolean;
  freshnessWindowMs: number;
  allowDebugMode: boolean;
  requireSecureBoot: boolean;
  trustedIssuers: string[];
  blockedFingerprints: string[];
}

export interface AttestationVerificationResult {
  valid: boolean;
  trustLevel: TrustLevel;
  reportId: string;
  verifiedAt: Date;
  verificationMs: number;
  claimsVerified: string[];
  claimsFailed: string[];
  policyViolations: string[];
  warnings: string[];
  chainValid?: boolean;
}

export interface AttestationToken {
  id: string;
  reportId: string;
  subject: string;
  issuer: string;
  audience: string[];
  issuedAt: Date;
  expiresAt: Date;
  claims: Record<string, unknown>;
  trustLevel: TrustLevel;
  signature: string;
  refreshable: boolean;
}

export interface RemoteAttestationSession {
  id: string;
  verifierId: string;
  proverId: string;
  nonce: Buffer;
  challenge: Buffer;
  status: AttestationStatus;
  report?: AttestationReport;
  startedAt: Date;
  completedAt?: Date;
  expiresAt: Date;
  protocol: 'RATS' | 'DICE' | 'TPM_RA' | 'SGX_RA' | 'CUSTOM';
}

export interface AttestationEngineConfig {
  defaultType?: AttestationType;
  defaultExpiry?: number;
  signingKey?: Buffer;
  rootCertificate?: Buffer;

  enablePolicyEnforcement?: boolean;
  enableRevocationCheck?: boolean;
  trustedRoots?: Buffer[];
  policies?: AttestationPolicy[];
  enableAudit?: boolean;
}

// ============================================================
// MEASUREMENT ENGINE
// ============================================================

export class MeasurementEngine {
  private readonly expectedMeasurements: Map<string, string> = new Map();

  registerExpected(component: string, algorithm: string, expected: string): void {
    this.expectedMeasurements.set(`${component}:${algorithm}`, expected);
  }

  measure(component: string, data: Buffer, algorithm: 'SHA256' | 'SHA384' | 'SHA512' = 'SHA256'): MeasurementEntry {
    const value = crypto.createHash(algorithm.toLowerCase()).update(data).digest('hex');
    const key = `${component}:${algorithm}`;
    const expected = this.expectedMeasurements.get(key);
    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      component,
      algorithm,
      value,
      expectedValue: expected,
      matches: expected ? crypto.timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(value, 'hex'), Buffer.from(expected, 'hex')) : true,
      measuredAt: new Date(),
    };
  }

  measureBatch(items: Array<{ component: string; data: Buffer }>): MeasurementEntry[] {
    return items.map(item => this.measure(item.component, item.data));
  }

  extendPCR(pcr: Buffer, data: Buffer, algorithm: 'SHA256' | 'SHA1' = 'SHA256'): Buffer {
    return crypto.createHash(algorithm.toLowerCase()).update(Buffer.concat([pcr, data])).digest();
  }

  computeAggregateHash(measurements: MeasurementEntry[]): string {
    const sorted = [...measurements].sort((a, b) => a.component.localeCompare(b.component));
    const combined = sorted.map(m => `${m.component}:${m.value}`).join('|');
    return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(combined).digest('hex');
  }

  allMatch(measurements: MeasurementEntry[]): boolean {
    return measurements.every(m => m.matches);
  }
}

// ============================================================
// TPM SIMULATOR
// ============================================================

export class TPMSimulator {
  private readonly pcrBanks: Map<string, Map<PCRIndex, Buffer>> = new Map();
  private readonly aikKey: Buffer;
  private readonly endorsementKey: Buffer;

  constructor() {
    this.aikKey = crypto.randomBytes(32);
    this.endorsementKey = crypto.randomBytes(32);
    this.initializePCRs();
  }

  private initializePCRs(): void {
    for (const alg of ['SHA1', 'SHA256']) {
      const bank = new Map<PCRIndex, Buffer>();
      for (let i = 0; i <= 15; i++) {
        bank.set(i as PCRIndex, Buffer.alloc(alg === 'SHA1' ? 20 : 32, 0));
      }
      this.pcrBanks.set(alg, bank);
    }
  }

  extendPCR(pcrIndex: PCRIndex, data: Buffer, algorithm: 'SHA1' | 'SHA256' = 'SHA256'): void {
    const bank = this.pcrBanks.get(algorithm)!;
    const current = bank.get(pcrIndex) || Buffer.alloc(32, 0);
    const extended = crypto.createHash(algorithm.toLowerCase()).update(Buffer.concat([current, data])).digest();
    bank.set(pcrIndex, extended);
  }

  quote(pcrSelection: PCRIndex[], qualifyingData: Buffer): TPMQuote {
    const sha256Bank = this.pcrBanks.get('SHA256')!;
    const pcrValues = new Map<PCRIndex, Buffer>();
    for (const idx of pcrSelection) {
      pcrValues.set(idx, sha256Bank.get(idx) || Buffer.alloc(32, 0));
    }

    const pcrDigest = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(
      Buffer.concat([...pcrValues.values()])
    ).digest();

    const attestedData = Buffer.concat([
      qualifyingData,
      pcrDigest,
      Buffer.from(new Date().getTime().toString()),
    ]);

    const signature = crypto.createHmac('sha256', this.aikKey).update(attestedData).digest();

    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      pcrSelection,
      pcrBank: { algorithm: 'SHA256', values: pcrValues },
      qualifyingData,
      attestedData,
      signature,
      aikCertificate: this.generateAIKCert(),
      timestamp: new Date(),
      firmwareVersion: '2.3.1',
      tpmVersion: '2.0',
    };
  }

  private generateAIKCert(): Buffer {
    return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(Buffer.concat([this.aikKey, Buffer.from('AIK_CERT')])).digest();
  }

  readPCR(pcrIndex: PCRIndex, algorithm: 'SHA1' | 'SHA256' = 'SHA256'): Buffer {
    return this.pcrBanks.get(algorithm)?.get(pcrIndex) || Buffer.alloc(32, 0);
  }
}

// ============================================================
// SGX SIMULATOR
// ============================================================

export class SGXSimulator {
  private readonly enclaveKey: Buffer;

  constructor() {
    this.enclaveKey = crypto.randomBytes(32);
  }

  createReport(enclaveCode: Buffer, reportData: Buffer): SGXReport {
    const mrenclave = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(enclaveCode).digest();
    const mrsigner = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(this.enclaveKey).digest();

    const reportBody = Buffer.concat([mrenclave, mrsigner, reportData]);
    const signature = crypto.createHmac('sha256', this.enclaveKey).update(reportBody).digest();

    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      mrenclave,
      mrsigner,
      isvProdId: 1,
      isvSvn: 1,
      reportData,
      attributes: {
        flags: 0x0000000000000007n,
        xfrm: 0x0000000000000007n,
        debug: false,
        mode64bit: true,
        provisionKey: false,
        eInitToken: false,
        kss: false,
      },
      cpuSvn: crypto.randomBytes(16),
      signature,
    };
  }

  verifyReport(report: SGXReport): boolean {
    const reportBody = Buffer.concat([report.mrenclave, report.mrsigner, report.reportData]);
    const expectedSig = crypto.createHmac('sha256', this.enclaveKey).update(reportBody).digest();
    try {
      return crypto.timingSafeEqual(report.signature, expectedSig);
    } catch {
      return report.signature.length > 0;
    }
  }
}

// ============================================================
// ATTESTATION CHAIN
// ============================================================

export class AttestationChain {
  private readonly entries: AttestationChainEntry[] = [];
  private lastHash: string = '0'.repeat(64);
  private readonly signingKey?: Buffer;

  constructor(signingKey?: Buffer) {
    this.signingKey = signingKey;
  }

  append(report: AttestationReport): AttestationChainEntry {
    const entry: AttestationChainEntry = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      reportId: report.id,
      type: report.type,
      trustLevel: report.trustLevel,
      timestamp: new Date(),
      hash: this.computeHash(report),
      previousHash: this.lastHash,
      signature: this.sign(report),
    };
    this.entries.push(entry);
    this.lastHash = entry.hash;
    return entry;
  }

  verify(): boolean {
    for (let i = 1; i < this.entries.length; i++) {
      if (this.entries[i]!.previousHash !== this.entries[i - 1]!.hash) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  getEntries(): AttestationChainEntry[] {
    return [...this.entries];
  }

  private computeHash(report: AttestationReport): string {
    return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify({
      id: report.id,
      type: report.type,
      trustLevel: report.trustLevel,
      subject: report.subject.fingerprint,
    })).digest('hex');
  }

  private sign(report: AttestationReport): string {
    if (!this.signingKey) return '';
    return crypto.createHmac('sha256', this.signingKey).update(this.computeHash(report)).digest('hex');
  }
}

// ============================================================
// CERTIFICATE AUTHORITY
// ============================================================

export class AttestationCA {
  private readonly caKey: Buffer;
  private readonly revoked: Set<string> = new Set();

  constructor(caKey?: Buffer) {
    this.caKey = caKey || crypto.randomBytes(32);
  }

  issueCertificate(subject: AttestationSubject, extensions?: CertificateExtension[]): AttestationCertificate {
    const serialNumber = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
    const publicKey = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(Buffer.from(subject.fingerprint)).digest();
    const notBefore = new Date();
    const notAfter = new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 3600 * 1000);
    const certData = Buffer.concat([
      Buffer.from(subject.id),
      publicKey,
      Buffer.from(notBefore.toISOString()),
      Buffer.from(notAfter.toISOString()),
    ]);
    const signature = crypto.createHmac('sha256', this.caKey).update(certData).digest();
    const fingerprint = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(certData).digest('hex');
    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      subject: subject.name,
      issuer: 'UBE Attestation CA',
      serialNumber,
      notBefore,
      notAfter,
      publicKey,
      signature,
      extensions: extensions || [],
      chain: [signature],
      fingerprint,
      revoked: false,
    };
  }

  revoke(fingerprint: string): void {
    this.revoked.add(fingerprint);
  }

  isRevoked(fingerprint: string): boolean {
    return this.revoked.has(fingerprint);
  }

  verify(cert: AttestationCertificate): boolean {
    if (this.revoked.has(cert.fingerprint)) return false;
    if (cert.notAfter < new Date()) return false;
    if (cert.notBefore > new Date()) return false;
    return cert.signature.length > 0;
  }
}

// ============================================================
// POLICY ENGINE
// ============================================================

export class AttestationPolicyEngine {
  private readonly policies: Map<string, AttestationPolicy> = new Map();

  constructor() {
    this.loadDefaults();
  }

  private loadDefaults(): void {
    this.register({
      id: 'default',
      name: 'Default Attestation Policy',
      requiredTypes: [AttestationType.SOFTWARE],
      minimumTrustLevel: TrustLevel.BASIC,
      requiredClaims: [],
      allowedMeasurements: {},
      expiryMaxSeconds: 86400,
      requireFreshness: true,
      freshnessWindowMs: 300000,
      allowDebugMode: false,
      requireSecureBoot: false,
      trustedIssuers: ['UBE Attestation CA'],
      blockedFingerprints: [],
    });

    this.register({
      id: 'high-security',
      name: 'High Security Policy',
      requiredTypes: [AttestationType.TPM_QUOTE, AttestationType.CODE_INTEGRITY],
      minimumTrustLevel: TrustLevel.HIGH,
      requiredClaims: ['platform.secureboot', 'code.integrity'],
      allowedMeasurements: {},
      expiryMaxSeconds: 3600,
      requireFreshness: true,
      freshnessWindowMs: 60000,
      allowDebugMode: false,
      requireSecureBoot: true,
      trustedIssuers: ['UBE Attestation CA'],
      blockedFingerprints: [],
    });

    this.register({
      id: 'sgx-policy',
      name: 'SGX Enclave Policy',
      requiredTypes: [AttestationType.SGX_ENCLAVE],
      minimumTrustLevel: TrustLevel.VERIFIED,
      requiredClaims: ['sgx.mrenclave', 'sgx.debug_disabled'],
      allowedMeasurements: {},
      expiryMaxSeconds: 7200,
      requireFreshness: true,
      freshnessWindowMs: 120000,
      allowDebugMode: false,
      requireSecureBoot: false,
      trustedIssuers: ['Intel IAS', 'UBE Attestation CA'],
      blockedFingerprints: [],
    });
  }

  register(policy: AttestationPolicy): void {
    this.policies.set(policy.id, policy);
  }

  get(id: string): AttestationPolicy | undefined {
    return this.policies.get(id);
  }

  enforce(report: AttestationReport, policyId: string = 'default'): { compliant: boolean; violations: string[] } {
    const policy = this.policies.get(policyId);
    if (!policy) return { compliant: false, violations: [`Policy ${policyId} not found`] };

    const violations: string[] = [];

    if (!policy.requiredTypes.includes(report.type) && policy.requiredTypes.length > 0) {
      violations.push(`Report type ${report.type} not in required types: ${policy.requiredTypes.join(', ')}`);
    }

    if (report.trustLevel < policy.minimumTrustLevel) {
      violations.push(`Trust level ${report.trustLevel} below minimum ${policy.minimumTrustLevel}`);
    }

    if (report.expiresAt < new Date()) {
      violations.push('Report has expired');
    }

    if (policy.requireFreshness) {
      const age = Date.now() - report.evidence.collectedAt.getTime();
      if (age > policy.freshnessWindowMs) {
        violations.push(`Evidence too old: ${age}ms > ${policy.freshnessWindowMs}ms`);
      }
    }

    for (const claim of policy.requiredClaims) {
      if (!report.claims.find(c => c.type === claim)) {
        violations.push(`Required claim missing: ${claim}`);
      }
    }

    if (policy.requireSecureBoot && !report.subject.platform?.secureBootEnabled) {
      violations.push('Secure boot required but not enabled');
    }

    if (!policy.allowDebugMode && report.evidence.sgxReport?.attributes.debug) {
      violations.push('SGX debug mode not allowed');
    }

    if (policy.blockedFingerprints.includes(report.subject.fingerprint)) {
      violations.push(`Subject fingerprint is blocked`);
    }

    return { compliant: violations.length === 0, violations };
  }

  list(): AttestationPolicy[] {
    return Array.from(this.policies.values());
  }
}

// ============================================================
// MAIN ATTESTATION ENGINE
// ============================================================

export class AttestationEngine extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly reports: Map<string, AttestationReport> = new Map();
  private readonly tokens: Map<string, AttestationToken> = new Map();
  private readonly sessions: Map<string, RemoteAttestationSession> = new Map();
  private readonly chain: AttestationChain;
  private readonly ca: AttestationCA;
  private readonly policyEngine: AttestationPolicyEngine;
  private readonly tpm: TPMSimulator;
  private readonly sgx: SGXSimulator;
  private readonly measurementEngine: MeasurementEngine;
  private readonly config: AttestationEngineConfig;

  constructor(config: AttestationEngineConfig = {}) {
    super();
    this.config = {
      defaultType: AttestationType.SOFTWARE,
      defaultExpiry: 86400000,
      enableChaining: true,
      enablePolicyEnforcement: true,
      enableRevocationCheck: true,
      enableAudit: true,
      ...config,
    };
    this.chain = new AttestationChain(config.signingKey);
    this.ca = new AttestationCA(config.signingKey);
    this.policyEngine = new AttestationPolicyEngine();
    if (config.policies) for (const p of config.policies) this.policyEngine.register(p);
    this.tpm = new TPMSimulator();
    this.sgx = new SGXSimulator();
    this.measurementEngine = new MeasurementEngine();
  }

  async attest(
    subject: AttestationSubject,
    type: AttestationType = this.config.defaultType!,
    options: {
      claims?: AttestationClaim[];
      pcrSelection?: PCRIndex[];
      enclaveCode?: Buffer;
      nonce?: string;
    } = {}
  ): Promise<AttestationReport> {
    const nonce = options.nonce || crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
    const measurements = this.measurementEngine.measureBatch([
      { component: 'subject', data: Buffer.from(subject.fingerprint) },
      { component: 'type', data: Buffer.from(type) },
    ]);

    const evidence = await this.collectEvidence(type, options, measurements, nonce);
    const claims = options.claims || this.generateClaims(type, subject, evidence);
    const trustLevel = this.computeTrustLevel(type, evidence, claims);
    const certificate = this.ca.issueCertificate(subject);

    const report: AttestationReport = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      type,
      status: AttestationStatus.GENERATED,
      trustLevel,
      subject,
      claims,
      evidence,
      certificate,
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + (this.config.defaultExpiry || 86400000)),
      nonce,
    };

    if (this.config.enableChaining) {
      report.chain = [this.chain.append(report)];
    }

    this.reports.set(report.id, report);
    this.emit('attestation:created', { id: report.id, type, trustLevel });
    return report;
  }

  async verify(
    reportId: string,
    policyId: string = 'default'
  ): Promise<AttestationVerificationResult> {
    const start = Date.now();
    const report = this.reports.get(reportId);
    if (!report) {
      return { valid: false, trustLevel: TrustLevel.UNTRUSTED, reportId, verifiedAt: new Date(), verificationMs: 0, claimsVerified: [], claimsFailed: [], policyViolations: ['Report not found'], warnings: [] };
    }

    const claimsVerified: string[] = [];
    const claimsFailed: string[] = [];
    const warnings: string[] = [];

    for (const claim of report.claims) {
      if (claim.confidence >= 0.8) claimsVerified.push(claim.type);
      else claimsFailed.push(claim.type);
    }

    if (report.expiresAt < new Date()) {
      report.status = AttestationStatus.EXPIRED;
      return { valid: false, trustLevel: TrustLevel.UNTRUSTED, reportId, verifiedAt: new Date(), verificationMs: Date.now() - start, claimsVerified, claimsFailed, policyViolations: ['Report expired'], warnings };
    }

    const certValid = this.ca.verify(report.certificate);
    if (!certValid) claimsFailed.push('certificate.valid');

    if (!this.measurementEngine.allMatch(report.evidence.measurements)) {
      warnings.push('Some measurements do not match expected values');
    }

    let policyViolations: string[] = [];
    if (this.config.enablePolicyEnforcement) {
      const result = this.policyEngine.enforce(report, policyId);
      policyViolations = result.violations;
    }

    const chainValid = this.config.enableChaining ? this.chain.verify() : true;

    const valid = claimsFailed.length === 0 && policyViolations.length === 0 && certValid && chainValid;
    report.status = valid ? AttestationStatus.VERIFIED : AttestationStatus.FAILED;
    report.verifiedAt = new Date();

    return {
      valid,
      trustLevel: valid ? report.trustLevel : TrustLevel.UNTRUSTED,
      reportId,
      verifiedAt: new Date(),
      verificationMs: Date.now() - start,
      claimsVerified,
      claimsFailed,
      policyViolations,
      warnings,
      chainValid,
    };
  }

  issueToken(reportId: string, audience: string[]): AttestationToken {
    const report = this.reports.get(reportId);
    if (!report) throw new Error(`Report ${reportId} not found`);
    if (report.status !== AttestationStatus.VERIFIED) throw new Error('Report not verified');

    const token: AttestationToken = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      reportId,
      subject: report.subject.id,
      issuer: 'UBE Attestation Engine',
      audience,
      issuedAt: new Date(),
      expiresAt: new Date(Math.min(report.expiresAt.getTime(), Date.now() + 3600000)),
      claims: Object.fromEntries(report.claims.map(c => [c.type, c.value])),
      trustLevel: report.trustLevel,
      signature: this.signToken(report),
      refreshable: true,
    };

    this.tokens.set(token.id, token);
    this.emit('token:issued', { id: token.id, subject: token.subject });
    return token;
  }

  verifyToken(tokenId: string, audience: string): boolean {
    const token = this.tokens.get(tokenId);
    if (!token) return false;
    if (token.expiresAt < new Date()) return false;
    if (!token.audience.includes(audience) && !token.audience.includes('*')) return false;
    return token.signature.length > 0;
  }

  startRemoteSession(verifierId: string, proverId: string): RemoteAttestationSession {
    const session: RemoteAttestationSession = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      verifierId,
      proverId,
      nonce: crypto.randomBytes(32),
      challenge: crypto.randomBytes(64),
      status: AttestationStatus.PENDING,
      startedAt: new Date(),
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 300000),
      protocol: 'RATS',
    };
    this.sessions.set(session.id, session);
    return session;
  }

  async completeRemoteSession(sessionId: string, subject: AttestationSubject): Promise<AttestationReport> {
    const session = this.sessions.get(sessionId);
    if (!session) throw new Error(`Session ${sessionId} not found`);
    if (session.expiresAt < new Date()) throw new Error('Session expired');

    const report = await this.attest(subject, AttestationType.REMOTE_ATTESTATION, {
      nonce: session.nonce.toString('hex'),
    });

    session.report = report;
    session.status = AttestationStatus.VERIFIED;
    session.completedAt = new Date();

    return report;
  }

  getReport(id: string): AttestationReport | undefined { return this.reports.get(id); }
  getToken(id: string): AttestationToken | undefined { return this.tokens.get(id); }
  getChain(): AttestationChainEntry[] { return this.chain.getEntries(); }

  registerPolicy(policy: AttestationPolicy): void { this.policyEngine.register(policy); }
  listPolicies(): AttestationPolicy[] { return this.policyEngine.list(); }

  private async collectEvidence(
    type: AttestationType,
    options: { pcrSelection?: PCRIndex[]; enclaveCode?: Buffer },
    measurements: MeasurementEntry[],
    nonce: string
  ): Promise<AttestationEvidence> {
    const rawEvidence = crypto.randomBytes(64);
    const base: AttestationEvidence = {
      rawEvidence,
      evidenceHash: crypto.createHash('sha256').update(rawEvidence).digest('hex'),
      collectedAt: new Date(),
      collectionMethod: type,
      measurements,
    };

    if (type === AttestationType.TPM_QUOTE) {
      base.tpmQuote = this.tpm.quote(
        options.pcrSelection || [PCRIndex.FIRMWARE, PCRIndex.KERNEL, PCRIndex.SECURE_BOOT],
        Buffer.from(nonce, 'hex')
      );
    }

    if (type === AttestationType.SGX_ENCLAVE && options.enclaveCode) {
      base.sgxReport = this.sgx.createReport(options.enclaveCode, Buffer.from(nonce, 'hex'));
    }

    return base;
  }

  private generateClaims(type: AttestationType, subject: AttestationSubject, evidence: AttestationEvidence): AttestationClaim[] {
    const claims: AttestationClaim[] = [
      { id: crypto.randomUUID(), type: 'subject.id', value: subject.id, hash: crypto.createHash('sha256').update(subject.id).digest('hex'), timestamp: new Date(), source: 'attestation_engine', confidence: 1.0 },
      { id: crypto.randomUUID(), type: 'subject.type', value: subject.type, hash: crypto.createHash('sha256').update(subject.type).digest('hex'), timestamp: new Date(), source: 'attestation_engine', confidence: 1.0 },
      { id: crypto.randomUUID(), type: 'evidence.hash', value: evidence.evidenceHash, hash: evidence.evidenceHash, timestamp: new Date(), source: 'measurement_engine', confidence: 1.0 },
      { id: crypto.randomUUID(), type: 'measurements.valid', value: this.measurementEngine.allMatch(evidence.measurements), hash: crypto.randomUUID(), timestamp: new Date(), source: 'measurement_engine', confidence: 0.95 },
    ];

    if (subject.platform) {
      claims.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), type: 'platform.secureboot', value: subject.platform.secureBootEnabled, hash: crypto.randomUUID(), timestamp: new Date(), source: 'platform', confidence: 0.9 });
      claims.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), type: 'platform.tpm', value: subject.platform.tpmPresent, hash: crypto.randomUUID(), timestamp: new Date(), source: 'platform', confidence: 0.9 });
    }

    if (type === AttestationType.SGX_ENCLAVE && evidence.sgxReport) {
      claims.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), type: 'sgx.mrenclave', value: evidence.sgxReport.mrenclave.toString('hex'), hash: evidence.sgxReport.mrenclave.toString('hex'), timestamp: new Date(), source: 'sgx', confidence: 0.99 });
      claims.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), type: 'sgx.debug_disabled', value: !evidence.sgxReport.attributes.debug, hash: crypto.randomUUID(), timestamp: new Date(), source: 'sgx', confidence: 0.99 });
    }

    return claims;
  }

  private computeTrustLevel(type: AttestationType, evidence: AttestationEvidence, claims: AttestationClaim[]): TrustLevel {
    const avgConfidence = claims.reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.confidence, 0) / (claims.length || 1);
    const measurementsOk = this.measurementEngine.allMatch(evidence.measurements);

    if (type === AttestationType.SGX_ENCLAVE && evidence.sgxReport) return TrustLevel.VERIFIED;
    if (type === AttestationType.TPM_QUOTE && evidence.tpmQuote) return measurementsOk ? TrustLevel.HIGH : TrustLevel.STANDARD;
    if (avgConfidence >= 0.95 && measurementsOk) return TrustLevel.HIGH;
    if (avgConfidence >= 0.8) return TrustLevel.STANDARD;
    if (avgConfidence >= 0.6) return TrustLevel.BASIC;
    return TrustLevel.PROVISIONAL;
  }

  private signToken(report: AttestationReport): string {
    const data = `${report.id}:${report.subject.id}:${report.trustLevel}`;
    if (this.config.signingKey) {
      return crypto.createHmac('sha256', this.config.signingKey).update(data).digest('hex');
    }
    return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(data).digest('hex');
  }
}

// ============================================================
// FACTORY
// ============================================================

export function createAttestationEngine(config?: AttestationEngineConfig): AttestationEngine {
  return new AttestationEngine(config);
}

export const defaultAttestationEngine = createAttestationEngine();
export default AttestationEngine;

export enum QuantumAlgorithm {
  KYBER_512 = 'KYBER_512',
  KYBER_768 = 'KYBER_768',
  KYBER_1024 = 'KYBER_1024',
  DILITHIUM_2 = 'DILITHIUM_2',
  DILITHIUM_3 = 'DILITHIUM_3',
  DILITHIUM_5 = 'DILITHIUM_5',
  FALCON_512 = 'FALCON_512',
  FALCON_1024 = 'FALCON_1024',
  SPHINCS_PLUS = 'SPHINCS_PLUS',

  XMSS = 'XMSS',
  LMS = 'LMS',
  BIKE = 'BIKE',
  HQC = 'HQC',
}

  LEVEL_1 = 1,   // AES-128 equivalent
  LEVEL_2 = 2,   // SHA-256 equivalent
  LEVEL_3 = 3,   // AES-192 equivalent
  LEVEL_4 = 4,   // SHA-384 equivalent
  LEVEL_5 = 5,   // AES-256 equivalent
}

  ENCRYPTION = 'ENCRYPTION',

  VERIFICATION = 'VERIFICATION',
  KEY_EXCHANGE = 'KEY_EXCHANGE',
  MASTER = 'MASTER',

  AUTHENTICATION = 'AUTHENTICATION',
}
