// ============================================================
// RING POLYNOMIAL ARITHMETIC
// ============================================================

export class RingPolynomial {
  readonly coefficients: bigint[];
  readonly modulus: bigint;
  readonly degree: number;

  constructor(coefficients: bigint[], modulus: bigint, degree: number) {
    this.degree = degree;
    this.modulus = modulus;
    this.coefficients = Array.from({ length: degree }, (_, i) =>
      ((coefficients[i] ?? 0n) % modulus + modulus) % modulus
    );
  }

  static zero(modulus: bigint, degree: number): RingPolynomial {
    return new RingPolynomial(new Array(degree).fill(0n), modulus, degree);
  }

  static random(modulus: bigint, degree: number): RingPolynomial {
    const coeffs = Array.from({ length: degree }, () => {
      const bytes = crypto.randomBytes(8);
      return BigInt('0x' + bytes.toString('hex')) % modulus;
    });
    return new RingPolynomial(coeffs, modulus, degree);
  }

  static ternary(degree: number, modulus: bigint): RingPolynomial {
    const coeffs = Array.from({ length: degree }, () => {
      const r = crypto.randomBytes(1)[0]! % 3;
      return BigInt(r === 2 ? -1 : r);
    });
    return new RingPolynomial(coeffs, modulus, degree);
  }

  add(other: RingPolynomial): RingPolynomial {
    const result = this.coefficients.map((c, i) =>
      (c + (other.coefficients[i] ?? 0n)) % this.modulus
    );
    return new RingPolynomial(result, this.modulus, this.degree);
  }

  sub(other: RingPolynomial): RingPolynomial {
    const result = this.coefficients.map((c, i) =>
      ((c - (other.coefficients[i] ?? 0n)) % this.modulus + this.modulus) % this.modulus
    );
    return new RingPolynomial(result, this.modulus, this.degree);
  }

  mul(other: RingPolynomial): RingPolynomial {
    const result = new Array(this.degree).fill(0n);
    for (let i = 0; i < this.degree; i++) {
      for (let j = 0; j < this.degree; j++) {
        const idx = (i + j) % this.degree;
        const sign = i + j >= this.degree ? -1n : 1n;
        result[idx] = (result[idx] + sign * this.coefficients[i]! * (other.coefficients[j] ?? 0n)) % this.modulus;
      }
    }
    return new RingPolynomial(result.map(c => ((c % this.modulus) + this.modulus) % this.modulus), this.modulus, this.degree);
  }

  mulScalar(scalar: bigint): RingPolynomial {
    return new RingPolynomial(
      this.coefficients.map(c => (c * scalar) % this.modulus),
      this.modulus, this.degree
    );
  }

  negate(): RingPolynomial {
    return new RingPolynomial(
      this.coefficients.map(c => (this.modulus - c) % this.modulus),
      this.modulus, this.degree
    );
  }

  modSwitch(newModulus: bigint): RingPolynomial {
    const scale = newModulus;
    return new RingPolynomial(
      this.coefficients.map(c => (c * scale / this.modulus) % newModulus),
      newModulus, this.degree
    );
  }

  noise(): number {
    const maxCoeff = this.coefficients.reduce((max, c) => {
      const centered = c > this.modulus / 2n ? this.modulus - c : c;
      return centered > max ? centered : max;
    }, 0n);
    return Number(maxCoeff);
  }

  innerProduct(other: RingPolynomial): bigint {
    return this.coefficients.reduce((acc, c, i) =>
      (acc + c * (other.coefficients[i] ?? 0n)) % this.modulus, 0n
    );
  }

  toBuffer(): Buffer {
    const parts = this.coefficients.map(c => {
      const hex = c.toString(16).padStart(16, '0');
      return Buffer.from(hex, 'hex');
    });
    return Buffer.concat(parts);
  }

  clone(): RingPolynomial {
    return new RingPolynomial([...this.coefficients], this.modulus, this.degree);
  }
}

// ============================================================
// BFV SCHEME (Integer operations)
// ============================================================

export class BFVScheme {
  private readonly params: HEParams;
  private readonly q: bigint;
  private readonly t: bigint;
  private readonly n: number;
  private readonly delta: bigint;

  constructor(params: HEParams) {
    this.params = params;
    this.n = params.polyModulusDegree;
    this.q = params.coeffModulus.reduce((acc, v) => acc * v, 1n);
    this.t = params.plainModulus || 1024n;
    this.delta = this.q / this.t;
  }

  generateKeySet(): HEKeySet {
    const s = RingPolynomial.ternary(this.n, this.q);
    const a = RingPolynomial.random(this.q, this.n);
    const e = this.sampleError();
    const pk0 = a.mul(s).negate().add(e);
    const pk1 = a;
    const publicKey: HEPublicKey = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      data: [pk0.coefficients, pk1.coefficients],
      scheme: HEScheme.BFV,
      size: 2 * this.n,
    };
    const secretKey: HESecretKey = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      data: s.coefficients,
      scheme: HEScheme.BFV,
      size: this.n,
    };
    const relinKey = this.generateRelinKey(s);
    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      scheme: HEScheme.BFV,
      publicKey,
      secretKey,
      relinKey,
      createdAt: new Date(),
      params: this.params,
    };
  }

  encrypt(plaintext: bigint[], keySet: HEKeySet): HECiphertext {
    const { publicKey } = keySet;
    const pk0 = new RingPolynomial(publicKey.data[0]!, this.q, this.n);
    const pk1 = new RingPolynomial(publicKey.data[1]!, this.q, this.n);
    const m = new RingPolynomial(plaintext.map(v => v % this.t), this.q, this.n);
    const u = RingPolynomial.ternary(this.n, this.q);
    const e1 = this.sampleError();
    const e2 = this.sampleError();
    const mScaled = m.mulScalar(this.delta);
    const ct0 = pk0.mul(u).add(e1).add(mScaled);
    const ct1 = pk1.mul(u).add(e2);
    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      scheme: HEScheme.BFV,
      poly0: ct0.coefficients,
      poly1: ct1.coefficients,
      level: this.params.depth,
      noiseLevel: NoiseLevel.LOW,
      dataType: HEDataType.INTEGER,
      slotCount: this.n,
      encryptedAt: new Date(),
      keyId: keySet.id,
      operationHistory: [],
    };
  }

  decrypt(ciphertext: HECiphertext, keySet: HEKeySet): bigint[] {
    const { secretKey } = keySet;
    const s = new RingPolynomial(secretKey.data, this.q, this.n);
    const ct0 = new RingPolynomial(ciphertext.poly0, this.q, this.n);
    const ct1 = new RingPolynomial(ciphertext.poly1, this.q, this.n);
    const phase = ct0.add(ct1.mul(s));
    return phase.coefficients.map(c => {
      const centered = c > this.q / 2n ? c - this.q : c;
      return ((centered * this.t + this.q / 2n) / this.q % this.t + this.t) % this.t;
    });
  }

  add(ct1: HECiphertext, ct2: HECiphertext): HECiphertext {
    const p0_1 = new RingPolynomial(ct1.poly0, this.q, this.n);
    const p1_1 = new RingPolynomial(ct1.poly1, this.q, this.n);
    const p0_2 = new RingPolynomial(ct2.poly0, this.q, this.n);
    const p1_2 = new RingPolynomial(ct2.poly1, this.q, this.n);
    return this.buildCiphertext(p0_1.add(p0_2), p1_1.add(p1_2), ct1, HEOperation.ADD);
  }

  addPlain(ct: HECiphertext, plaintext: bigint[]): HECiphertext {
    const p0 = new RingPolynomial(ct.poly0, this.q, this.n);
    const m = new RingPolynomial(plaintext.map(v => v % this.t), this.q, this.n);
    const mScaled = m.mulScalar(this.delta);
    return this.buildCiphertext(p0.add(mScaled), new RingPolynomial(ct.poly1, this.q, this.n), ct, HEOperation.ADD);
  }

  sub(ct1: HECiphertext, ct2: HECiphertext): HECiphertext {
    const p0_1 = new RingPolynomial(ct1.poly0, this.q, this.n);
    const p1_1 = new RingPolynomial(ct1.poly1, this.q, this.n);
    const p0_2 = new RingPolynomial(ct2.poly0, this.q, this.n);
    const p1_2 = new RingPolynomial(ct2.poly1, this.q, this.n);
    return this.buildCiphertext(p0_1.sub(p0_2), p1_1.sub(p1_2), ct1, HEOperation.SUB);
  }

  mul(ct1: HECiphertext, ct2: HECiphertext): HECiphertext {
    const c0_1 = new RingPolynomial(ct1.poly0, this.q, this.n);
    const c1_1 = new RingPolynomial(ct1.poly1, this.q, this.n);
    const c0_2 = new RingPolynomial(ct2.poly0, this.q, this.n);
    const c1_2 = new RingPolynomial(ct2.poly1, this.q, this.n);
    const d0 = c0_1.mul(c0_2);
    const d1 = c0_1.mul(c1_2).add(c1_1.mul(c0_2));
    const d2 = c1_1.mul(c1_2);
    const t = this.t;
    const scaled0 = d0.mulScalar(t).modSwitch(this.q);
    const scaled1 = d1.mulScalar(t).modSwitch(this.q);
    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      scheme: HEScheme.BFV,
      poly0: scaled0.coefficients,
      poly1: scaled1.coefficients,
      poly2: d2.coefficients,
      level: ct1.level - 1,
      noiseLevel: Math.min(ct1.noiseLevel + 1, NoiseLevel.EXHAUSTED) as NoiseLevel,
      dataType: HEDataType.INTEGER,
      slotCount: this.n,
      encryptedAt: new Date(),
      keyId: ct1.keyId,
      operationHistory: [...ct1.operationHistory, this.opRecord(HEOperation.MUL, [ct1.id, ct2.id])],
    };
  }

  relinearize(ct: HECiphertext, relinKey: HERelinKey): HECiphertext {
    const c2 = ct.poly2 ? new RingPolynomial(ct.poly2, this.q, this.n) : RingPolynomial.zero(this.q, this.n);
    const rl0 = new RingPolynomial(relinKey.data[0]?.[0] || new Array(this.n).fill(0n), this.q, this.n);
    const correction = c2.mul(rl0);
    const p0 = new RingPolynomial(ct.poly0, this.q, this.n).add(correction);
    const p1 = new RingPolynomial(ct.poly1, this.q, this.n);
    const result = this.buildCiphertext(p0, p1, ct, HEOperation.RELINEARIZE);
    delete (result as Partial<HECiphertext>).poly2;
    return result;
  }

  mulPlain(ct: HECiphertext, plaintext: bigint[]): HECiphertext {
    const p0 = new RingPolynomial(ct.poly0, this.q, this.n);
    const p1 = new RingPolynomial(ct.poly1, this.q, this.n);
    const m = new RingPolynomial(plaintext.map(v => v % this.t), this.q, this.n);
    return this.buildCiphertext(p0.mul(m), p1.mul(m), ct, HEOperation.MUL);
  }

  negate(ct: HECiphertext): HECiphertext {
    const p0 = new RingPolynomial(ct.poly0, this.q, this.n).negate();
    const p1 = new RingPolynomial(ct.poly1, this.q, this.n).negate();
    return this.buildCiphertext(p0, p1, ct, HEOperation.NEG);
  }

  private sampleError(): RingPolynomial {
    const coeffs = Array.from({ length: this.n }, () => {
      const r = crypto.randomBytes(1)[0]! % 3;
      return BigInt(r === 2 ? -1 : r);
    });
    return new RingPolynomial(coeffs, this.q, this.n);
  }

  private generateRelinKey(s: RingPolynomial): HERelinKey {
    const a = RingPolynomial.random(this.q, this.n);
    const e = this.sampleError();
    const s2 = s.mul(s);
    const rl0 = s2.negate().add(a.mul(s).add(e));
    const rl1 = a;
    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      data: [[rl0.coefficients, rl1.coefficients]],
      decompositionBase: 2,
    };
  }

  private buildCiphertext(p0: RingPolynomial, p1: RingPolynomial, source: HECiphertext, op: HEOperation): HECiphertext {
    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      scheme: HEScheme.BFV,
      poly0: p0.coefficients,
      poly1: p1.coefficients,
      level: source.level,
      noiseLevel: source.noiseLevel,
      dataType: source.dataType,
      slotCount: source.slotCount,
      encryptedAt: new Date(),
      keyId: source.keyId,
      operationHistory: [...source.operationHistory, this.opRecord(op, [source.id])],
    };
  }

  private opRecord(op: HEOperation, inputIds: string[]): HEOperationRecord {
    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      operation: op,
      timestamp: new Date(),
      inputIds,
      outputId: crypto.randomUUID(),
      durationMs: 0,
      noiseGrowth: op === HEOperation.MUL ? 2 : 1,
    };
  }
}

// ============================================================
// CKKS SCHEME (Approximate floating point)
// ============================================================

export class CKKSScheme {
  private readonly params: HEParams;
  private readonly n: number;
  private readonly q: bigint;
  private readonly scale: bigint;

  constructor(params: HEParams) {
    this.params = params;
    this.n = params.polyModulusDegree;
    this.q = params.coeffModulus.reduce((acc, v) => acc * v, 1n);
    this.scale = BigInt(Math.pow(2, params.scaleBits || 40));
  }

  encodeFloat(values: number[]): HEPlaintext {
    const scaled = values.map(v => BigInt(Math.round(v * Number(this.scale))));
    const coefficients = new Array(this.n).fill(0n);
    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(values.length, this.n / 2); i++) {
      coefficients[i] = scaled[i] ?? 0n;
      coefficients[this.n / 2 + i] = scaled[i] ?? 0n;
    }
    return {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      data: coefficients,
      dataType: HEDataType.FLOAT,
      scale: Number(this.scale),
      originalValues: values,
      encoding: 'ckks',
    };
  }

  decodeFloat(plaintext: HEPlaintext): number[] {
    const scale = BigInt(plaintext.scale || Number(this.scale));
    return plaintext.data.slice(0, this.n / 2).map(c => {
      const centered = c > this.q / 2n ? c - this.q : c;
      return Number(centered) / Number(scale);
    });
  }

  encryptFloat(values: number[], keySet: HEKeySet): HECiphertext {
    const plain = this.encodeFloat(values);
    const bfv = new BFVScheme(this.params);
    const ct = bfv.encrypt(plain.data, keySet);
    ct.scale = Number(this.scale);
    ct.dataType = HEDataType.FLOAT;
    return ct;
  }

  decryptFloat(ct: HECiphertext, keySet: HEKeySet): number[] {
    const bfv = new BFVScheme(this.params);
    const plain = bfv.decrypt(ct, keySet);
    const scale = BigInt(ct.scale || Number(this.scale));
    return plain.slice(0, this.n / 2).map(c => {
      const centered = c > this.q / 2n ? c - this.q : c;
      return Number(centered) / Number(scale);
    });
  }

  addFloat(ct1: HECiphertext, ct2: HECiphertext): HECiphertext {
    const bfv = new BFVScheme(this.params);
    return bfv.add(ct1, ct2);
  }

  mulFloat(ct1: HECiphertext, ct2: HECiphertext, relinKey: HERelinKey): HECiphertext {
    const bfv = new BFVScheme(this.params);
    const product = bfv.mul(ct1, ct2);
    const relinearized = bfv.relinearize(product, relinKey);
    if (relinearized.scale) relinearized.scale = Math.sqrt(relinearized.scale);
    return relinearized;
  }
}

// ============================================================
// ENCRYPTED ANALYTICS
// ============================================================

export class EncryptedAnalytics {
  private readonly bfv: BFVScheme;

  constructor(params: HEParams) {
    this.bfv = new BFVScheme(params);
  }

  encryptedSum(ciphertexts: HECiphertext[]): HECiphertext {
    if (ciphertexts.length === 0) throw new Error('Empty array');
    return ciphertexts.slice(1).reduce((acc, ct) => this.bfv.add(acc, ct), ciphertexts[0]!);
  }

  encryptedDotProduct(a: HECiphertext[], b: HECiphertext[], relinKey: HERelinKey): HECiphertext {
    if (a.length !== b.length) throw new Error('Length mismatch');
    const products = a.map((ai, i) => this.bfv.relinearize(this.bfv.mul(ai, b[i]!), relinKey));
    return this.encryptedSum(products);
  }

  encryptedSort(ciphertexts: HECiphertext[], ascending = true): HECiphertext[] {
    const n = ciphertexts.length;
    const result = [...ciphertexts];
    for (let i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) {
      for (let j = 0; j < n - i - 1; j++) {
        if (result[j]!.noiseLevel > result[j + 1]!.noiseLevel) {
          const temp = result[j]!;
          result[j] = result[j + 1]!;
          result[j + 1] = temp;
        }
      }
    }
    return ascending ? result : result.reverse();
  }

  encryptedAverage(ciphertexts: HECiphertext[], n: bigint): HECiphertext {
    const sum = this.encryptedSum(ciphertexts);
    const nInv = [1n, 0n].map(() => 1n);
    return this.bfv.mulPlain(sum, nInv);
  }

  encryptedInnerProduct(a: HECiphertext, b: HECiphertext, relinKey: HERelinKey): HECiphertext {
    const product = this.bfv.mul(a, b);
    return this.bfv.relinearize(product, relinKey);
  }
}

// ============================================================
// ENCRYPTED ML INFERENCE
// ============================================================

export class EncryptedMLInference {
  private readonly ckks: CKKSScheme;
  private readonly params: HEParams;

  constructor(params: HEParams) {
    this.params = params;
    this.ckks = new CKKSScheme(params);
  }

  encryptWeights(weights: number[][], keySet: HEKeySet): HECiphertext[] {
    return weights.map(layer => this.ckks.encryptFloat(layer, keySet));
  }

  linearLayer(input: HECiphertext, weightsCt: HECiphertext, bias: HECiphertext, relinKey: HERelinKey): HECiphertext {
    const bfv = new BFVScheme(this.params);
    const product = this.ckks.mulFloat(input, weightsCt, relinKey);
    return bfv.add(product, bias);
  }

  reluApprox(ct: HECiphertext, _relinKey: HERelinKey): HECiphertext {
    const bfv = new BFVScheme(this.params);
    const square = bfv.mul(ct, ct);
    const squareScaled = bfv.mulPlain(square, new Array(ct.slotCount).fill(1n));
    const halfCt = bfv.mulPlain(ct, new Array(ct.slotCount).fill(1n));
    return bfv.add(squareScaled, halfCt);
  }

  sigmoidApprox(ct: HECiphertext, _relinKey: HERelinKey): HECiphertext {
    const bfv = new BFVScheme(this.params);
    const half = bfv.mulPlain(ct, new Array(ct.slotCount).fill(1n));
    return bfv.addPlain(half, new Array(ct.slotCount).fill(1n));
  }

  inference(input: number[], model: HEMLModel, keySet: HEKeySet): HECiphertext {
    let current = this.ckks.encryptFloat(input, keySet);
    const relinKey = { id: 'dummy', data: [], decompositionBase: 2 } as HERelinKey;
    for (let i = 0; i < model.layers; i++) {
      const weight = model.weights[i];
      const bias = model.biases[i];
      if (weight && bias) {
        current = this.linearLayer(current, weight, bias, relinKey);
      }
      if (model.activationFunction === 'relu_approx') {
        current = this.reluApprox(current, relinKey);
      } else if (model.activationFunction === 'sigmoid_approx') {
        current = this.sigmoidApprox(current, relinKey);
      }
    }
    return current;
  }
}

// ============================================================
// MAIN HOMOMORPHIC ENGINE
// ============================================================

export class HomomorphicEngine extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly keySets: Map<string, HEKeySet> = new Map();
  private readonly ciphertexts: Map<string, HECiphertext> = new Map();
  private readonly circuits: Map<string, HECircuit> = new Map();
  private readonly config: HEEngineConfig;
  private readonly defaultParams: HEParams;
  private bfv?: BFVScheme;
  private ckks?: CKKSScheme;
  private analytics?: EncryptedAnalytics;
  private mlInference?: EncryptedMLInference;

  constructor(config: HEEngineConfig = {}) {
    super();
    this.config = {
      defaultScheme: HEScheme.BFV,
      enableBootstrapping: false,
      enableBatching: true,
      enableParallelism: false,
      maxCiphertexts: 10000,
      autoRescale: true,
      noiseThreshold: NoiseLevel.HIGH,
      ...config,
    };
    this.defaultParams = {
      scheme: this.config.defaultScheme!,
      polyModulusDegree: 4096,
      coeffModulus: [
        576460752303423489n,
        576460752303423233n,
      ],
      plainModulus: 1032193n,
      scaleBits: 40,
      securityLevel: 128,
      depth: 10,
      batchSize: 4096,
      ...config.defaultParams,
    };
    this.initializeSchemes();
  }

  private initializeSchemes(): void {
    this.bfv = new BFVScheme(this.defaultParams);
    this.ckks = new CKKSScheme(this.defaultParams);
    this.analytics = new EncryptedAnalytics(this.defaultParams);
    this.mlInference = new EncryptedMLInference(this.defaultParams);
  }

  generateKeySet(scheme?: HEScheme): HEKeySet {
    const params = { ...this.defaultParams, scheme: scheme || this.config.defaultScheme! };
    const bfv = new BFVScheme(params);
    const keySet = bfv.generateKeySet();
    this.keySets.set(keySet.id, keySet);
    this.emit('keyset:created', { id: keySet.id, scheme });
    return keySet;
  }

  encryptInt(values: bigint[], keySetId: string): HECiphertext {
    const keySet = this.getKeySet(keySetId);
    const ct = this.bfv!.encrypt(values, keySet);
    this.store(ct);
    return ct;
  }

  decryptInt(ctId: string, keySetId: string): bigint[] {
    const ct = this.getCiphertext(ctId);
    const keySet = this.getKeySet(keySetId);
    return this.bfv!.decrypt(ct, keySet);
  }

  encryptFloat(values: number[], keySetId: string): HECiphertext {
    const keySet = this.getKeySet(keySetId);
    const ct = this.ckks!.encryptFloat(values, keySet);
    this.store(ct);
    return ct;
  }

  decryptFloat(ctId: string, keySetId: string): number[] {
    const ct = this.getCiphertext(ctId);
    const keySet = this.getKeySet(keySetId);
    return this.ckks!.decryptFloat(ct, keySet);
  }

  add(ctId1: string, ctId2: string): HECiphertext {
    const ct1 = this.getCiphertext(ctId1);
    const ct2 = this.getCiphertext(ctId2);
    const result = this.bfv!.add(ct1, ct2);
    this.store(result);
    this.emit('operation', { op: HEOperation.ADD, inputs: [ctId1, ctId2], output: result.id });
    return result;
  }

  sub(ctId1: string, ctId2: string): HECiphertext {
    const ct1 = this.getCiphertext(ctId1);
    const ct2 = this.getCiphertext(ctId2);
    const result = this.bfv!.sub(ct1, ct2);
    this.store(result);
    return result;
  }

  mul(ctId1: string, ctId2: string, keySetId: string): HECiphertext {
    const ct1 = this.getCiphertext(ctId1);
    const ct2 = this.getCiphertext(ctId2);
    const keySet = this.getKeySet(keySetId);
    const product = this.bfv!.mul(ct1, ct2);
    const result = this.bfv!.relinearize(product, keySet.relinKey!);
    this.store(result);
    this.emit('operation', { op: HEOperation.MUL, inputs: [ctId1, ctId2], output: result.id });
    return result;
  }

  mulPlain(ctId: string, plaintext: bigint[]): HECiphertext {
    const ct = this.getCiphertext(ctId);
    const result = this.bfv!.mulPlain(ct, plaintext);
    this.store(result);
    return result;
  }

  addPlain(ctId: string, plaintext: bigint[]): HECiphertext {
    const ct = this.getCiphertext(ctId);
    const result = this.bfv!.addPlain(ct, plaintext);
    this.store(result);
    return result;
  }

  negate(ctId: string): HECiphertext {
    const ct = this.getCiphertext(ctId);
    const result = this.bfv!.negate(ct);
    this.store(result);
    return result;
  }

  sum(ctIds: string[]): HECiphertext {
    const cts = ctIds.map(id => this.getCiphertext(id));
    const result = this.analytics!.encryptedSum(cts);
    this.store(result);
    return result;
  }

  dotProduct(ctIds1: string[], ctIds2: string[], keySetId: string): HECiphertext {
    const cts1 = ctIds1.map(id => this.getCiphertext(id));
    const cts2 = ctIds2.map(id => this.getCiphertext(id));
    const keySet = this.getKeySet(keySetId);
    const result = this.analytics!.encryptedDotProduct(cts1, cts2, keySet.relinKey!);
    this.store(result);
    return result;
  }

  encryptedMLInference(input: number[], model: HEMLModel, keySetId: string): HECiphertext {
    const keySet = this.getKeySet(keySetId);
    const result = this.mlInference!.inference(input, model, keySet);
    this.store(result);
    return result;
  }

  estimateNoise(ctId: string): NoiseLevel {
    const ct = this.getCiphertext(ctId);
    return ct.noiseLevel;
  }

  needsBootstrap(ctId: string): boolean {
    const ct = this.getCiphertext(ctId);
    return ct.noiseLevel >= (this.config.noiseThreshold || NoiseLevel.HIGH);
  }

  getCiphertext(id: string): HECiphertext {
    const ct = this.ciphertexts.get(id);
    if (!ct) throw new Error(`Ciphertext ${id} not found`);
    return ct;
  }

  getKeySet(id: string): HEKeySet {
    const ks = this.keySets.get(id);
    if (!ks) throw new Error(`KeySet ${id} not found`);
    return ks;
  }

  private store(ct: HECiphertext): void {
    if (this.ciphertexts.size >= (this.config.maxCiphertexts || 10000)) {
      const oldest = Array.from(this.ciphertexts.entries()).sort((a, b) =>
        a[1].encryptedAt.getTime() - b[1].encryptedAt.getTime()
      )[0];
      if (oldest) this.ciphertexts.delete(oldest[0]);
    }
    this.ciphertexts.set(ct.id, ct);
  }

  getStats() {
    return {
      activeCiphertexts: this.ciphertexts.size,
      activeKeySets: this.keySets.size,
      registeredCircuits: this.circuits.size,
    };
  }
}

// ============================================================
// FACTORY
// ============================================================

export function createHomomorphicEngine(config?: HEEngineConfig): HomomorphicEngine {
  return new HomomorphicEngine(config);
}

export const defaultHEEngine = createHomomorphicEngine();
export default HomomorphicEngine;
